# Colombia - Statutory Report Specifications - COMPLETE REFERENCE

**Source artifacts:** the six in-scope specifications in `04-reports-spec/`, each built to the Spain 9-sheet
standard with a 21-column (A-U) Sheet 4 field list.
**Jurisdiction:** Colombia (CO) - **Currency:** COP - **Fiscal year:** 2026
**Totals:** 6 specs - 54 sheets - **563 Sheet-4 field rows** (Nomina Electronica 225 - PILA 170 - Formulario 220
75 - Formulario 350 69 - Cesantias 52 - Formato 2276 50).

## The six in-scope filings

| CCG ID | Filing ID (scope_manifest) | Report | Authority | Frequency | Deadline | Channel | Spec file |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| CO-RPT-001 | CO-DIAN-NE-MONTHLY-001 | Electronic Payroll Support Document (*documento soporte de pago de nomina electronica*) | DIAN | Monthly | Within the first **10 days** of the following month | **MACHINE-TO-MACHINE** SOAP 1.2 (the only M2M channel in Colombia) | `CO-DIAN-001-NominaElectronica-Spec.xlsx` |
| CO-RPT-002 | CO-DIAN-F350-MONTHLY-002 | Form 350 - monthly withholding return (*declaracion de retenciones en la fuente*) | DIAN | Monthly | **7th to 16th business day** of the following month, by the last digit of the NIT | Portal round-trip (Muisca), IFE-signed | `CO-DIAN-002-Formulario350-Spec.xlsx` |
| CO-RPT-003 | CO-PILA-MONTHLY-003 | PILA - integrated social-security and payroll-levy return | Ministerio de Salud y Proteccion Social, via a licensed *operador de informacion* | Monthly | **2nd to 16th business day** of the following month, by the last two digits of the NIT | Portal round-trip / fixed-width flat file to an operator | `CO-PILA-003-AportesSeguridadSocial-Spec.xlsx` |
| CO-RPT-004 | CO-DIAN-F220-ANNUAL-004 | Form 220 - annual income and withholding certificate to each employee | DIAN (issued to the employee) | Annual | **Last business day of March** | Issued to the employee (no filing) | `CO-DIAN-004-Formulario220-Spec.xlsx` |
| CO-RPT-005 | CO-DIAN-EXO2276-ANNUAL-005 | Formato 2276 v4 - third-party information return on work and pension income | DIAN | Annual | **May-June**, by NIT | Portal upload of XML prepared in the *prevalidador* | `CO-DIAN-005-Formato2276-Spec.xlsx` |
| CO-RPT-006 | CO-CESANTIAS-DEPOSIT-ANNUAL-006 | Severance-savings annual deposit allocation | *Fondos de Cesantias* (Porvenir, Proteccion, Colfondos, Skandia, FNA), via an *operador de informacion* | Annual | **By 14 February** following the 31 December liquidation (for accrual year 2026: **Friday 12 February 2027**) | Portal round-trip / file upload to an operator | `CO-CESANTIAS-006-ConsignacionAnual-Spec.xlsx` |

**CO-RPT-007** (MinTrabajo disability-hiring contract report) is **descoped** as HR-scope in
`scope_manifest.json` - no spec exists and none should. See QA MAJOR-03 in `07-qa-report.md`.

## Sheet-4 21-column layout (A-U), identical in all six specs

| Col | Header (as used in the Colombia specs) | Standard label (variance noted in QA MINOR-07) |
|---|---|---|
| A | Field Name (EN) | same |
| B | Field Name (Local ES) | same |
| C | **Record Type** | `Record Type / Section` |
| D | Field Position | same |
| E | Payroll Field (Y/N) | same |
| F | Mandatory (Y/N/C) | same |
| G | Conditional Reason | same |
| H | **Position (in file)** | `Position (Start-End)` |
| I | **Length** | `Field Length` |
| J | Format | same |
| K | Example | same |
| L | System Mapping | same |
| M | **Explanation** | `Field Explanation` |
| N | Relevant for Payroll (Y/N) | same |
| O | Option Values | same |
| P | Rounding | same |
| Q | Negative (Y/N) | same |
| R | Value Type | same |
| S | **Frequency** | `Reporting Frequency` |
| T | Query to RA | same |
| U | RA Comments | same |

**System Mapping buckets (4-bucket DD reconciliation, 563 rows):** `DD` (`Employee.*`, `Company.*`,
`Contract.*`) 160 rows / 94 distinct tokens - all 94 present in the Data Dictionary (**100%**) -
`CALC` (`Payroll.*`, `SUM(Payroll...)`, casilla / numeral arithmetic) 249 - `SYS` (`Mercans.*`, `System.*`,
`PayPeriod.*`, `Constant*`, `(system total)`) 136 - `TX / out-of-payroll` (`(employee declaration)`,
`(client tax function)`, `(client accounts payable)`) 18.

> **[QA MINOR-10]** Column L carries no WTC code for amount-bearing fields (it uses `Payroll.*` tokens). The
> WTC-to-spec direction is covered by the WTC's 65-entry Form Reference Guide, but the spec-to-WTC direction is
> not: a developer mapping `NIE071` has no WTC code in front of them. Suggested fix:
> `Payroll.Transport_Allowance [WTC: CO-AUX-TRANSPORTE / 62520]`.

> **[QA MINOR-07 / MINOR-08]** Five of the 21 Sheet-4 column labels and the Version-Control change-log column
> order differ in wording from `shared/report-spec-template/REPORT_SPEC_STANDARD_FORMAT.md`. Semantics, order
> and count are correct and the deviation is consistent across all six specs.

> **[QA MINOR-02]** The PILA spec does not label `T2R2-003` + `T2R2-004` (contributor document type + number)
> as a **composite key** in column M, even though `CO-CESANTIAS-006` annotates the identical pair as
> `COMPOSITE FIELD` at row 4. Column M calls `T2R2-004` a *"Join key to NIE045 / F220 casilla 25 / 2276
> numeral 2"* only. Treat type + number **together** as the record key. (Formulario 350 needs no composite key -
> it is an aggregate return with no per-employee record.)

**Source-status honesty (per spec, as declared in Sheet 1 / Sheet 5):** PILA is `PRIMARY, BYTE-EXACT` with
field counts and position totals arithmetically verified (Archivo Tipo 1 = 34 fields / 567 positions;
Archivo Tipo 2 registro 1 = 22 / 359; registro 2 = 97 / 686). Formato 2276 is marked **DERIVED** for the XML
layout because the DIAN *especificaciones tecnicas* annex is not held locally. No spec overclaims its sourcing.

## CO-DIAN-001-NominaElectronica - Sheet 2: Report Matrix


```
REPORT MATRIX - ELECTRONIC PAYROLL SUPPORT DOCUMENT
```


| Report ID | Report Name (EN) | Report Name (Local ES) | Authority | Frequency | Due Date Rule | Submission Method | File Format | Legal Basis | Accuracy Assessment |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| CO-DIAN-001 | Electronic Payroll Support Document | Documento Soporte de Pago de Nomina Electronica | Direccion de Impuestos y Aduanas Nacionales (DIAN) | Monthly - ONE document per employee per month, irrespective of the pay cycle (Res. DIAN 000013 de 2021 art. 3) | Transmitted for validation within the FIRST TEN DAYS of the month following the payment or credit in account (Res. DIAN 000013 de 2021 art. 8). The deadline runs from the payment, not from the accrual. | MACHINE-TO-MACHINE web service. SOAP 1.2, Document/Literal, operation SendNominaSync, over TLS 1.2 with MUTUAL certificate authentication; WS-Security 1.0 with X.509 Certificate Token Profile 1.1. Portal: DIAN Sistema de Validacion y Gestion de Documentos (catalogo-vpfe.dian.gov.co). Companion query operation GetStatus. | XML (UBL 2.1 extension area for the signature; DIAN's own structure elsewhere), base64 of a ZIP containing the XML, maximum 500 KB, UTF-8 only, XAdES-EPES signature. XSD version 1.0.6; Anexo Tecnico version 1.0. | Resolucion DIAN 000013 de 11 de febrero de 2021, arts. 1, 2, 3, 5, 8, 15, 22, 23, 24, 26 and its Anexo Tecnico version 1.0 (269 pp.); Estatuto Tributario arts. 383, 385, 386, 387, 387-1, 388 (resident) and 408-415 (non-resident); CST (Decreto 2663 de 1950) arts. 127, 128, 130, 132, 186, 192, 232, 249, 306; Ley 50 de 1990 art. 99; Ley 52 de 1975 art. 1; Ley 1393 de 2010 art. 30; Ley 2101 de 2021; Ley 2466 de 2025 arts. 21, 52-54, 64; Decreto 0223 de 5 de marzo de 2026. | 95% (PRIMARY SOURCE). Every field code, XPath, length, cardinality and code table in this Spec is transcribed from the official 269-page Anexo Tecnico version 1.0 held at 00-official-sources/pdfs/AnexoTecnico-NominaElectronica-V1.0.pdf, and the SOAP contract and CUNE algorithm are quoted from its sections 8 and 9. The 5% reservation is: (a) the Spec is written against Anexo v1.0 / XSD v1.0.6 and DIAN has not published a v1.1, so a silent XSD revision would not be reflected; (b) the annex's own table 5.5.5 Sunday / holiday percentages conflict with Ley 2101 de 2021 from 1 July 2026 (see the RA query on those rows); (c) three schema defects recorded as CO-GAP-06 and CO-GAP-07 have no published DIAN workaround. It would rise to 100% with a DIAN-issued sample XML and a written DIAN position on the table 5.5.5 percentages. |


```
SUBMISSION CHANNEL VERIFICATION (v2.7 STEP 3.5)
- Channel: xml (machine-to-machine) | Portal: DIAN Sistema de Validacion y Gestion de Documentos, catalogo-vpfe.dian.gov.co | Source: Anexo Tecnico v1.0 s.9.3 to s.9.8 (SOAP 1.2, Document/Literal, TLS 1.2 mutual authentication, WS-Security 1.0 X.509 Token Profile 1.1) | CONFIRMED.
- This is the ONE Colombian payroll filing that is genuinely machine-to-machine. It inverts the Colombian default: Formulario 350, Formulario 220 and Formato 2276 are portal round-trips, and PILA is filed through a licensed private operador de informacion. Do not generalise the M2M posture from this filing to the others.
- Operation SendNominaSync takes a single parameter, wcf:contentFile, carrying the base64 of a ZIP that contains the signed XML. Maximum 500 KB. Anexo s.9.5 and s.9.7.2 print the exact envelope, reproduced on Sheet 9.
- Enablement (habilitacion) with DIAN is a PROJECT PREREQUISITE with a lead time, not a configuration step: global validation rule 92 rejects any document from a non-enabled issuer, and rule 90 permits a given document number only once per employee.

INTEGRATION TYPE AND DIRECTION
- integration_type = BOTH (scope_manifest CO-BOTH-NE). Outbound: the signed XML. Inbound: the synchronous base64 ApplicationResponse in the same call.
- The inbound leg (CO-IN-NE-APPRESPONSE) is specified in this workbook rather than in a separate file, because it is not an independent government-to-employer flow - it is the response half of a single synchronous exchange and shares this filing's identifiers. See the section 'INBOUND - DIAN VALIDATION RESPONSE' on the Spec sheet.
- product_mapping_required = true. The XML separates salary from non-salary at ELEMENT level with paired S / NS element names, so every wage type must carry a HARD salary / non-salary flag set at configuration time. It cannot be derived at reporting time.

RESIDENT / NON-RESIDENT TREATMENT (v2.8 control B1)
- RESIDENT: RetencionFuente (NIE177) carries the ET art. 383 progressive table (0 / 19 / 28 / 33 / 35 / 37 / 39% on the tax-unit bands 0-95, 95-150, 150-360, 360-640, 640-945, 945-2,300, above 2,300 UVT), applied to the art. 388 depurated monthly base under Procedure 1 or Procedure 2.
- NON-RESIDENT: RetencionFuente (NIE177) carries the FLAT 20% of ET art. 408 on the GROSS payment - no art. 388 depuration, no 25% exempt income, no art. 387 deductions, no tax-free band. Confirmed by DIAN Concepto 011108 of 29 June 2026, upholding Concepto 1789 (int. 14595) of 2025.
- The XML has NO residence attribute. Residence is therefore invisible to DIAN in this document and visible only through the amount in NIE177 and, on Formulario 350, through the hoja-2 pagos-al-exterior section. The engine must carry a residence flag with an effective date on the employee record, re-evaluate it EVERY period, and switch the schedule PROSPECTIVELY from the payment following the date the 183-day test is met. Never retroactively - there is no payroll true-up of the earlier months.
- Where the non-resident's work is performed ENTIRELY outside Colombia the income is foreign-source and not subject to withholding at all (DIAN Oficio 28380 of 2019); LugarTrabajoPais (NIE050) is the field that evidences this.
- Treaty relief can reduce or eliminate the 20% where a double-tax treaty assigns taxing rights to the residence state. Mercans applies treaty relief ONLY on written client instruction supported by a residence certificate.

RELATED FILINGS / CROSS-REFERENCES
- The monthly sum of RetencionFuente (NIE177) across all employees must reconcile to Formulario 350 casilla 93 (retenciones - rentas de trabajo, personas naturales) for the same period - CO-DIAN-002.
- Salud (NIE163), FondoPension (NIE166) and FondoSP (NIE168 / NIE170) must reconcile to the corresponding PILA Registro Tipo 2 fields 55, 47 and 51 / 52 - CO-PILA-003 - subject to the different rounding conventions.
- Cesantias (NIE120) and PagoIntereses (NIE122) feed Formulario 220 casillas 47-49 and Formato 2276 numerals 18-20 - CO-DIAN-004 and CO-DIAN-005.
- The annual accumulation of every Devengados element feeds Formulario 220 casillas 36-52 and Formato 2276 numerals 5-22.
- The graphical representation (payslip) is PERMISSIVE under art. 24, but if issued it must carry the thirteen art. 5 items and a QR of at least 2 cm on EVERY page. Colombia has NO statutory payslip-content article in its labour code - the itemisation rule is this tax resolution. See 06-payslip.
```


## CO-DIAN-001-NominaElectronica - Sheet 3: Payroll Scope Summary


```
PAYROLL SCOPE SUMMARY - ELECTRONIC PAYROLL SUPPORT DOCUMENT
```


| SCENARIO | REPORT? | NOTES / REASONING |
|---|---|---|
| Indefinite-term employee (contrato a termino indefinido) | YES | The core private-sector population. CST art. 47; Ley 2466 de 2025 art. 5. |
| Fixed-term employee (contrato a termino fijo, max 4 years) | YES | Identical reporting treatment to an indefinite-term contract. CST art. 46; Ley 2466 de 2025 art. 6. |
| Specific-work or task employee (contrato por obra o labor) | YES | An employment contract; CST art. 46. Nomina electronica TipoContrato = 3 (Obra o Labor). |
| Occasional / accidental / transitory worker (< 1 month, non-core activity) | YES | Still an employee under CST art. 6. Reported on every filing; the PILA planilla type and the day counts differ, not the obligation. |
| Part-time employee | YES | Colombia has no separate part-time regime. Reported normally; the PILA contribution base is floored at 1 SMMLV (COP 1,750,905) except for the worker type 51 partial-time regime. |
| All-inclusive salary employee (salario integral, >= 13 SMMLV) | YES | CST art. 132 num. 2. Reported with the integral-salary flag set; the contribution base is 70% of the integral salary, and the nomina electronica carries SalarioIntegral = true. |
| Apprentice (contrato de aprendizaje) | YES | Ley 2466 de 2025 art. 21 converted the apprenticeship into a special fixed-term LABOUR contract, regulated by Decreto 0223 of 5 March 2026. Nomina electronica TipoContrato = 4; PILA worker type 12 (lectiva) or 19 (productiva). |
| Intern / practical placement (practicas o pasantias) | YES | Nomina electronica TipoContrato = 5. Reported where remunerated. |
| Teleworkers and remote workers | YES | Ley 1221 de 2008 as amended by Ley 2466 de 2025 arts. 52-54. No reporting difference; the nomina electronica carries a dedicated Teletrabajo devengado element (NIE158). |
| Resident foreign national under a Colombian contract | YES | Full reporting. Withheld under the ET art. 383 progressive table once tax residence is acquired. |
| NON-RESIDENT foreign national under a Colombian contract | YES | IN SCOPE and frequently mis-configured. Withheld at the flat 20% of ET art. 408 on the GROSS, with no art. 388 depuration, until the 183-day residence test is met (DIAN Concepto 011108 of 29 June 2026). See the resident / non-resident block in the Report Matrix. |
| Employed old-age pensioner in active service | YES | PILA worker sub-type 01; no pension contribution (PILA pension rate field = 0), health and the levies still apply. |
| Executive director / statutory legal representative holding an employment contract | YES | Reported as an ordinary employee where an employment contract exists. |
| Independent contractor (contrato de prestacion de servicios) | NO | Not an employee. The contractor self-files PILA on 40% of fees (planilla types I / Y, descoped) and is reported by the payer in the third-party information return, not in this employer filing. Withholding on their fees does pass through Formulario 350, but under a different casilla. |
| Public-sector employee (contributor type 47, SGP-funded entity) | NO | Public-sector only; PILA planilla types T and F are descoped in scope_manifest. |
| Non-executive director paid honorarios only (junta directiva) | NO | Not employment income. Reported by the payer as honorarios, not as rentas de trabajo of an employee. |
| Pensioner whose pension is paid by a fund, not by the employer | NO | The paying fund is the withholding agent and the reporting party, not the employer. |
| Basic salary for the days worked | YES | SueldoTrabajado (NIE070). The single largest input to every downstream filing. |
| Statutory transport allowance | YES | AuxilioTransporte (NIE071), where the employee earns up to 2 SMMLV. Not salary for contributions, but IS in the base of cesantias, prima and the severance indemnity. |
| Overtime and premium hours, itemised by type with times and percentages | YES | Seven separate hour-type blocks (table 5.5.5), each with start time, end time, quantity, percentage and amount. A single aggregated 'overtime' figure is NOT admissible. |
| Vacation taken and vacation compensated in cash, as separate elements | YES | VacacionesComunes and VacacionesCompensadas. They must not be merged: enjoyed vacation is in the contribution base, compensated vacation is not. |
| Service bonus, severance savings and interest on severance savings | YES | Primas, Cesantias and Cesantias/@PagoIntereses. Reported here even though severance savings are excluded from the monthly withholding base. |
| Incapacity, maternity / paternity leave and paid leave | YES | Each with its own dates, quantity and amount, and - for incapacity - its type code. |
| Unpaid leave and lawful strike | YES | Reported as days ONLY. Both blocks deliberately carry no amount element. |
| Benefits in kind (salary in kind) | YES | Through the paired OtroConcepto S / NS elements or the specific allowance elements. Valued contractually or by expert appraisal; Colombia has no notional valuation scale. |
| Employer social-security contributions | NO | The document reports what the EMPLOYEE accrued and what was DEDUCTED from the employee. The employer's 12% pension, 8.5% health, occupational-risk premium and 9% levies are an employer cost reported on PILA, never a deduction here. |
| Employer payroll levies (SENA 2%, ICBF 3%, CCF 4%) | NO | Employer cost, reported on PILA. Never an employee deduction. |
| Documented business-expense reimbursements | NO | Not remuneration. Excluded from the accrued block; only the permanent portion of viaticos (NIE072) is reportable, and only because it is salary. |
| Amounts in a currency other than the Colombian peso | NO | Res. 000013 de 2021 art. 26 makes the peso mandatory. A foreign currency may be ADDED to a graphical representation but never substituted; the TRM element (NIE200) exists in the schema but is out of scope for a Colombian payroll. |
| Reported on the accrual (causacion) date | NO | Every Colombian payroll filing is keyed to the PAYMENT or credit-in-account date, not to the accrual date. A December salary paid in January falls in the January filing. |
| Reported on the payment or credit-in-account date | YES | The universal Colombian rule - pago o abono en cuenta. Applies to the nomina electronica (Res. DIAN 000013 de 2021 art. 3), to Formulario 350, to Formulario 220 and to Formato 2276. |
| A semi-monthly payroll produces two filings a month | NO | It does not. The nomina electronica is expressly ONE monthly document per employee 'irrespective of whether the obliged party makes payments in different or shorter periods' (art. 3). PILA and Formulario 350 are likewise monthly. |
| Nil period still filed | VARIES | Formulario 350: NOT required where no operation subject to withholding occurred in the period (ET art. 606 par. 2). Nomina electronica: no document where there was no payment. PILA: a planilla is still required while the employment relationship subsists. |
| Corrections handled by re-filing the whole period | VARIES | Nomina electronica: NO - a dedicated adjustment note (NominaIndividualDeAjuste, Reemplazar or Eliminar) referencing the original CUNE. Formulario 350: yes, a corrective return. PILA: a planilla type N (correcciones) or M (mora). |
| Employee with zero net pay (deductions equal accrued) | YES | A document is still required. ComprobanteTotal (NIE189) is 0.00; the accrued and deducted blocks are fully itemised. |
| Employee whose withholding falls in the 0% band | YES | The payment is still reported. RetencionFuente (NIE177) is simply omitted rather than zero-filled. |
| Mid-month hire or mid-month leaver | YES | DiasTrabajados (NIE069) is reduced; FechaIngreso or FechaRetiro is set; TiempoLaborado is computed to the settlement end on the 360/30 convention. |
| Second employer of a multi-employer employee | YES | Each employer issues its own document for its own payments. There is no aggregation across employers at document level. |
| Employee paid semi-monthly | YES | ONE monthly document carrying PeriodoNomina = 4 (Quincenal) and TWO FechaPago (NIE203) occurrences. Not two documents. |
| Correction of a previously validated document | YES | By adjustment note (NominaIndividualDeAjuste), never by re-sending the original number - global rule 90 forbids reuse. |
| Employee holding a PPT (Permiso por Proteccion Temporal) | YES | Reported, but see CO-GAP-06: the annex has no PPT document-type code because the PPT post-dates it. Code 47 (PEP) is the only available proxy. |

| CUNE - unique payroll document code | CUNE - Codigo Unico de Nomina Electronica | InformacionGeneral | NIE024 | N | Y |  | /NominaIndividual/InformacionGeneral/@CUNE | 96 | A (hex, 96 chars) | 16560dc8956122e84ffb743c817fe7d494e058a44d9ca3fa4c234c268b4f766003253fbee7ea4af9682dd57210f3bac2 | System.CUNE | COMPOSITE KEY - position breakdown. The CUNE is the SHA-384 hexadecimal digest (96 hex characters) of ELEVEN concatenated inputs, in this exact order and with no separators (Anexo Tecnico s.8.1.1.1): [1] NumNE = Prefijo + Consecutivo (NIE012); [2] FecNE = FechaGen (NIE026, AAAA-MM-DD); [3] HorNE = HoraGen including the GMT offset (NIE027); [4] ValDev = DevengadosTotal (NIE187), decimal point, 2 decimals TRUNCATED, no thousand separators, no currency symbol; [5] ValDed = DeduccionesTotal (NIE188), same format; [6] ValTolNE = ComprobanteTotal (NIE189), same format; [7] NitNE = employer NIT (NIE033) without dots, hyphens or the verification digit; [8] DocEmp = employee document number (NIE045) without dots or hyphens; [9] TipoXML (NIE202); [10] Software-Pin - the secret PIN, which NEVER appears in the XML; [11] TipAmb = Ambiente (NIE023). PERSISTENCE: the CUNE is immutable once the document is validated. Any change to any of the eleven inputs produces a different CUNE and therefore a different document; corrections are made by an adjustment note carrying its own CUNE and referencing the original. | N | - | Not an amount - no rounding. | N | Identifier | Per document |  | Pending RA review |
| CUNE algorithm literal | EncripCUNE | InformacionGeneral | NIE025 | N | Y |  | /NominaIndividual/InformacionGeneral/@EncripCUNE | 11 | Fixed text | CUNE-SHA384 | Mercans.CUNE_Algorithm | A CONSTANT. Must contain exactly the words 'CUNE-SHA384'. Declares the hash algorithm used for NIE024 so that a verifier can reproduce the digest. | N | -> Sheet 6: Algoritmo CUNE (table 5.1.2) | Not an amount - no rounding. | N | Text | Per document |  | Pending RA review |

Distinct from the payment date (NIE203) and from the settlement period (NIE004 / NIE005). It is input [2] to the CUNE, and it is the date against which the art. 8 deadline - transmission within the first 10 days of the month following the payment - is measured. | Y | - | Not an amount - no rounding. | N | Date | Per document |  | Pending RA review |

Colombia is UTC-05:00 all year and does not observe daylight saving, so the offset is always -05:00. Input [3] to the CUNE - a missing or wrong offset silently breaks the hash. | N | - | Not an amount - no rounding. | N | Date | Per document |  | Pending RA review |

| Contractual salary | Sueldo | Trabajador | NIE062 | N | Y |  | /NominaIndividual/Trabajador/@Sueldo | Var | N (decimal 2) | 12000000.00 | Payroll.Contractual_Salary | SCOPE ROUTING (v1.1 alignment patch): the amount is owned by the Wage Type Catalogue, not the Data Dictionary - the pipeline scope rule routes 'Base Salary / Basic Salary amounts -> WTC, NOT DD'. The 'Payroll.' prefix marks it as outside DD master data; it is a standing contract attribute, not a per-run computation. Column E is therefore N and the Data Dictionary correctly holds no Contractual Salary field. The CONTRACTUAL monthly salary, not the amount actually paid for the period. It stays constant across a month with unpaid leave or a mid-month hire, whereas SueldoTrabajado (NIE070) moves. Never below 1 SMMLV (COP 1,750,905 in 2026) for a full-time employee. | Y | - | 2 decimals, round-half-to-even (NTC 3711 / JIS Z 8401); DIAN validation tolerance +/- 2.00; any residual is carried by the Redondeo element NIE186. | Time worked | TiempoLaborado | Periodo | NIE006 | N | Y |  | /NominaIndividual/Periodo/@TiempoLaborado | Var | N (days, decimal 2) | 1875.00 | Payroll.Service_Days | COMPOSITE VALUE - construction rule. Cumulative service with the employer expressed in DAYS on the Colombian commercial-year convention fixed by Anexo Tecnico s.8.3.1: 1 year = 360 days, 1 month = 30 days. The annex's own worked example is 5 years + 3 months + 18 days = (5 x 360) + (3 x 30) + 18 = 1908.00. Sub-fields: [years x 360] + [months x 30] + [odd days]. PERSISTENCE: recomputed every month from FechaIngreso (NIE002) to the settlement end; it is cumulative service, NOT days worked in the period (that is DiasTrabajados, NIE069). | Y | - | Two decimals, per the annex example (1908.00). | N | Count | Monthly |  | Pending RA review |

| Day overtime hour, Sunday / public holiday - start time | Hora Extra Diurna Dominical y Festivos - Hora de inicio | Devengados / HEDDF | NIE089 | N | C | Optional in the schema (0-1) but required by the RA whenever the client reports the day/night split for the overtime register. Pairs with 'Day overtime hour, Sunday / public holiday - end time' (NIE090): if one is present the other must be present. | /NominaIndividual/Devengados/HEDDFs/HEDDF/@HoraInicio | 19 | AAAA-MM-DDTHH:MM:SS | 2026-03-11T18:00:00 | Payroll.Overtime_Detail.Start_Time | Clock time at which this overtime or surcharge block began. The XML models overtime as a repeating block, not as a single monthly total, so each contiguous stretch of hours is one HEDDF element. Cardinality 0-N under HEDDFs. | Y | - | Not an amount - no rounding. | N | Date | Per record | SOURCE CONFLICT, escalate. Anexo Tecnico table 5.5.5 hard-codes the Sunday / holiday surcharge at 75.00 and the derived Sunday / holiday overtime rates at 100.00 / 150.00 / 110.00. Those figures pre-date Ley 2101 de 2021 and Ley 2466 de 2025, under which the Sunday and public-holiday surcharge steps to 80% on 1 July 2025 and to 90% on 1 JULY 2026. From 1 July 2026 the statutory surcharge (90%) therefore no longer equals the annex value (75.00). The annex is still at version 1.0 and DIAN has not reissued table 5.5.5. Confirm with the RA whether DIAN expects the ANNEX code percentage or the STATUTORY percentage in @Porcentaje. | Pending RA review |

## CO-DIAN-001-NominaElectronica - Sheet 5: File Structure


```
FILE STRUCTURE AND TECHNICAL SPECIFICATIONS
```


| Item | Value |
|---|---|
| File type | XML 1.0. The signature area uses OASIS UBL 2.1; every other section uses DIAN's own structure as published in the Anexo Tecnico. This is a REAL XML submission with a published schema - not a portal form - so the wire format below is the actual submission format, not a logical model. |
| Schema | XSD version 1.0.6, referenced by Anexo Tecnico version 1.0. Root elements: NominaIndividual (TipoXML 102) and NominaIndividualDeAjuste (TipoXML 103). |
| Character encoding | UTF-8 ONLY. Anexo s.9.7.1. Any other encoding is rejected at unzip time. |
| Transport packaging | The XML is placed in a ZIP; the ZIP is base64-encoded; the base64 string is the single wcf:contentFile parameter of SendNominaSync. |
| Maximum size | 500 KB for the transmitted payload. |
| Signature | XAdES-EPES enveloped signature inside /NominaIndividual/UBLExtensions/UBLExtension/ExtensionContent/ds:Signature, using an X.509 certificate from a certification authority accredited by ONAC. The ClaimedRole must be thirdparty or supplier (Anexo s.7). |
| Decimal convention | Two decimals, decimal POINT, no thousand separators, no currency symbol. Rounding is round-half-to-even; validation tolerance is +/- 2.00. |
| Date / time convention | Dates AAAA-MM-DD. Times HH:MM:SS with the GMT offset, always -05:00 for Colombia (no daylight saving). Overtime start / end times use the 19-character form AAAA-MM-DDTHH:MM:SS. |
| Protocol | SOAP 1.2, Style/Encoding Document/Literal, over HTTPS with TLS 1.2 and MUTUAL authentication by digital certificate. |
| Security model | WS-Security 1.0 (OASIS) with X.509 Certificate Token Profile 1.1. |
| Namespaces | soap = http://www.w3.org/2003/05/soap-envelope ; wcf = http://wcf.dian.colombia |
| Submission operation | SendNominaSync - one parameter, wcf:contentFile, carrying the base64 of the ZIP. Synchronous. |
| Response action | http://wcf.dian.colombia/IWcfDianCustomerServices/SendNominaSyncResponse |
| Query operation | GetStatus - retrieves the status of a previously transmitted document (Anexo s.9.8). |
| Server-side checks, in order | 1. ZIP is not empty. 2. ZIP is not corrupt. 3. The UBL 2.1 signed section exists. 4. The document matches the NominaIndividual or NominaIndividualDeAjuste XSD. 5. No error against the Anexo's own structural rules. Only then is the ApplicationResponse produced. |


```








































































































```


| Item | Value |
|---|---|
| Item 1 | Employer surnames and names or corporate name, and tax identification number (NIE032 / NIE210-213, NIE033, NIE034). |
| Item 2 | Employee surnames and names, and identity document number (NIE046-NIE049, NIE045). |
| Item 3 | Unique consecutive document number preceded by the authorised prefix (NIE010-NIE012). |
| Item 4 | Generation date (NIE026). |
| Item 5 | Payment or credit date (NIE203). |
| Item 6 | Accrued payroll values, itemised by concept (the whole Devengados block). |
| Item 7 | Deducted payroll values, itemised by concept (the whole Deducciones block). |
| Item 8 | Total difference between accrued and deducted (NIE187, NIE188, NIE189). |
| Item 9 | Payment or credit period (NIE004, NIE005). |
| Item 10 | Payment method and medium (NIE064, NIE065). |
| Item 11 | Country, department and municipality of origin (NIE035, NIE036, NIE037). |
| Item 12 | Content of the authorised technical annex (the whole document). |
| Item 13 | CUNE - the unique electronic payroll code (NIE024). |
| CUNE (Anexo s.8.1) | SHA-384 of NumNE + FecNE + HorNE + ValDev + ValDed + ValTolNE + NitNE + DocEmp + TipoXML + Software-Pin + TipAmb, concatenated with no separators. Money inputs are TRUNCATED to two decimals, not rounded. Result is 96 lower-case hexadecimal characters. |
| SoftwareSC (Anexo s.8.2) | SHA-384 of SoftwareID + PIN + document number. Changes with every document, unlike SoftwareID. |
| TiempoLaborado (Anexo s.8.3.1) | Days on the 360/30 commercial convention: (years x 360) + (months x 30) + odd days. Annex example: 5 years + 3 months + 18 days = 1908.00. |
| QR (Anexo s.13.1) | URL of the DIAN document-search service keyed on the CUNE. If a graphical representation is issued, the QR must be at least 2 cm x 2 cm on EVERY page. |
| Key validation rules | NIE022 - the Version literal must be exactly 'V1.0: Documento Soporte de Pago de Nomina Electronica'. Global rule 90 - a document number may be transmitted only ONCE per employee. Global rule 92 - a document from a non-enabled issuer is rejected. Full rule set at Anexo s.6.1. |

## CO-DIAN-001-NominaElectronica - Sheet 7: Population Rules


```
POPULATION RULES - HOW TO FILL EACH FIELD FROM PAYROLL
```


| Field Name | Source System | Population Logic | Edge Cases |
|---|---|---|---|
| Version (NIE022) | Configuration | Emit the constant string 'V1.0: Documento Soporte de Pago de Nomina Electronica' exactly, including accents and the colon-space. Never build it from a version variable, never trim, never transliterate. Validation rule NIE022 compares the whole literal. | A configuration file saved as ASCII will silently strip the accent on 'Nomina' and every document will be rejected. |
| Numero (NIE012) | Engine sequence + configuration | Concatenate the DIAN-authorised Prefijo (NIE010) with the next unused Consecutivo (NIE011), with no separator. Persist the last-used consecutive per prefix range in a transactional store, and increment it BEFORE transmission, never after, so that a failed transmission does not leave a reusable number. Global rule 90 permits a given number only once per employee. | A rejected document consumes its number. On re-transmission the corrected document must carry a NEW consecutive. Never re-send a rejected number. |
| CUNE (NIE024) | Engine (computed) | Compute SHA-384 over the eleven inputs concatenated with no separators, in the published order: Numero, FechaGen, HoraGen (with the -05:00 offset), DevengadosTotal, DeduccionesTotal, ComprobanteTotal, employer NIT (no DV), employee document number, TipoXML, the secret software PIN, and Ambiente. The three money inputs must be TRUNCATED to two decimals - not rounded - and written with a decimal point, no thousand separators and no currency symbol. Emit the result as 96 lower-case hexadecimal characters. | The four classic failures: (1) rounding instead of truncating the money inputs; (2) omitting the -05:00 offset from HoraGen; (3) including the employer DV in NitNE; (4) using the SoftwareID instead of the PIN. All four produce a well-formed but wrong CUNE. |
| SoftwareSC (NIE020) | Engine (computed) | Compute SHA-384 over SoftwareID + PIN + document number. Recompute for EVERY document, because the document number is an input. Do not cache it at software level. | Caching SoftwareSC as if it were a constant is a common error - it is constant only for a single document. |
| TiempoLaborado (NIE006) | Engine (computed) | Compute cumulative service from FechaIngreso to FechaLiquidacionFin on the Colombian commercial convention: 1 year = 360 days, 1 month = 30 days. Decompose the elapsed period into whole years, whole months and residual days, then evaluate (years x 360) + (months x 30) + days. Emit with two decimals. The annex's own example is 5 years + 3 months + 18 days = 1908.00. | Do NOT use a calendar day-count. A naive (date2 - date1).days gives 1913 for the annex's own example instead of 1908, and DIAN will not flag it - the error surfaces only in a seniority dispute. |
| DiasTrabajados (NIE069) | Payroll engine | Days actually worked and paid in the period on the 30-day commercial month. Start at 30 (or the number of days from a mid-month hire), then subtract unpaid-leave days (LicenciaNR), suspension days and lawful-strike days. Do NOT subtract incapacity, maternity or vacation days - those are paid and appear in their own devengado blocks. | February always counts 30 days in Colombian payroll, not 28. A 31-day month also counts 30. |
| DevengadosTotal / DeduccionesTotal / ComprobanteTotal (NIE187-NIE189) | Engine (computed) | DevengadosTotal is the sum of every populated element in the Devengados block; DeduccionesTotal is the sum of every populated element in Deducciones; ComprobanteTotal is DevengadosTotal minus DeduccionesTotal, adjusted for the Redondeo residual. Each element is first rounded half-to-even to two decimals, then summed - not summed and then rounded. Where a residual remains, disclose it in Redondeo (NIE186) rather than absorbing it into a concept. Validation tolerance is +/- 2.00. | Summing unrounded values and rounding the total produces a figure that ties to the cent but does not match the sum of the printed lines on the payslip. Round first, then sum. |
| Salary / non-salary paired elements | Wage-type configuration | Every paired element (ViaticoManuAlojS / NS, BonificacionS / NS, AuxilioS / NS, ConceptoS / NS, PagoS / NS, PagoAlimentacionS / NS, Primas Pago / PagoNS) must be driven by a HARD salary / non-salary flag carried on the wage type and set at configuration time. Never infer the split at reporting time from the amount, the description or the wage-type name. | The 40% non-salary clamp of Ley 1393 de 2010 art. 30 depends on this split, as does the contribution base. An inference error propagates into PILA and into the employer cost. |
| Employer contributions must never appear in Deducciones | Wage-type configuration | The Deducciones block reports only what was withheld FROM THE EMPLOYEE. The employer's 12% pension, 8.5% health, occupational-risk premium and the 9% payroll levies (SENA 2%, ICBF 3%, CCF 4%) are employer costs reported on PILA. Configure them on a cost, not a deduction, wage-type class so they cannot leak into this block. | A single mis-classified employer wage type inflates DeduccionesTotal and understates ComprobanteTotal, which then disagrees with the actual bank payment. |
| Solidarity fund must not be merged into pension | Wage-type configuration | FondoSP carries FOUR attributes - the solidarity rate and amount, and the subsistence rate and amount. They are validated independently of FondoPension. Configure the solidarity surcharge as its own wage type from the outset. | This is the single most common Colombian mapping error. Merging produces a 5% 'pension' line that fails reconciliation against PILA fields 47, 51 and 52. |
| Overtime cannot be aggregated | Payroll engine | Emit one element per contiguous block of hours of a single type. Split a block that crosses 21:00 or midnight, because the applicable code changes. Populate quantity, percentage and amount for every block, plus start and end times where the client reports them. | A block that crosses the 21:00 boundary but is emitted as a single HED element misstates both the code and the premium. |
| All monetary elements | Engine (formatting) | Two decimals, decimal POINT, no thousand separator, no currency symbol, no leading plus sign. Rounding is round-half-to-even (NTC 3711 / JIS Z 8401), not half-up. Negative values are not used in the accrued or deducted blocks; only Redondeo (NIE186) may legitimately be negative. | A locale-aware formatter will emit a comma decimal separator in an es-CO locale and the document will fail schema validation. |
| Three rounding conventions coexist | Engine (formatting) | The XML uses two decimals round-half-to-even. PILA rounds the base UP to the peso and each contribution UP to the next multiple of 100. Formulario 350 rounds to the nearest 1,000. The same health contribution therefore legitimately appears as three different numbers in three artefacts. Store the unrounded value once and apply each convention at output time. | Never display the PILA figure on the payslip as if it were the deduction. If the client wants it shown, it must be a separate, labelled line (CCG Module 10.3). |
| Percentage attributes | Configuration | Rate attributes (NIE077 and the other six @Porcentaje, NIE161, NIE164, NIE167, NIE169, NIE121, NIE171) carry the rate itself, expressed to two decimals - 4.00 not 0.04, 25.00 not 0.25. | Emitting 0.04 for the 4% health rate passes schema validation and produces a document that is internally inconsistent with @Deduccion. |
| RetencionFuente (NIE177) - RESIDENT | Payroll engine (tax) | Build the ET art. 388 depurated base: gross taxable payments, less the mandatory health, pension and solidarity-fund contributions (ingresos no constitutivos de renta), less the art. 387 deductions elected by the employee (dependants at 10% of gross capped at 32 UVT per month, prepaid health up to 16 UVT per month, housing-loan interest up to 100 UVT per month), less the 25% exempt income of art. 206 num. 10. Apply the two caps: deductions plus exempt income may not exceed 40% of gross less non-constitutive income, nor 1,340 UVT per year (Mercans apportions at 111.6667 UVT per month - see CO-GAP-05). Convert the resulting base to UVT at 52,374, read the art. 383 table, convert back to pesos, and approximate to the nearest 1,000 under ET art. 868. Under Procedure 2 apply instead the fixed percentage recomputed each June and December on the preceding twelve months divided by 13. | Exclude severance savings and the interest on them from the monthly base under BOTH procedures (art. 206 num. 4). Under Procedure 1 the statutory minimum service bonus gets its OWN separate read of the table (art. 385) and does not merge into the ordinary base. For a sub-30-day period apply the art. 385 rule: gross the payments to 30 days, read the table for a PERCENTAGE, then apply that percentage to the actual smaller payment - never read the table for an amount on the grossed-up figure. |
| RetencionFuente (NIE177) - NON-RESIDENT | Payroll engine (tax) | Apply a FLAT 20% to the GROSS payment under ET art. 408. Do not depurate: no deduction of the mandatory contributions, no art. 387 deductions, no 25% exempt income, no tax-free band, no Procedure 1 or 2 election. This applies to salary, to the service bonus, to severance savings, to dismissal indemnity, to equity income and to benefits in kind alike. Carry a residence flag with an effective date on the employee record, re-evaluate it EVERY period against the art. 10 test (more than 183 days in any 365-day period), and switch to the art. 383 table PROSPECTIVELY from the payment following the date the test is met. | Never apply the switch retroactively and never true up the earlier months through payroll - DIAN Concepto 011108 of 29 June 2026 expressly rejects anticipating future residence. The employee reconciles on their own return. Where the work is performed ENTIRELY outside Colombia the income is foreign-source and there is no withholding at all; LugarTrabajoPais (NIE050) is the evidencing field. Treaty relief is applied ONLY on written client instruction with a residence certificate. |
| AuxilioTransporte (NIE071) | Payroll engine | Emit only where the employee's salary does not exceed 2 SMMLV (COP 3,501,810 in 2026). The 2026 amount is COP 249,095 per month, prorated by days worked. Where the employee is above the threshold, OMIT the element rather than emitting zero. | The allowance is NOT salary for contribution purposes but IS included in the base of cesantias, prima and the severance indemnity (Ley 1 de 1963 art. 7). Carrying a single 'transport' wage type with one base flag will get one of the two treatments wrong. |
| Cesantias (NIE120) and PagoIntereses (NIE122) | Payroll engine | Cesantias accrue at one month's salary per year of service, on salary plus the transport allowance. Interest accrues at 12% per annum on the 31 December balance and is paid TO THE EMPLOYEE during January. Where the client has adopted the Ley 2466 de 2025 art. 64 option, the 8.33% is deposited monthly and the 1% interest paid monthly, by written agreement - a configurable engine option. | Cesantias are EXCLUDED from the monthly withholding base but must still be reported here. They split THREE ways downstream - paid to the employee, deposited to the fund, and under the pre-1991 traditional regime - for Formulario 220 casillas 47-49 and Formato 2276 numerals 18-20. Maintain the three buckets from the outset. |
| Meal payments (NIE153 / NIE154) | Payroll engine | Maintain the ET art. 387-1 split independently of the S / NS split: the amount UP TO 41 UVT per month (COP 2,147,334 in 2026) where salary does not exceed 310 UVT (COP 16,235,940) goes to Formato 2276 numeral 32, and the EXCESS goes to numeral 8. Both are still reported in the XML as PagoAlimentacionS or NS. | Two orthogonal splits apply to the same amount - salary / non-salary for the XML and contributions, and under-41-UVT / excess for the information return. Model them as two independent attributes, not one. |
| Incapacity (NIE123-NIE127) | Payroll engine | Emit one Incapacidad element per episode with its dates, day count, type code and amount. For type 1 (comun) the employer bears days 1-2 and the EPS funds from day 3; for types 2 and 3 the ARL funds from day one at 100%. The employer ADVANCES the benefit and recovers it from the institution, so the amount here is what the employee received. | CO-GAP-03: the first-two-days rate is a Mercans configurable default of 66.67% (CST art. 227 two-thirds for the first 90 days); the primary source does not state it. CO-GAP-04: the one-minimum-wage floor on incapacity pay follows settled practice, not a retrieved regulation. Both require the client's documented election. |
| AltoRiesgoPension (NIE043) | Employee master data | Set true where the employee performs an activity listed in Decreto Ley 2090 de 2003. The flag is always emitted (1-1); write the literal true or false, not 1 or 0. | CO-GAP-02: the ADDITIONAL employer pension contribution for high-risk work (the Resolucion 2388 rate table implies 26% total against the standard 16%, so ten additional points borne by the employer) and its precise base were not confirmed from a primary 2026 source. Verify before configuring any high-risk employee. |
| NIE014 / NIE015 and NIE051 / NIE052 | Engine (validation) | The five-digit municipality code must begin with the two-digit department code. Validate this for both the generation location and the place of work before transmission. | DIVIPOLA codes are re-used across departments in their last three digits; only the full five-digit code is unique. |
| NIE065 and NIE066 / NIE067 / NIE068 | Engine (validation) | Where the payment method is a bank-mediated medium (42, 45, 46, 47, 48, 49), the bank, account type and account number must all be present. Where it is 10 (Efectivo), all three must be absent. | Colombia does NOT use IBAN. Account numbers are numeric, of bank-specific length, with no national check digit, so no structural validation is possible - only a bank-specific rule or pre-notification. |
| NIE199 and NIE204 | Engine (validation) | Where the Novedad element is present, CUNENov is mandatory inside it; where Novedad is absent, CUNENov must not appear. Bidirectional. | A Novedad without a referenced CUNE breaks the cross-reference chain for the employment relationship. |
| NIAE214 and the Reemplazar / Eliminar blocks | Engine (validation) | TipoNota = 1 requires the Reemplazar block and forbids Eliminar; TipoNota = 2 requires Eliminar and forbids Reemplazar. Mutually exclusive. | The Eliminar CUNE uses ValDev = ValDed = ValTol = 0.00 and DocEmp = 0 (Anexo s.8.1.1.4). A shared CUNE routine that reads the real totals will produce a wrong hash for cancellations. |
| NIE041 and NIE042 and NIE164 | Engine (validation) | Where SubTipoTrabajador (NIE042) = 01 (employed old-age pensioner), the pension rate NIE164 must be 0.00 and no pension deduction may be emitted. | The PILA equivalent (subtipo 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 9 or 12 forces field 46 to zero) is broader; map, do not copy. |
| NIE157 and NIE061 and NIE041 | Engine (validation) | Where the apprentice maintenance support (NIE157) is populated, the contract type must be 4 (Aprendizaje) and the worker type must be 12 or 19. | Ley 2466 de 2025 art. 21 made the apprenticeship a labour contract from 2026, so an apprentice now also generates a full PILA settlement - not only the support payment. |
| Cross-report reconciliation | Engine (reconciliation) | Monthly: SUM(NIE177) over all employees must equal Formulario 350 casilla 93 after the nearest-1,000 rounding. SUM(NIE163), SUM(NIE166) and SUM(NIE168 + NIE170) must equal the PILA totals for health, pension and the solidarity fund, allowing for PILA's round-up-to-100. Annually: the accumulation of each Devengados element must equal the corresponding Formulario 220 casilla and Formato 2276 numeral. | Run the reconciliation BEFORE transmission. A mismatch discovered after validation can only be corrected by an adjustment note, which consumes a new document number. |


## CO-DIAN-001-NominaElectronica - Sheet 8: Example Analysis


```
EXAMPLE ANALYSIS - SAMPLE RECORD PARSED FIELD BY FIELD
```


| Raw Value | Field Name | Interpretation | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| (scenario) | Employer | MERCANS COLOMBIA S.A.S., NIT 900123456-7, Bogota D.C. (department 11, municipality 11001). | The same employer, employee and period are used across all six Colombia Spec workbooks so the cross-report reconciliations can be followed end to end. |
| (scenario) | Employee | Juan Carlos Perez Gomez, cedula de ciudadania 1020304050, hired 11 January 2021, indefinite-term contract, contractual salary COP 12,000,000, not integral, not high-risk, tax RESIDENT. | Salary is above 2 SMMLV so there is no transport allowance, and below 16 SMMLV so there is no subsistence surcharge - which keeps the example legible while still exercising the solidarity surcharge. |
| V1.0: Documento Soporte de Pago de Nomina Electronica | Version (NIE022) | The mandatory constant literal. | Any deviation is rejected by rule NIE022. |
| 1 | Ambiente (NIE023) | Production environment. | Also CUNE input [11]. A test document (2) can never be replayed into production. |
| 102 | TipoXML (NIE202) | NominaIndividual - the payroll support document itself. | 103 would be an adjustment note. CUNE input [9]. |
| NE1042 | Numero (NIE012) | Prefix NE + consecutive 1042. | Composite: positions 1-2 = authorised prefix, remainder = consecutive. CUNE input [1]. Rule 90 permits this number only once for this employee. |
| 2026-04-05 | FechaGen (NIE026) | Document generated on 5 April 2026. | Within the first ten days of the month following the 31 March payment, so the art. 8 deadline is met. CUNE input [2]. |
| 10:53:10-05:00 | HoraGen (NIE027) | Generated at 10:53:10 Colombian time. | The -05:00 offset is part of the string and part of CUNE input [3]. Colombia has no daylight saving, so the offset never varies. |
| 5 | PeriodoNomina (NIE029) | Monthly pay cycle. | Had the employee been paid quincenal this would read 4 - and there would still be exactly ONE document, with two FechaPago occurrences. |
| COP | TipoMoneda (NIE030) | Colombian peso. | Mandatory under art. 26. TRM (NIE200) stays absent. |
| 900123456 | Empleador/@NIT (NIE033) | Employer tax number without the verification digit. | CUNE input [7] uses the NIT ALONE. Including the DV here is a classic hash failure. |
| 7 | Empleador/@DV (NIE034) | Verification digit of the employer NIT. | Carried separately; deliberately excluded from the CUNE. |
| 11 / 11001 | Empleador department / municipality (NIE036 / NIE037) | Bogota D.C. | The five-digit municipality begins with the two-digit department. Art. 5 item 11. |
| 01 | TipoTrabajador (NIE041) | Dependiente - standard employee. | One of only sixteen values in annex table 5.5.3. The PILA equivalent for this employee is tipo cotizante 1 - the codes are similar but the lists are not the same. |
| 00 | SubTipoTrabajador (NIE042) | No aplica. | Only 00 and 01 exist in table 5.5.4. Had this been an employed old-age pensioner (01), the pension rate NIE164 would have to be 0.00. |
| false | AltoRiesgoPension (NIE043) | Not a Decreto Ley 2090 de 2003 high-risk activity. | Always emitted, as the literal true or false. See CO-GAP-02 before setting true. |
| 13 / 1020304050 | TipoDocumento / NumeroDocumento (NIE044 / NIE045) | Cedula de ciudadania number 1020304050. | CUNE input [8]. Had this been a passport (41), CO-GAP-07 would bite: the XSD types the number as xsd:integer, so an alphanumeric passport number cannot be schema-valid. |
| 2 / 12000000.00 | TipoContrato / Sueldo (NIE061 / NIE062) | Indefinite-term contract, contractual salary COP 12,000,000. | Sueldo is the CONTRACTUAL figure and does not move with days worked; SueldoTrabajado (NIE070) does. |
| 2021-01-11 | FechaIngreso (NIE002) | Hired 11 January 2021. | Drives TiempoLaborado and the cesantias accrual. |
| 2026-03-01 / 2026-03-31 | FechaLiquidacionInicio / Fin (NIE004 / NIE005) | The document settles the whole of March 2026. | One document per employee per month, whatever the pay cycle (art. 3). |
| 1850.00 | TiempoLaborado (NIE006) | Cumulative service on the 360/30 convention: 5 years + 1 month + 20 days from 11 January 2021 to 31 March 2026 = (5 x 360) + (1 x 30) + 20 = 1850.00. | A calendar day-count would give 1905 and be wrong. The annex's own example is 5y 3m 18d = 1908.00. |
| 30 | DiasTrabajados (NIE069) | A full commercial month. | February would also be 30, and so would a 31-day month. Reduced only by unpaid leave, suspension or strike. |
| 2026-03-31 | FechaPago (NIE203) | Single payment on 31 March 2026. | Cardinality 1-N. A quincenal payroll would carry two occurrences here. |
| 12000000.00 | SueldoTrabajado (NIE070) | Full month's basic salary. | The only devengado in this simple month. It is the entire ET art. 388 gross and the entire PILA contribution base. |
| (absent) | AuxilioTransporte (NIE071) | Not payable: COP 12,000,000 exceeds 2 SMMLV (COP 3,501,810). | OMITTED, not zero-filled. Had it been payable it would be COP 249,095 - not salary for contributions, but in the base of cesantias, prima and the severance indemnity. |
| 12000000.00 | DevengadosTotal (NIE187) | Sum of the Devengados block. | Art. 5 item 6. CUNE input [4], truncated to two decimals. |
| 4.00 / 480000.00 | Salud @Porcentaje / @Deduccion (NIE161 / NIE163) | Employee health at 4% of COP 12,000,000. | The employer's 8.5% does NOT appear here - and in this case is nil anyway, because the employee earns under 10 SMMLV and the employer is exonerated under ET art. 114-1. The PILA figure for the same contribution is COP 480,000 rounded UP to the next 100, which happens to coincide here. |
| 4.00 / 480000.00 | FondoPension @Porcentaje / @Deduccion (NIE164 / NIE166) | Employee pension at 4% of COP 12,000,000. | The employer's 12% is a PILA cost, not a deduction. Total pension into the system is 16% = COP 1,920,000. |
| 1.00 / 120000.00 | FondoSP @Porcentaje / @DeduccionSP (NIE167 / NIE168) | Solidarity sub-account surcharge at 1% of COP 12,000,000, because the base is at or above 4 SMMLV (COP 7,003,620). | SEPARATE from pension. Merging this into FondoPension to show a 5% pension line is the single most common Colombian mapping error. |
| (absent) | FondoSP @PorcentajeSub / @DeduccionSub (NIE169 / NIE170) | Subsistence surcharge not payable: the base is below 16 SMMLV (COP 28,014,480). | The two sub-accounts have DIFFERENT triggers - 4 SMMLV and 16 SMMLV. |
| 617000.00 | RetencionFuente (NIE177) | Withholding computed under Procedure 1: gross 12,000,000 less non-constitutive contributions 1,080,000 = 10,920,000; less 25% exempt income 2,730,000 (within both the 40% and the apportioned 1,340-UVT caps) = base 8,190,000; = 156.375 UVT; band 150-360 UVT at 28%: (156.375 - 150) x 0.28 + 10 = 11.785 UVT; x 52,374 = COP 617,232, approximated to the nearest 1,000 under ET art. 868 = COP 617,000. | NON-RESIDENT CONTRAST: the same employee, before acquiring residence, would be withheld at the flat 20% of ET art. 408 on the GROSS - 20% x 12,000,000 = COP 2,400,000, with no depuration and no exempt income. Nearly four times the resident figure. |
| 1697000.00 | DeduccionesTotal (NIE188) | 480,000 + 480,000 + 120,000 + 617,000. | Art. 5 item 7. CUNE input [5]. |
| (absent) | Redondeo (NIE186) | No residual: every concept is a whole peso, so the round-half-to-even sums tie exactly. | Where a residual arises it must be disclosed here, within the +/- 2.00 tolerance, not absorbed into a concept. |
| 10303000.00 | ComprobanteTotal (NIE189) | 12,000,000 - 1,697,000. | Art. 5 item 8. CUNE input [6]. This is the amount that must match the bank payment file. |
| 16560dc8...f3bac2 | CUNE (NIE024) | SHA-384 over NE1042 + 2026-04-05 + 10:53:10-05:00 + 12000000.00 + 1697000.00 + 10303000.00 + 900123456 + 1020304050 + 102 + <PIN> + 1. | 96 hexadecimal characters. The PIN never appears in the XML. Any change to any of the eleven inputs yields a different document. |
| 617000 | -> Formulario 350 casilla 93 | Retenciones - rentas de trabajo, personas naturales. | The March total across all employees, rounded to the nearest 1,000. |
| 8190000 | -> Formulario 350 casilla 77 | Base sujeta a retencion - rentas de trabajo. | The depurated base, not the gross. |
| 12000000 | -> PILA Registro Tipo 2 fields 42, 43, 44, 45 | IBC for pension, health, occupational risk and the family compensation fund. | All four bases coincide here; they diverge as soon as a non-salary payment or the 40% clamp enters. |
| 144000000 | -> Formulario 220 casilla 36 and Formato 2276 numeral 5 | Twelve months of salary at COP 12,000,000. | Annual accumulation. The certificate is issued to the employee by the last business day of March 2027 for the 2026 tax year. |


## CO-DIAN-001-NominaElectronica - Sheet 9: Sample File


```
SAMPLE FILE - XML AND SOAP ENVELOPE - CONSTRUCTED FROM THE OFFICIAL ANEXO TECNICO XPATH INVENTORY

PROVENANCE NOTE - READ FIRST

The SOAP envelopes below are QUOTED VERBATIM from the official Anexo Tecnico
version 1.0, sections 9.5 and 9.7.2.

The XML instance below is CONSTRUCTED from the annex's own XPath inventory
(sections 3 and 4, 195 NIE codes) and populated with the worked example on the
Example Analysis sheet. DIAN publishes no sample instance in the annex and none
was supplied in 00-official-sources/sample-files/, so this is a faithful
reconstruction of the documented structure - NOT an official DIAN sample.
Validate it against XSD v1.0.6 in the DIAN habilitacion environment before use.
The ds:Signature block is shown as a placeholder; it is produced by the signing
component, not by payroll.

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1. TRANSMISSION - SOAP 1.2 request (Anexo Tecnico s.9.5 / s.9.7.2, verbatim)
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
<soap:Envelope xmlns:soap="http://www.w3.org/2003/05/soap-envelope"
               xmlns:wcf="http://wcf.dian.colombia">
   <soap:Header/>
   <soap:Body>
      <wcf:SendNominaSync>
         <!--Optional:-->
         <wcf:contentFile>cid:1057568194758</wcf:contentFile>
      </wcf:SendNominaSync>
   </soap:Body>
</soap:Envelope>

   wcf:contentFile carries the base64 encoding of a ZIP containing the XML below.
   Maximum 500 KB. UTF-8 only. TLS 1.2 with mutual certificate authentication.

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
2. RESPONSE - SOAP 1.2 response header (Anexo Tecnico s.9.7.2, verbatim)
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
<s:Envelope xmlns:s="http://www.w3.org/2003/05/soap-envelope"
            xmlns:a="http://www.w3.org/2005/08/addressing"
            xmlns:u="http://docs.oasis-open.org/wss/2004/01/oasis-200401-wss-wssecurity-utility-1.0.xsd">
   <s:Header>
      <a:Action s:mustUnderstand="1">
         http://wcf.dian.colombia/IWcfDianCustomerServices/SendNominaSyncResponse
      </a:Action>
   </s:Header>
   ...
   <!-- the body carries an ApplicationResponse encoded in Base64 with DIAN's
        validation verdict. THIS MUST BE PARSED AND PERSISTED - a payroll cost
        is deductible only where the document has been validated. -->
</s:Envelope>

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
3. PAYLOAD - NominaIndividual (constructed from the annex XPath inventory)
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<NominaIndividual>

  <UBLExtensions>
    <UBLExtension>
      <ExtensionContent>
        <!-- XAdES-EPES enveloped signature, X.509 from an ONAC-accredited CA,
             ClaimedRole = thirdparty | supplier (Anexo s.7) -->
        <ds:Signature xmlns:ds="http://www.w3.org/2000/09/xmldsig#">...</ds:Signature>
      </ExtensionContent>
    </UBLExtension>
  </UBLExtensions>

  <Periodo FechaIngreso="2021-01-11"
           FechaLiquidacionInicio="2026-03-01"
           FechaLiquidacionFin="2026-03-31"
           TiempoLaborado="1850.00"
           FechaGen="2026-04-05"/>

  <NumeroSecuenciaXML CodigoTrabajador="EMP-00417"
                      Prefijo="NE"
                      Consecutivo="1042"
                      Numero="NE1042"/>

  <LugarGeneracionXML Pais="CO"
                      DepartamentoEstado="11"
                      MunicipioCiudad="11001"
                      Idioma="es"/>

  <ProveedorXML RazonSocial="MERCANS COLOMBIA S.A.S."
                NIT="901234567"
                DV="3"
                SoftwareID="56f2ae23-4a7c-4e51-9b7d-1f2c3d4e5f60"
                SoftwareSC="9b1c2d3e4f5061728394a5b6c7d8e9f0a1b2c3d4e5f6071829
                            3a4b5c6d7e8f9001122334455667788990aabbccddeeff"/>

  <CodigoQR>https://catalogo-vpfe.dian.gov.co/document/searchqr?documentkey=16560dc8...f3bac2</CodigoQR>

  <InformacionGeneral Version="V1.0: Documento Soporte de Pago de Nomina Electronica"
                      Ambiente="1"
                      TipoXML="102"
                      CUNE="16560dc8956122e84ffb743c817fe7d494e058a44d9ca3fa4c234c268b4f766003253fbee7ea4af9682dd57210f3bac2"
                      EncripCUNE="CUNE-SHA384"
                      FechaGen="2026-04-05"
                      HoraGen="10:53:10-05:00"
                      PeriodoNomina="5"
                      TipoMoneda="COP"/>

  <Notas>Nomina correspondiente al mes de marzo de 2026</Notas>

  <Empleador RazonSocial="MERCANS COLOMBIA S.A.S."
             NIT="900123456"
             DV="7"
             Pais="CO"
             DepartamentoEstado="11"
             MunicipioCiudad="11001"
             Direccion="CALLE 100 No 7-33 PISO 12"/>

  <Trabajador TipoTrabajador="01"
              SubTipoTrabajador="00"
              AltoRiesgoPension="false"
              TipoDocumento="13"
              NumeroDocumento="1020304050"
              PrimerApellido="PEREZ"
              SegundoApellido="GOMEZ"
              PrimerNombre="JUAN"
              OtrosNombres="CARLOS"
              LugarTrabajoPais="CO"
              LugarTrabajoDepartamentoEstado="11"
              LugarTrabajoMunicipioCiudad="11001"
              LugarTrabajoDireccion="CALLE 100 No 7-33 PISO 12"
              SalarioIntegral="false"
              TipoContrato="2"
              Sueldo="12000000.00"
              CodigoTrabajador="EMP-00417"/>

  <Pago Forma="1"
        Metodo="42"
        Banco="BANCOLOMBIA"
        TipoCuenta="AHORROS"
        NumeroCuenta="12345678901"/>

  <FechasPagos>
    <FechaPago>2026-03-31</FechaPago>
  </FechasPagos>

  <Devengados>
    <Basico DiasTrabajados="30" SueldoTrabajado="12000000.00"/>
    <!-- AuxilioTransporte OMITTED: salary exceeds 2 SMMLV (COP 3,501,810).
         Omitted, never zero-filled. -->
    <!-- No overtime, vacation, prima, cesantias, incapacity or leave in this month. -->
  </Devengados>

  <Deducciones>
    <Salud Porcentaje="4.00" Deduccion="480000.00"/>
    <FondoPension Porcentaje="4.00" Deduccion="480000.00"/>
    <FondoSP Porcentaje="1.00" DeduccionSP="120000.00"/>
    <!-- PorcentajeSub / DeduccionSub OMITTED: base is below 16 SMMLV
         (COP 28,014,480). The two sub-accounts have different triggers. -->
    <RetencionFuente>617000.00</RetencionFuente>
  </Deducciones>

  <DevengadosTotal>12000000.00</DevengadosTotal>
  <DeduccionesTotal>1697000.00</DeduccionesTotal>
  <ComprobanteTotal>10303000.00</ComprobanteTotal>

</NominaIndividual>

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
4. NON-RESIDENT VARIANT (v2.8 control B1)
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
The same document for a NON-RESIDENT employee differs in exactly one element -
there is no residence attribute in the schema:

  <Deducciones>
    <Salud Porcentaje="4.00" Deduccion="480000.00"/>
    <FondoPension Porcentaje="4.00" Deduccion="480000.00"/>
    <FondoSP Porcentaje="1.00" DeduccionSP="120000.00"/>
    <RetencionFuente>2400000.00</RetencionFuente>   <!-- ET art. 408: 20% x GROSS -->
  </Deducciones>

  <DevengadosTotal>12000000.00</DevengadosTotal>
  <DeduccionesTotal>3480000.00</DeduccionesTotal>
  <ComprobanteTotal>8520000.00</ComprobanteTotal>

Flat 20% of ET art. 408 applied to the GROSS COP 12,000,000, with no art. 388
depuration, no 25% exempt income, no art. 387 deductions and no tax-free band
(DIAN Concepto 011108 of 29 June 2026). Where the work were performed ENTIRELY
outside Colombia, LugarTrabajoPais would not be CO and there would be NO
withholding at all (DIAN Oficio 28380 of 2019).

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
5. ADJUSTMENT NOTE SKELETON (NominaIndividualDeAjuste, TipoXML 103)
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<NominaIndividualDeAjuste>
  <TipoNota>1</TipoNota>                    <!-- 1 Reemplazar | 2 Eliminar -->
  <Reemplazar>
    <!-- the ENTIRE NominaIndividual structure repeated with corrected values,
         under NIAE-prefixed codes, carrying its OWN CUNE computed on the same
         eleven inputs taken from this Reemplazar block -->
  </Reemplazar>
</NominaIndividualDeAjuste>

For TipoNota = 2 the Eliminar block is used instead, and its CUNE is computed
with ValDev = ValDed = ValTol = 0.00 and DocEmp = 0 (Anexo s.8.1.1.4). A shared
CUNE routine that reads the real totals will produce a wrong hash for a
cancellation - this is a deliberate divergence in the specification, not an
error in it.
```
