The employer chooses one commercially; the code is therefore client-specific and must be obtained at implementation, not configured from a national catalogue. |
| (6 positions) | - | Codigos de administradoras | T2R1-014 (employer ARL), T2R2-031 / 032 (AFP), T2R2-033 / 034 (EPS), T2R2-035 (CCF) and T2R2-077 (employee ARL) are all SIX positions. The catalogue is maintained by the Ministry and distributed through the operadores de informacion; the placeholders used in the worked example (AFP001, EPS001, CCF001, ARL001) are illustrative only and MUST be replaced with the real codes obtained from the client's operador. |


## CO-PILA-003-AportesSeguridadSocial - Sheet 7: Population Rules


```
POPULATION RULES - HOW TO FILL EACH FIELD FROM PAYROLL
```


| Field Name | Source System | Population Logic | Edge Cases |
|---|---|---|---|
| The five contribution bases (T2R2-042 to T2R2-045 and T2R2-095) | Payroll engine | Compute and STORE five separate bases per employee per month. Four are the IBC for pension, health, occupational risk and the CCF: total salary-constituting payments, plus the excess over the 40% non-salary clamp of Ley 1393 de 2010 art. 30, floored at 1 SMMLV (COP 1,750,905) and capped at 25 SMMLV (COP 43,772,625). The fifth is the PARAFISCAL base of campo 95, defined by Ley 21 de 1982 art. 17: it INCLUDES paid rest days and it is UNCAPPED. Never populate all five from one variable. | The four IBC values legitimately differ from one another once a change event enters - for example health may remain payable during an unpaid leave where pension does not. The header total in T2R1-020 is the sum of campo 95, NOT of campo 45; using the CCF base there is a silent, systematic understatement for any employer whose employees earn above the ceiling or who has significant paid rest days. |
| The four day counts (T2R2-036 to T2R2-039) | Payroll engine | Maintain a contributory-day counter PER SUBSYSTEM on the 30-day commercial month. Start at 30 (or the proportion from a mid-month hire or to a mid-month leave), then apply the change events: SLN and lawful strike reduce all four; IGE and IRL follow the subsystem-specific rules; vacation and remunerated leave do not reduce them at all because they are paid. | February is 30 days and a 31-day month is 30 days. The counts must reconcile to the novedad date pairs: a five-day SLN in a full month gives 25 contributory days and dates in T2R2-083 and T2R2-084 that span exactly five days. |
| Health period one month ahead (T2R1-016) | Engine (control) | Set the health payment period to the month AFTER the period in T2R1-015. Health is paid one month in advance; the other subsystems are paid in arrears. A March salary settlement carries period 2026-03 in T2R1-015 and 2026-04 in T2R1-016. | Setting the two equal is the single most common first-run defect. Note also the split of responsibility the annex records: T2R1-015 is CALCULATED BY THE OPERADOR, while T2R1-016 is SUPPLIED BY THE EMPLOYER. |
| Exoneration flags (A1-033 and T2R2-076) | Engine (control) | A1-033 is an EMPLOYER-LEVEL, standing declaration that the employer qualifies under ET art. 114-1. T2R2-076 is a PER-EMPLOYEE, PER-MONTH application of it, and may be S only where A1-033 is S. The employee test is that the health base (T2R2-043) is BELOW 10 SMMLV = COP 17,509,050 in 2026; at or above that it MUST be N. Where a NATURAL-person employer employs two or more workers and the base reaches 10 SMMLV it must also be N. Where T2R2-076 is S, set T2R2-054 to the EMPLOYEE rate alone (4.0000%), and set the SENA and ICBF rates and values (T2R2-066 to T2R2-069) to zero. | Because the test is MONTHLY, an employee who crosses 10 SMMLV in a bonus month loses the exoneration for that month alone and regains it the next. Evaluating it annually, or at hire, systematically under-contributes. The same test drives Formulario 350 casilla 27. |
| Header-to-detail consistency | Engine (validation) | Before submission assert: T2R1-004, 005, 006 and 007 equal Archivo Tipo 1 campos 1, 2, 3 and 4 byte for byte; T2R1-011, 012 and 013 equal Archivo Tipo 1 campos 10, 5 and 6; T2R1-021 equals Archivo Tipo 1 campo 30; T2R1-019 equals the count of UNIQUE contributors in the detail EXCLUDING those with contributor type 40; and T2R1-020 equals the sum of T2R2-095 across the detail. | The type-40 exclusion from the contributor count is easy to miss and produces a header-to-detail mismatch the operador rejects outright. |
| Pension rate forced to zero | Engine (validation) | Where the contributor sub-type (T2R2-006) is 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 9 or 12, the pension rate (T2R2-046) MUST be 0 and no pension contribution may be settled. | The nomina electronica equivalent is narrower - only SubTipoTrabajador 01 forces the rate to zero there. Map between the two lists; do not assume they agree. |
| Health rate may be zero only in two cases | Engine (validation) | The annex is explicit: T2R2-054 may be reported as zero ONLY where (a) SLN (T2R2-024) is X and the exoneration flag (T2R2-076) is S, or (b) SLN is C and the employer's legal nature is public. And where the health contribution (T2R2-055) is zero, the health days (T2R2-037), the health base (T2R2-043) and the health rate (T2R2-054) must ALL be zero - except for those same SLN-plus-exoneration contributors. | This is a four-field interlock. Implement it as one validation, not four independent ones. |
| Novedad flag to date-pair interlock | Engine (validation) | Every marked novedad flag must have its corresponding date field or pair populated, and every populated date field must have its flag marked: ING to 080, RET to 081, VSP to 082, SLN to 083 and 084, IGE to 085 and 086, LMA to 087 and 088, VAC-LR to 089 and 090, VCT to 091 and 092, IRL days to 093 and 094, TAE to 034, TAP to 032, AVP to 048 and 049. | Build the interlock as a table, not as a chain of conditionals - there are twelve pairs and a missed one produces a rejection at the operador rather than at Mercans. |
| Contribution calculation checks published in the annex | Engine (validation) | The annex prints the arithmetic the operador will apply: campo 55 = campo 43 x campo 54; campo 65 = campo 45 x campo 64; campo 67 = campo 95 x campo 66; campo 69 = campo 95 x campo 68; campo 71 = campo 95 x campo 70; campo 73 = campo 95 x campo 72. Note that the CCF uses the CCF base while SENA, ICBF, ESAP and MEN all use the PARAFISCAL base. | Reproduce these checks internally before submission. Each must then be rounded UP to the next multiple of 100. |
| Rounding is UP, not to nearest | Engine (formatting) | Round the contribution base UP to the whole peso, and round EACH contribution value UP to the next multiple of 100 (Decreto 1990 de 2016 art. 1). Worked illustration: occupational risk at class I on a base of 12,000,000 is 0.522% = 62,640, which becomes 62,700. | This is neither the nomina electronica convention (2 decimals, round-half-to-even) nor the Formulario 350 convention (nearest 1,000). Blending them produces reconciliation differences that look like errors but are not - the reconciliation must be run on the UNROUNDED values with a tolerance. |
| Numeric and alphanumeric justification | Engine (formatting) | Numeric fields are right-justified and ZERO-filled to the left; alphanumeric fields are left-justified and SPACE-filled to the right. The annex states this at s.0 and it applies without exception across all three records. | An empty numeric field is a run of zeros, not a run of spaces. This catches optional numeric fields such as A1-016 (Fax) and the unused parafiscal rate and value fields. |
| Rate fields carry implied decimals | Engine (formatting) | The rate fields are numeric with implied decimals in their published width - seven positions for T2R2-046, 054, 064, 066, 068, 070 and 072, and nine for T2R2-061. Confirm the implied decimal placement with the chosen operador before go-live; the annex does not print it unambiguously for every rate field. | FORMAT TO CONFIRM. This is the one presentation detail of the layout that the annex leaves under-specified, and it differs between the seven-position and nine-position rate fields. |
| The 40% non-salary clamp | Payroll engine | Total the salary and the non-salary payments for the period. Where non-salary payments exceed 40% of total remuneration, add the EXCESS back into the four contribution bases (Ley 1393 de 2010 art. 30). Store the added-back amount as its own quantity: it is IN the contribution base but OUT of the statutory-benefit base for cesantias, prima and the severance indemnity. | The clamp is computed on the period's totals, so a single large non-salary bonus can push an otherwise compliant employee over the line for one month. It also interacts with the exoneration test, because the added-back amount raises the health base toward the 10-SMMLV threshold. |
| All-inclusive salary at 70% | Payroll engine | Where the employee is on salario integral (CST art. 132 num. 2, minimum 13 SMMLV = COP 22,761,765 in 2026), mark T2R2-041 and set the four IBC fields to 70% of the integral salary. The remaining 30% is the statutory prestacional factor and is not a contribution base. | Note the interaction with the ceiling: 70% of a large integral salary can still exceed 25 SMMLV, in which case the ceiling applies to the 70% figure, not to the full salary. |
| Work centre and risk class | Employee master data | Carry the work centre on the employee record and derive the risk class from it, not from the employer. Where an employee performs several occupations, contribute at the HIGHEST class (Decreto 1072 de 2015 art. 2.2.4.2.5.6). Where the employee moves work centre mid-period, mark VCT (T2R2-029) and populate T2R2-091 and T2R2-092. | A mid-period move can change the ARL rate. Confirm with the operador whether the period is settled at one rate or split - the annex does not prescribe a split, so market practice governs. |
| Foreign employee electing out of pension | Employee master data | Mark T2R2-007 where the employee is a foreign national who has proved affiliation to a pension system abroad and elected out under Decreto 1072 de 2015 art. 2.2.1.2.1.4. Health, occupational risk and the parafiscal levies remain fully payable. The election narrows the valid document types to CE, PA, CD, SC and PE. | THIS IS NOT A TAX FIELD. It says nothing about tax residence: the same employee may be withheld at the flat 20% of ET art. 408 on hoja 2 of Formulario 350 while contributing to Colombian health and occupational risk on ordinary terms. |
| Incapacity and leave advanced by the employer | Payroll engine | Colombian employers universally ADVANCE the EPS and ARL benefits through payroll and recover from the institution. Report the IGE and LMA authorisation numbers (T2R2-057, T2R2-059) and values (T2R2-058, T2R2-060), and model the benefit as PAID to the employee and RECEIVABLE from the institution. | Without the authorisation number the institution will not reimburse. CO-GAP-03 and CO-GAP-04: the employer's first-two-days rate for general illness (Mercans default 66.67%) and the one-minimum-wage floor on incapacity pay are configurable and need the client's documented election. |
| A1-009 drives A1-004, A1-018, A1-020, A1-021 and A1-023 | Engine (validation) | Where the employer person type is J, the verification digit and the legal representative's identification number, document type, first surname and first name are all mandatory. Where it is N, the verification digit must be ZERO and the legal-representative block is optional. | Note the asymmetry: A1-019 (the representative's own verification digit) is ALWAYS zero whatever the person type, because the annex says so expressly. |
| A1-010 drives A1-005 and A1-006 | Engine (validation) | Where the form of presentation is U, the branch code and name must be BLANK. Where it is S, both must be populated - and where the branch has no name, the annex directs that the CODE be repeated in the name field. | The same interlock repeats at T2R1-011, T2R1-012 and T2R1-013. |
| T2R1-008 drives T2R1-009 and T2R1-010 | Engine (validation) | Where the planilla type is E, A, I, M, S, Y, T, X or K, both associated-planilla fields must be blank (or zero for the numeric one). Where it is N or F they carry the period planilla; where it is U, the judicial deposit title; where it is J or O, the planilla of the original payment. | The annex adds that where the operador cannot find the initial planilla for that contributor and period, it must NOT permit the correction planilla at all. |
| A1-007 = D drives A1-031, A1-032 and A1-034 | Engine (validation) | A Ley 1429 de 2010 beneficiary must carry the commercial-registry date and the registered department, and the registry date must fall between 29 December 2010 and 31 December 2014. | The window is closed and historical: an employer registered after 2014 cannot be class D, whatever its size. |
| T2R2-008 drives T2R2-009, T2R2-010 and T2R2-097 | Engine (validation) | Where 'Colombiano en el exterior' is marked, the DIVIPOLA work-location department and municipality must be BLANK and the foreign filing date becomes relevant. The flag is available only for document types CC and TI. | Contributor type 40 also blanks the two DIVIPOLA fields, for a different reason. |
| Cross-report reconciliation | Engine (reconciliation) | After settlement assert: T2R2-055 (employee portion) reconciles to nomina electronica NIE163; the employee 4% within T2R2-047 reconciles to NIE166; T2R2-051 and T2R2-052 reconcile to NIE168 and NIE170. Reconcile on the UNROUNDED values with a tolerance of 100 pesos per contribution, because PILA rounds up to the next 100 and the XML does not. | The employer-borne amounts - pension 12%, health 8.5%, occupational risk, CCF, SENA, ICBF - have NO nomina electronica counterpart at all and cannot be reconciled to it. They reconcile only to the employer-cost ledger. |


## CO-PILA-003-AportesSeguridadSocial - Sheet 8: Example Analysis


```
EXAMPLE ANALYSIS - SAMPLE RECORD PARSED FIELD BY FIELD
```


| Raw Value | Field Name | Interpretation | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| (scenario) | Employer | MERCANS COLOMBIA S.A.S., NIT 900123456-7, private legal person, fewer than 200 contributors, exonerated under ET art. 114-1, Bogota D.C. | Same employer as the other five Colombia Spec workbooks. |
| (scenario) | Contributor | Juan Carlos Perez Gomez, CC 1020304050, dependiente, full month, salary COP 12,000,000, risk class I. | Below 10 SMMLV (COP 17,509,050), so the art. 114-1 exoneration applies; at or above 4 SMMLV (COP 7,003,620), so the solidarity surcharge applies; below 16 SMMLV, so the subsistence surcharge does not. |
| NI / 900123456 / 7 | A1-002 / A1-003 / A1-004 | Employer document type, number and verification digit. | The DV is mandatory because A1-009 = J. All three are echoed byte for byte into T2R1-005, T2R1-006 and T2R1-007. |
| B | A1-007 - Clase de aportante | Fewer than 200 contributors, counted across all branches. | Class C would require the operador to verify MONTHLY against the DIAN file under Decreto 525 de 2009 art. 6; class D would require a commercial-registry date between 29 Dec 2010 and 31 Dec 2014. |
| J / U | A1-009 / A1-010 | Legal person; single consolidated planilla. | U forces the branch code and name (A1-005, A1-006) to be blank, here and in T2R1-012 and T2R1-013. |
| 11 / 001 | A1-013 / A1-012 | Department 11, municipality 001 - Bogota D.C. | PILA splits DIVIPOLA across two fields; the nomina electronica carries the combined five-digit code 11001. Concatenate department + municipality to convert. |
| 0 | A1-019 - DV del representante legal | ALWAYS zero. | The annex says expressly 'Este campo se debera reportar en cero'. Do not compute a check digit for the representative - a genuinely counter-intuitive rule. |
| S | A1-033 - Aportante exonerado | The employer declares the ET art. 114-1 exoneration. | This is the standing employer-level declaration. It is applied per employee per month at T2R2-076, and it rests on the same test as Formulario 350 casilla 27. |
| N | A1-034 - Beneficios art. 5 Ley 1429 | Not a Ley 1429 beneficiary. | Distinct from A1-033: the CCF 4% is NEVER exonerated under art. 114-1, so this is the only route to relief on it. |
| 01 / 1 / E | T2R1-001 / T2R1-002 / T2R1-008 | Header record, electronic modality, planilla de empleados. | Record type 01 distinguishes the header from the 02 detail records in the same file. |
| 2026-03 / 2026-04 | T2R1-015 / T2R1-016 | Pension, occupational risk and levies for March; HEALTH FOR APRIL. | COLOMBIA'S MOST-MISSED PILA RULE. Health is paid one month in advance. Setting these equal is the classic first-run defect. Note also that T2R1-015 is calculated by the OPERADOR while T2R1-016 is supplied by the EMPLOYER. |
| 00001 | T2R1-019 - Numero total de cotizantes | One contributor. | Must exclude any contributor whose type (T2R2-005) is 40 - Beneficiario UPC Adicional. |
| 000012000000 | T2R1-020 - Valor total de la nomina | The sum of the PARAFISCAL base (T2R2-095) across the detail - not the CCF base. | The annex defines it as 'la sumatoria de los IBC para el pago de los aportes de PARAFISCALES'. Using campo 45 instead of campo 95 is a silent, systematic understatement for any employer with employees above the ceiling or with material paid rest days. |
| 30 / 30 / 30 / 30 | T2R2-036 to T2R2-039 | Full contributory month for pension, health, occupational risk and the CCF. | Four SEPARATE counters. They diverge as soon as a change event enters - a five-day SLN gives 25 and a date pair spanning exactly five days. |
| 012000000 x4 | T2R2-042 to T2R2-045 | The four IBC values, all COP 12,000,000 in this simple month. | Between the floor of 1 SMMLV (COP 1,750,905) and the ceiling of 25 SMMLV (COP 43,772,625). They coincide here only because there is no non-salary payment and no change event. |
| 012000000 | T2R2-095 - IBC otros parafiscales | The FIFTH base, for SENA, ICBF, ESAP and MEN. | Defined by Ley 21 de 1982 art. 17: includes paid rest days and is UNCAPPED. Equal to the others here, but it diverges as soon as the employee earns above the ceiling. |
| 0160000 | T2R2-046 - Tarifa de aportes pensiones | 16% total - employer 12% plus employee 4%. | Would be forced to 0 if the contributor sub-type were 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 9 or 12. |
| 001920000 | T2R2-047 - Cotizacion obligatoria a pensiones | 16% of COP 12,000,000 = COP 1,920,000. Already a multiple of 100. | THE WHOLE contribution, employer and employee together. The nomina electronica reports only the employee's 4% (COP 480,000) at NIE166 - the two figures are not interchangeable. |
| 000120000 | T2R2-051 - FSP subcuenta de solidaridad | 1% of COP 12,000,000 = COP 120,000, because the base is at or above 4 SMMLV. | Employee-borne and ADDITIONAL to the 4% pension. Reconciles to nomina electronica NIE168. Reported SEPARATELY from pension in both artefacts - merging them is the most common Colombian mapping error. |
| 000000000 | T2R2-052 - FSP subcuenta de subsistencia | Nil: the base is below 16 SMMLV (COP 28,014,480). | The two sub-accounts have DIFFERENT triggers - 4 SMMLV and 16 SMMLV. |
| S | T2R2-076 - Cotizante exonerado de pago | The employee earns below 10 SMMLV and the employer has declared itself exonerated in A1-033. | TESTED MONTHLY. A bonus that pushes this employee above COP 17,509,050 in one month forces this field to N for that month alone. |
| 0040000 | T2R2-054 - Tarifa de aportes salud | 4% - THE EMPLOYEE RATE ALONE, because the exoneration applies. | The annex directs at s.2.1.2.3.21 that where A1-033 and T2R2-076 are both S, the rate to enter here is 'la tarifa correspondiente al trabajador'. Without the exoneration this field would read 0125000 (12.5%) and the contribution would be COP 1,500,000. |
| 000480000 | T2R2-055 - Cotizacion obligatoria a salud | 4% of COP 12,000,000 = COP 480,000. The annex's own check is campo 43 x campo 54. | Reconciles to nomina electronica NIE163, which reports the same COP 480,000 - here they coincide because the figure is already a multiple of 100. |
| 1 / 000005220 | T2R2-078 / T2R2-061 | Risk class I, rate 0.522%. | The class attaches to the WORK CENTRE (T2R2-062), not to the employer. |
| 000062700 | T2R2-063 - Cotizacion obligatoria riesgos laborales | 0.522% of COP 12,000,000 = COP 62,640, ROUNDED UP to the next multiple of 100 = COP 62,700. | THE CLEAREST ILLUSTRATION OF THE PILA ROUNDING CONVENTION. Decreto 1990 de 2016 art. 1 rounds UP, never to nearest. Employer-borne in full - there is no employee occupational-risk deduction in Colombia, which is why no nomina electronica element corresponds to it. |
| 0040000 / 000480000 | T2R2-064 / T2R2-065 | CCF at 4% of COP 12,000,000 = COP 480,000. | NEVER exonerated under art. 114-1. Only the Ley 1429 art. 5 regime gives relief on the CCF, and this employer has declared N at A1-034. |
| 0000000 / 000000000 | T2R2-066 / T2R2-067 | SENA at 0% and nil. | Exonerated under ET art. 114-1 because T2R2-076 is S. Without the exoneration this would be 2% of the PARAFISCAL base = COP 240,000. |
| 0000000 / 000000000 | T2R2-068 / T2R2-069 | ICBF at 0% and nil. | Exonerated. Without it, 3% of the parafiscal base = COP 360,000. The exoneration is therefore worth COP 1,620,000 a month for this one employee - health 1,020,000 plus SENA 240,000 plus ICBF 360,000. |
| 0 / 0 | T2R2-070 to T2R2-073 | ESAP and MEN at nil. | Public-sector levies. Out of scope for private corporate payroll, but the positions remain in the record and must be zero-filled. |
| 480000 |  | Employee health. | Coincides exactly here. It will differ by up to 99 pesos where the raw figure is not a multiple of 100 - PILA rounds up, the XML does not. |
| 480000 (4% within 1,920,000) |  | Employee pension. | The XML reports the employee 4% alone; PILA campo 47 reports the whole 16%. Derive, do not copy. |
| 120000 |  | Solidarity fund. | Reported separately in both artefacts. Never merged into pension. |
| (no counterpart) | Employer 12% pension, 8.5% health, ARL, CCF, SENA, ICBF | COP 1,440,000 + 0 + 62,700 + 480,000 + 0 + 0 = COP 1,982,700 of employer cost. | None of this appears on the nomina electronica, which reports only what the employee accrued and what was deducted from the employee. It reconciles to the employer-cost ledger alone. |


## CO-PILA-003-AportesSeguridadSocial - Sheet 9: Sample File


```
SAMPLE FILE - FIXED-WIDTH RECORDS GENERATED FROM THE OFFICIAL POSITION TABLE

PROVENANCE NOTE - READ FIRST

The three records below were GENERATED PROGRAMMATICALLY by placing each
value at the position published in the official Anexo Tecnico 2 (Resolucion
MSPS 2388 de 2016, AT2 version 09, 16-09-2019) and applying the annex's own
justification rules - numeric fields right-justified and zero-filled,
alphanumeric fields left-justified and space-filled.

The record lengths are therefore EXACT, not approximate:
    Archivo Tipo 1                   567 characters (annex total: 567)
    Archivo Tipo 2, Registro Tipo 1  359 characters (annex total: 359)
    Archivo Tipo 2, Registro Tipo 2  686 characters (annex total: 686)

This is NOT an official MSPS sample file - none is published - but the
LAYOUT is the official layout, byte for byte. What each operador de
informacion wraps around these two files, and what additional validations it
applies, is operador-specific and must be confirmed before go-live.

Administradora codes (AFP001, EPS001, CCF001, ARL001) are PLACEHOLDERS. The
real six-position codes come from the client's chosen operador.

Values are the worked example on the Example Analysis sheet: MERCANS
COLOMBIA S.A.S., NIT 900123456-7, one contributor on COP 12,000,000 for
March 2026, exonerated under ET art. 114-1, risk class I.

    pos 178-183  CCF001 - caja de compensacion familiar
    pos 184-191  30 30 30 30 - the FOUR separate contributory-day counters
    pos 192-200  012000000 - salario basico

    pos 238-244  0160000 - tarifa pension 16% (employer 12 + employee 4)

    pos 290-298  000000000 - FSP subsistencia nil: base is below 16 SMMLV
    pos 308-314  0040000 - tarifa salud 4%: the EMPLOYEE rate alone, because the

    pos 315-323  000480000 - cotizacion salud = IBC salud x tarifa
    pos 381-389  000005220 - tarifa riesgos laborales, class I, 0.522%
    pos 399-407  000062700 - cotizacion ARL: 0.522% of 12,000,000 = 62,640,
                           ROUNDED UP to the next multiple of 100 = 62,700
    pos 408-423  0040000 / 000480000 - CCF 4%, NEVER exonerated
    pos 424-455  zeros - SENA and ICBF nil under the art. 114-1 exoneration
    pos 456-487  zeros - ESAP and MEN, public sector only
    pos 506      S     - COTIZANTE EXONERADO de salud, SENA e ICBF
    pos 507-512  ARL001 - ARL del afiliado
    pos 513      1     - clase de riesgo I
    pos 515-524  2021-01-11 - fecha de ingreso
    pos 665-673  012000000 - IBC OTROS PARAFISCALES (the fifth base, uncapped)
    pos 674-676  000   - horas laboradas (only for tipo cotizante 51)
    pos 677-686  blank - fecha radicacion en el exterior; closes the record at 686

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
4. TOTALS RECORDS - GENERATED BY THE OPERADOR, NOT BY THE EMPLOYER
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Registro Tipo 3   Total aportes del periodo para pensiones
Registro Tipo 4   Total aportes al fondo de solidaridad pensional
Registro Tipo 5   Total aportes para salud
Registro Tipo 6   Total aportes para riesgos laborales
Registro Tipo 7   Total aportes para cajas de compensacion familiar
Registro Tipo 8   Total aportes al SENA
Registro Tipo 9   Total aportes al ICBF
Registro Tipo 10  Total aportes a la ESAP
Registro Tipo 11  Total aportes al Ministerio de Educacion Nacional
Registro Tipo 12  Total a pagar durante el periodo

These records also carry the days-in-arrears count and the interest on
arrears, which the operador computes from the payment date. The employer
neither produces nor validates them - but the ENGINE MUST PARSE THEM from
the settlement response in order to reconcile, capture the planilla number
and payment date, and detect rejected contributor records.
```



---

```


| Report ID | Report Name (EN) | Report Name (Local ES) | Authority | Frequency | Due Date Rule | Submission Method | File Format | Legal Basis | Accuracy Assessment |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| CO-DIAN-004 | Annual Certificate of Income and Withholding on Work and Pension Income - Form 220 | Formulario 220 - Certificado de ingresos y retenciones por rentas de trabajo y de pensiones | DIAN prescribes the form; the EMPLOYER issues it to each employee and retains it. It is NOT filed with DIAN. | Annual, one certificate per employee per tax year. | By the LAST BUSINESS DAY OF MARCH following the fiscal year (DUR 1625 de 2016 art. 1.6.1.13.2.40). For the 2026 tax year, the last business day of March 2027. | ISSUED TO THE EMPLOYEE and retained by the employer. There is NO submission to DIAN: this is a certificate, not a return. Delivery may be paper or electronic, as agreed with the employee. The employer must retain it for the statutory period as evidence of the withholding. | Prescribed form, 82 numbered casillas plus an unnumbered certification and signature block. All money amounts in Colombian pesos, approximated to the NEAREST 1,000, with ZERO written where a casilla has no value. | ET (Decreto-Ley 624 de 1989) arts. 55, 126-1, 206 (esp. nums. 4 y 5), 378, 379, 380, 383, 385, 386, 387 y su par. 2, 387-1, 388, 408; DUR 1625 de 2016 arts. 1.2.1.12.9, 1.2.1.22.41, 1.6.1.13.2.7, 1.6.1.13.2.40; form prescribed by Resolucion DIAN 000022 de 20 de febrero de 2024; CST arts. 127, 130; Ley 50 de 1990 art. 99; Ley 52 de 1975 art. 1; Ministerio del Trabajo Concepto 23959 de 13 de febrero. | 93% (PRIMARY SOURCE). Every casilla number, label and arithmetic rule is read off the official form design and its four-page instructivo at 00-official-sources/pdfs/Formulario_220_CertificadoIngresosRetenciones.pdf. The 7% reservation is: (a) a defect in the official instructivo - the casilla-42 instruction cross-refers severance deposited to the fund to 'casilla 48' when the form's own label puts it at casilla 49 - escalated in column T; (b) the document-type list printed on the form has only six values and contains NO code for a PEP or a PPT holder, so a regularised Venezuelan migrant cannot be certified with an exactly correct code; (c) the boundary between casilla 36 (salarios) and casillas 39 to 41 (honorarios, servicios, comisiones) for a person who is both an employee and a contractor of the same payer is a client-specific classification. It would rise to 98% with a DIAN clarification on (a) and (b). |


```
SUBMISSION CHANNEL VERIFICATION (v2.7 STEP 3.5)
- Channel: paper or electronic delivery TO THE EMPLOYEE | Portal: none - this artefact is NOT submitted to any authority | Source: ET arts. 378-380 and the form's own instructivo, which addresses only the issuing of the certificate | CONFIRMED.
- This is the one in-scope Colombian artefact with NO government channel at all. It is issued to the employee and retained by the employer. DIAN sees the same data annually through Formato 2276 (CO-DIAN-005), which IS filed - but the two are separate obligations with different field sets and different deadlines.
- The certificate SUBSTITUTES for the employee's income-tax return where the six ET art. 380 conditions hold (DUR 1625 de 2016 art. 1.6.1.13.2.7), which is why it carries employee-declared sections that payroll cannot populate.

INTEGRATION TYPE AND DIRECTION
- integration_type = OUTBOUND (scope_manifest CO-OUT-F220), to the EMPLOYEE rather than to an authority. There is no inbound leg.
- product_mapping_required = true. The certificate needs per-employee ANNUAL totals by concept, three separate severance buckets, the meal-payment split at 41 UVT, the rolling six-month average labour income, and the AVC deduction which has no dedicated nomina electronica element. None of these can be reconstructed at year end from a single annual gross figure.
- NOT DERIVABLE FROM THE MONTHLY FILINGS (scope_manifest failure check F21). Colombia imposes NO employer year-end tax reconciliation. This certificate is a SEPARATE annual obligation built from the annual accumulation, and it additionally carries data the employer never sees monthly - the employee's own asset, debt and other-income declarations under ET art. 380.

RESIDENT / NON-RESIDENT TREATMENT (v2.8 control B1)
- RESIDENT: casilla 60 carries the ET art. 383 progressive-table withholding for the year, and casillas 53 to 58 carry the contributions and voluntary savings that depurated the base. The employee may then use the certificate in place of a return under ET art. 380.
- NON-RESIDENT: the same certificate is issued, but casilla 60 carries the FLAT 20% of ET art. 408 applied to the GROSS - so casilla 52 (total gross income) and the withholding in casilla 60 stand in a simple 20% relationship, with NO depuration visible. The contributions in casillas 53 and 54 are still reported because they were still deducted, but they did NOT reduce the withholding base.
- The art. 206 num. 4 severance exemption in casilla 59 is an exemption of the RESIDENT cedula-general regime. It does NOT attach to a non-resident's flat withholding, so for a non-resident the six-month average is reported for completeness but has no effect on casilla 60.
- An employee who ACQUIRES RESIDENCE mid-year receives ONE certificate covering the whole period, with casilla 60 carrying the sum of the 20% months and the art. 383 months. The certificate does not disclose the split; the employee reconciles on their own return. The engine should retain the split internally for audit.
- The employee's own annual return - Formulario 110 - is expressly DESCOPED in scope_manifest: the employer's duty ends with this certificate.

RELATED FILINGS / CROSS-REFERENCES
- Casilla 60 must reconcile to twelve months of nomina electronica RetencionFuente (NIE177) for this employee - CO-DIAN-001.
- Casillas 53 and 54 must reconcile to twelve months of nomina electronica NIE163 and NIE166 + NIE168 + NIE170, and to the employee portions of PILA campos 55, 47, 51 and 52 - CO-PILA-003.
- The SUM of casilla 60 across all employees must reconcile to twelve months of Formulario 350 casilla 93 - CO-DIAN-002 - but the certificate is NOT derived from those returns.
- Casillas 36 to 60 map almost one for one to Formato 2276 numerals 5 to 33 - CO-DIAN-005 - but the ORDER OF THE THREE SEVERANCE LINES DIFFERS: Form 220 runs 47 paid / 48 traditional / 49 fund, while Formato 2276 runs 18 paid / 19 fund / 20 traditional. A positional copy between the two SWAPS the fund and traditional amounts.
- Casilla 49 must reconcile to the amount actually deposited under CO-CESANTIAS-006 by 14 February.
- Casillas 79 to 82 (dependants) map to Formato 2276 numeral 34.
```


## CO-DIAN-004-Formulario220 - Sheet 3: Payroll Scope Summary


```
PAYROLL SCOPE SUMMARY - ANNUAL CERTIFICATE OF INCOME AND WITHHOLDING ON WORK AND PENSION INCOME - FORM 220
```


| SCENARIO | REPORT? | NOTES / REASONING |
|---|---|---|
| Indefinite-term employee (contrato a termino indefinido) | YES | The core private-sector population. CST art. 47; Ley 2466 de 2025 art. 5. |
| Fixed-term employee (contrato a termino fijo, max 4 years) | YES | Identical reporting treatment to an indefinite-term contract. CST art. 46; Ley 2466 de 2025 art. 6. |
| Specific-work or task employee (contrato por obra o labor) | YES | An employment contract; CST art. 46. Nomina electronica TipoContrato = 3 (Obra o Labor). |
| Occasional / accidental / transitory worker (< 1 month, non-core activity) | YES | Still an employee under CST art. 6. Reported on every filing; the PILA planilla type and the day counts differ, not the obligation. |
| Part-time employee | YES | Colombia has no separate part-time regime. Reported normally; the PILA contribution base is floored at 1 SMMLV (COP 1,750,905) except for the worker type 51 partial-time regime. |
| All-inclusive salary employee (salario integral, >= 13 SMMLV) | YES | CST art. 132 num. 2. Reported with the integral-salary flag set; the contribution base is 70% of the integral salary, and the nomina electronica carries SalarioIntegral = true. |
| Apprentice (contrato de aprendizaje) | YES | Ley 2466 de 2025 art. 21 converted the apprenticeship into a special fixed-term LABOUR contract, regulated by Decreto 0223 of 5 March 2026. Nomina electronica TipoContrato = 4; PILA worker type 12 (lectiva) or 19 (productiva). |
| Intern / practical placement (practicas o pasantias) | YES | Nomina electronica TipoContrato = 5. Reported where remunerated. |
| Teleworkers and remote workers | YES | Ley 1221 de 2008 as amended by Ley 2466 de 2025 arts. 52-54. No reporting difference; the nomina electronica carries a dedicated Teletrabajo devengado element (NIE158). |
| Resident foreign national under a Colombian contract | YES | Full reporting. Withheld under the ET art. 383 progressive table once tax residence is acquired. |
| NON-RESIDENT foreign national under a Colombian contract | YES | IN SCOPE and frequently mis-configured. Withheld at the flat 20% of ET art. 408 on the GROSS, with no art. 388 depuration, until the 183-day residence test is met (DIAN Concepto 011108 of 29 June 2026). See the resident / non-resident block in the Report Matrix. |
| Employed old-age pensioner in active service | YES | PILA worker sub-type 01; no pension contribution (PILA pension rate field = 0), health and the levies still apply. |
| Executive director / statutory legal representative holding an employment contract | YES | Reported as an ordinary employee where an employment contract exists. |
| Independent contractor (contrato de prestacion de servicios) | NO | Not an employee. The contractor self-files PILA on 40% of fees (planilla types I / Y, descoped) and is reported by the payer in the third-party information return, not in this employer filing. Withholding on their fees does pass through Formulario 350, but under a different casilla. |
| Public-sector employee (contributor type 47, SGP-funded entity) | NO | Public-sector only; PILA planilla types T and F are descoped in scope_manifest. |
| Non-executive director paid honorarios only (junta directiva) | NO | Not employment income. Reported by the payer as honorarios, not as rentas de trabajo of an employee. |
| Pensioner whose pension is paid by a fund, not by the employer | NO | The paying fund is the withholding agent and the reporting party, not the employer. |
| Annual salary paid | YES | Casilla 36, on the CST art. 127 definition quoted in full in the instructivo. |
| Voucher, cheque, card and vale payments | YES | Casilla 37, including meal payments for an employee earning MORE than 310 UVT per month. |
| Meal payments up to 41 UVT for an employee earning up to 310 UVT | NO | Not income of the employee at all under ET art. 387-1 - income of the food supplier. Only the EXCESS over 41 UVT is reported, in casilla 38. |
| Statutory benefits other than severance | YES | Casilla 42 - prima de servicios, vacaciones, dotaciones. |
| Severance savings, split three ways | YES | Casillas 47 (paid to the employee), 48 (traditional pre-1991 regime) and 49 (deposited to the fund). Three separate buckets that must be maintained year-round. |
| Per diems and representation expenses | YES | Casillas 43 and 44. |
| Employee mandatory contributions | YES | Casillas 53 and 54. Note that pension and the solidarity fund are COMBINED in casilla 54, unlike the nomina electronica which separates them. |
| Voluntary savings - RAIS, voluntary pension, AFC, AVC | YES | Casillas 55 to 58, each separately. The nomina electronica has one element for the first two and none for AVC, so the engine must tag them itself. |
| Rolling six-month average labour income | YES | Casilla 59. Drives the ET art. 206 num. 4 severance exemption and its taper; must be maintained year-round. |
| Employer social-security contributions | NO | The certificate reports the EMPLOYEE's income and the EMPLOYEE's contributions. The employer's cost is reported only on PILA. |
| The employee's other income, assets and debts | NO | Casillas 61 to 78 are supplied BY THE EMPLOYEE, not by payroll. They exist so the certificate can substitute for the income-tax return under ET art. 380. |
| Reported on the accrual (causacion) date | NO | Every Colombian payroll filing is keyed to the PAYMENT or credit-in-account date, not to the accrual date. A December salary paid in January falls in the January filing. |
| Reported on the payment or credit-in-account date | YES | The universal Colombian rule - pago o abono en cuenta. Applies to the nomina electronica (Res. DIAN 000013 de 2021 art. 3), to Formulario 350, to Formulario 220 and to Formato 2276. |
| A semi-monthly payroll produces two filings a month | NO | It does not. The nomina electronica is expressly ONE monthly document per employee 'irrespective of whether the obliged party makes payments in different or shorter periods' (art. 3). PILA and Formulario 350 are likewise monthly. |
| Nil period still filed | VARIES | Formulario 350: NOT required where no operation subject to withholding occurred in the period (ET art. 606 par. 2). Nomina electronica: no document where there was no payment. PILA: a planilla is still required while the employment relationship subsists. |
| Corrections handled by re-filing the whole period | VARIES | Nomina electronica: NO - a dedicated adjustment note (NominaIndividualDeAjuste, Reemplazar or Eliminar) referencing the original CUNE. Formulario 350: yes, a corrective return. PILA: a planilla type N (correcciones) or M (mora). |
| Employee who left during the year | YES | Casillas 30 and 31 carry the actual employment window rather than 1 January to 31 December. The certificate is still issued by the last business day of March. |
| Employee whose withholding was nil all year | YES | A certificate is still issued. Casilla 60 is ZERO - the instructivo directs that a casilla with no value be written as zero, not left blank. |
| Employee who acquired tax residence mid-year | YES | ONE certificate for the whole period. Casilla 60 sums the 20% months and the art. 383 months; the split is not disclosed on the form but must be retained internally. |
| Employee with a dependant claimed for the art. 387 deduction | YES | Casillas 79 to 82 identify the dependant. The same dependant is reported in Formato 2276 numeral 34, where DIAN cross-checks a dependant claimed by more than one taxpayer. |
| Employee under the pre-1991 traditional severance regime | YES | Casilla 48 rather than casilla 49. A shrinking population - employees hired before 1 January 1991 who did not elect the Ley 50 de 1990 regime. |
| Employee holding a PEP or PPT | PARTIAL | Reported, but the form's six-value document-type list has no code for either. Escalated in column T of casilla 24. |
| Certificate substituting for the employee's income-tax return | YES | Where all six ET art. 380 conditions hold and the employee signs, the form states that it substitutes 'para todos los efectos legales' for the return. |

## CO-DIAN-004-Formulario220 - Sheet 5: File Structure


```
FILE STRUCTURE AND TECHNICAL SPECIFICATIONS
```


| Item | Value |
|---|---|
| Channel | Issued TO THE EMPLOYEE, on paper or electronically as agreed, and RETAINED BY THE EMPLOYER. There is NO government channel: Formulario 220 is a certificate, not a return, and is not submitted to DIAN. |
| Source | ET arts. 378, 379 and 380; DUR 1625 de 2016 art. 1.6.1.13.2.40 (deadline) and art. 1.6.1.13.2.7 (substitution for the income-tax return); the form's own instructivo, which addresses only issuing. CONFIRMED. |
| Why there is no file format | DIAN prescribes the FORM but not a transmission format, because nothing is transmitted. The structure below is therefore a LOGICAL MODEL - the casilla list and its hierarchy - not a wire format. |
| Where DIAN does see this data | Annually, through Formato 2276 (CO-DIAN-005), which IS filed. The two obligations overlap in content but differ in field set, ordering and deadline, and neither is derived from the other. |
| Retention | The employer retains the issued certificate as evidence of the withholding. Where the employee signs the six ET art. 380 declarations, the certificate substitutes for their income-tax return, so its evidential value runs beyond the employer. |
| Header | Formulario 220 \| Ano gravable \| 4 Numero de formulario (assigned BY THE EMPLOYER, not by DIAN) |
| Section 1 - Agente retenedor | 5 NIT \| 6 DV \| 7 Primer apellido \| 8 Segundo apellido \| 9 Primer nombre \| 10 Otros nombres \| 11 Razon social |
| Section 2 - Trabajador o pensionado | 24 Tipo de documento \| 25 Numero de identificacion \| 26 Primer apellido \| 27 Segundo apellido \| 28 Primer nombre \| 29 Otros nombres |
| Section 3 - Periodo y lugar | 30 Periodo DE \| 31 Periodo A \| 32 Fecha de expedicion \| 33 Lugar donde se practico la retencion \| 34 Cod. Dpto. \| 35 Cod. Ciudad/Municipio |
| Section 4 - Concepto de los ingresos | 36 Pagos por salarios \| 37 Pagos con bonos, cheques, tarjetas, vales \| 38 Exceso alimentacion > 41 UVT \| 39 Honorarios \| 40 Servicios \| 41 Comisiones \| 42 Prestaciones sociales \| 43 Viaticos \| 44 Gastos de representacion \| 45 Compensaciones trabajo asociado cooperativo \| 46 Otros pagos \| 47 Cesantia e intereses PAGADOS AL EMPLEADO \| 48 Cesantia REGIMEN TRADICIONAL \| 49 Cesantia CONSIGNADA AL FONDO \| 50 Pensiones \| 51 Apoyos educativos con recursos publicos \| 52 TOTAL INGRESOS BRUTOS (Sume 36 a 51) |
| Section 5 - Concepto de los aportes | 53 Aportes obligatorios salud \| 54 Aportes obligatorios pensiones Y SOLIDARIDAD (combined) \| 55 Cotizaciones voluntarias RAIS \| 56 Aportes voluntarios a fondos de pensiones \| 57 Aportes AFC \| 58 Aportes AVC \| 59 Ingreso laboral promedio de los ultimos seis meses \| 60 VALOR DE LA RETENCION EN LA FUENTE |
| Section 6 - Datos a cargo del trabajador | 61-66 Otros ingresos, valor recibido \| 68-73 Otros ingresos, valor retenido \| 67 Total recibido (Sume 61 a 66) \| 74 Total retenido (Sume 68 a 73) \| 75 TOTAL RETENCIONES ANO GRAVABLE (Sume 60 + 74) |
| Section 7 - Patrimonio | 76 Identificacion de los bienes poseidos (3 rows) \| 77 Valor patrimonial \| 78 Deudas vigentes a 31 de diciembre |
| Section 8 - Dependiente economico | 79 Tipo documento \| 80 No. Documento \| 81 Apellidos y nombres \| 82 Parentesco |
| Section 9 - Certificacion ET art. 380 | Six unnumbered declarations plus the employee's signature. DISPLAY-ONLY - not data fields. |
| Section 10 - Firma | Nombre del pagador o agente retenedor plus the issuing signature. |
| Casilla 52 | Sume 36 a 51 - sixteen income casillas. |
| Casilla 67 | Sume 61 a 66 - employee-declared other income received. |
| Casilla 74 | Sume 68 a 73 - employee-declared other income withheld. |
| Casilla 75 | Sume 60 + 74 - the employer's withholding plus the employee-declared withholding. |
| Rounding | The instructivo is explicit: values 'deben ser expresados en pesos colombianos y aproximados al multiplo de mil (1.000) mas cercano'. The SAME convention as Formulario 350, and NOT the nomina electronica convention of two decimals round-half-to-even. |
| Nil values | 'En el evento en que no haya valor a registrar en alguna casilla escriba cero (0)'. Write ZERO, do not leave blank. This differs from the nomina electronica, where an inapplicable element is OMITTED rather than zero-filled. |
| RUT consistency | The instructivo repeats for casillas 7 to 11 that where the value does not match the RUT, THE RUT MUST BE UPDATED BEFORE THE CERTIFICATE IS ISSUED. The certificate does not override the register. |
| Form 220 | 47 = paid to the employee \| 48 = traditional pre-1991 CST regime \| 49 = deposited to the fund |
| Formato 2276 | 18 = paid to the employee \| 19 = deposited to the fund \| 20 = traditional pre-1991 CST regime |
| The trap | THE SECOND AND THIRD LINES ARE TRANSPOSED BETWEEN THE TWO ARTEFACTS. An implementation that maps positionally - 47 to 18, 48 to 19, 49 to 20 - will swap the fund deposit and the traditional-regime amount in the information return. Map by MEANING, never by position. |
| Recorded defect | The instructivo for casilla 42 cross-refers severance deposited to the fund to 'la casilla 48'. The form's own printed label puts the fund deposit at casilla 49 and the traditional regime at casilla 48. The form layout is authoritative; the cross-reference is wrong. Escalated in column T of casilla 42. |

## CO-DIAN-004-Formulario220 - Sheet 7: Population Rules


```
POPULATION RULES - HOW TO FILL EACH FIELD FROM PAYROLL
```


| Field Name | Source System | Population Logic | Edge Cases |
|---|---|---|---|
| Casilla 4 - Numero de formulario | Engine sequence | Assign the certificate number from the ENGINE's own series - the instructivo says it is 'asignado por la persona natural o juridica que expide la certificacion'. Keep the series stable and unique per employee per tax year, because the employee may quote it when filing or when asking for a duplicate. | SHARP CONTRAST WITH FORMULARIO 350, where casilla 4 is DIAN-assigned. Do not reuse the Form 350 numbering logic here. |
| Casillas 30 and 31 - Periodo de la certificacion | Payroll engine | Set casilla 30 to 1 January or the hire date, whichever is later, and casilla 31 to 31 December or the termination date, whichever is earlier. Write year, month, day in that order, as the instructivo directs. | A leaver certificate covers only the employment window. It is still due by the last business day of March following the tax year, not on termination. |
| Casilla 36 - Pagos por salarios | Payroll engine | Accumulate salary payments ACTUALLY MADE in the period, on the CST art. 127 definition the instructivo quotes in full - ordinary fixed or variable remuneration plus everything received in money or in kind as direct consideration for the service: primas, sobresueldos, habitual bonuses, overtime, work on mandatory rest days, sales percentages and commissions. Then apply the exclusion rule: remove anything belonging in casillas 38 to 46. | The exclusion rule is the trap. Commissions under an employment contract are salary and stay in casilla 36; commissions outside an employment relationship go to casilla 41. Per diems go to casilla 43 even where the permanent portion is salary for contribution purposes. The certificate's classification and the contribution base's classification are NOT the same. |
| Casillas 47, 48 and 49 - the three severance lines | Payroll engine | Maintain THREE separate severance buckets from the outset, not one: (47) amounts and interest ACTUALLY PAID to the employee, (48) amounts RECOGNISED under the pre-1991 traditional CST regime, and (49) amounts DEPOSITED to the fund by 14 February. Casillas 48 and 49 are mutually exclusive for the same employee, because an employee is in one regime or the other. | They CANNOT be split retrospectively out of a single annual cesantias figure. And when mapping to Formato 2276, map by MEANING: 47 to numeral 18, 48 to numeral 20, 49 to numeral 19. The second and third lines are transposed between the two artefacts. |
| Casilla 59 - Ingreso laboral promedio de los ultimos seis meses | Payroll engine | Maintain a rolling six-month average of labour income YEAR-ROUND and store it monthly. On a termination, use the average of the last six months OF EMPLOYMENT. The average drives the ET art. 206 num. 4 severance exemption: full exemption where it does not exceed 350 UVT (COP 18,330,900 per month in 2026), tapered above. | This is the single field on the certificate that cannot be reconstructed at year end. A moving six-month window is not derivable from twelve annual totals, and getting it wrong changes the exemption on the severance, not merely the disclosure. |
| Casilla 60 - Valor de la retencion | Payroll engine (tax) | Accumulate the retencion en la fuente actually practised on this employee's work and pension income during the certified period. For a RESIDENT this is the ET art. 383 table result under Procedure 1 or 2; for a NON-RESIDENT it is the flat 20% of ET art. 408 on the gross. Where the employee changed residence status mid-year, sum both. | Reconcile to twelve months of nomina electronica NIE177 for this employee BEFORE issuing. A certificate that disagrees with the transmitted documents is evidence against the employer, because the XML is the tax support for the cost. |
| Write zero, never blank | Engine (formatting) | The instructivo directs: 'En el evento en que no haya valor a registrar en alguna casilla escriba cero (0)'. Every money casilla is populated, with zero where there is nothing. | This INVERTS the nomina electronica rule, where an inapplicable element is OMITTED rather than zero-filled. Two artefacts, two opposite conventions, from the same authority. |
| Round to the nearest 1,000 | Engine (formatting) | All money casillas are expressed in Colombian pesos and approximated to the nearest 1,000, exactly as on Formulario 350. Apply the rounding to each casilla value, then check that the printed sums still tie - casilla 52 = sum of 36 to 51, casilla 75 = 60 + 74. | Rounding sixteen casillas independently can make casilla 52 differ from the rounded sum of the unrounded parts by up to 8,000 pesos. Compute casilla 52 from the ROUNDED casillas so the arithmetic printed on the form holds. |
| RUT consistency before issue | Engine (control) | Casillas 5 to 11 must match casillas 5, 6, 31, 32, 33, 34 and 35 of the RUT. The instructivo repeats for each that where they do not match, THE RUT MUST BE UPDATED BEFORE THE CERTIFICATE IS ISSUED. | Do not let the certificate diverge from the register in order to meet the March deadline - the register governs. |
| Casilla 37 versus casilla 38 - the meal-payment fork | Payroll engine | Where the employee's monthly salary EXCEEDS 310 UVT (COP 16,235,940 in 2026), the WHOLE meal payment goes to casilla 37 and casilla 38 stays nil. Where it does not exceed 310 UVT, the payment is not income up to 41 UVT (COP 2,147,334) and only the EXCESS goes to casilla 38, with nothing in casilla 37 for that concept. | Two thresholds, applied in sequence, and the outcome routes the amount to a DIFFERENT casilla. Both tests are MONTHLY, so an employee can fork one way in some months and the other way in others within the same certificate. |
| Casilla 48 and casilla 49 are mutually exclusive | Engine (validation) | An employee is either in the pre-1991 traditional severance regime or in the Ley 50 de 1990 annual-deposit regime, not both. Assert that at most one of the two is non-zero for any employee. | The only exception is a transition year in which an employee formally elected the new regime - rare, and worth an explicit override rather than a silent allowance. |
| Casilla 54 combines two concepts | Engine (accumulation) | Casilla 54 carries mandatory PENSION contributions AND the solidarity-fund surcharge in ONE figure. The nomina electronica reports them as three separate elements (NIE166, NIE168, NIE170) and PILA as three separate campos (47, 51, 52). Keep them separate internally and sum them only at this casilla. | Summing them earlier makes the nomina electronica and PILA outputs impossible to produce correctly. |
| Casillas 55 and 56 split one nomina electronica element | Engine (accumulation) | Casilla 55 is voluntary contributions to the RAIS under ET art. 55; casilla 56 is voluntary contributions to VOLUNTARY pension funds under ET art. 126-1. The nomina electronica has ONE element for both (PensionVoluntaria, NIE198), so the engine must carry the distinction on the wage type. | Different legal bases, different caps in some scenarios. Combining them here is a misstatement even though the XML cannot tell them apart. |
| Casilla 58 has no nomina electronica element | Engine (accumulation) | AVC contributions to the Fondo Nacional del Ahorro have no dedicated nomina electronica element and fall to OtraDeduccion (NIE197). Tag the wage type so the amount can be recovered for this casilla and for Formato 2276 numeral 28. | Without the tag the amount is indistinguishable from any other 'other deduction' at year end. |
| Non-resident certificate | Payroll engine (tax) | Issue the same certificate. Casilla 52 carries the gross and casilla 60 carries 20% of it under ET art. 408 - so the two stand in a simple 20% relationship with no depuration visible. Casillas 53 and 54 still report the contributions actually deducted, even though they did NOT reduce the withholding base for a non-resident. | Casilla 59 is reported for completeness but has no effect: the ET art. 206 num. 4 severance exemption is an exemption of the RESIDENT cedula-general regime and does not attach to a non-resident's flat withholding. |
| Mid-year residence change | Payroll engine (tax) | Issue ONE certificate for the whole period. Casilla 60 sums the months withheld at 20% under art. 408 and the months withheld under the art. 383 table. The form has no field to disclose the split. | Retain the split internally for audit and for any DIAN enquiry. The employee reconciles on their own return; the employer performs no true-up. |
| Dependants (casillas 79 to 82) | Employee master data | Populate all four casillas together where the employee claimed the ET art. 387 par. 2 dependants deduction during the period. The deduction is 10% of gross employment income capped at 32 UVT per month (COP 1,675,968 in 2026). | The same dependant is reported in Formato 2276 numeral 34, which is where DIAN cross-checks a dependant claimed by more than one taxpayer. Keep the two consistent or the employee receives an enquiry. |
| Employee-declared sections (casillas 61 to 78) | The employee | Payroll does NOT populate casillas 61 to 78. They are supplied by the employee so that the certificate can substitute for the income-tax return under ET art. 380. Provide the employee with a means to supply them and leave them at zero where the employee does not. | Issuing the certificate with these sections at zero does not invalidate it - it simply means the employee cannot rely on it as a substitute return and must file. |
| Casilla 52 arithmetic | Engine (validation) | Casilla 52 must equal the sum of the ROUNDED casillas 36 to 51. Assert it before issuing. | Sixteen casillas. A single mis-classified concept both understates one casilla and overstates another while leaving casilla 52 correct - so also reconcile casilla 52 to the annual gross from the nomina electronica. |
| Casilla 75 arithmetic | Engine (validation) | Casilla 75 must equal casilla 60 plus casilla 74. Where the employee supplies nothing, casilla 74 is zero and casilla 75 equals casilla 60. | - |
| Casillas 79 to 82 populate together | Engine (validation) | All four dependant casillas are populated together or none is. A document number without a relationship, or a relationship without a number, is incomplete. | - |
| Cross-report reconciliation | Engine (reconciliation) | Before issuing, assert per employee: casilla 60 equals twelve months of nomina electronica NIE177; casilla 53 equals twelve months of NIE163 and the employee portion of PILA campo 55; casilla 54 equals twelve months of NIE166 + NIE168 + NIE170 and the employee portions of PILA campos 47, 51 and 52; casilla 49 equals the amount actually deposited under CO-CESANTIAS-006. Across the population, assert that the sum of casilla 60 equals twelve months of Formulario 350 casilla 93. | Reconcile on the UNROUNDED values with a tolerance, because the four artefacts round three different ways. A material difference means a payment was made without a nomina electronica document, or a document was rejected and never re-transmitted. |

| (scenario) | Annual build-up | Salary 12 x 12,000,000 = 144,000,000. Prima de servicios 12,000,000 (two instalments). Cesantias for 2026 = 12,000,000, deposited to the fund by 14 February 2027. Interest on the 2025 cesantias balance = 1,440,000, paid in January 2026. | The January interest payment falls in the 2026 certificate because Colombia certifies on the PAYMENT date, not the accrual date. |
| 2026-000417 | Casilla 4 - Numero de formulario | Assigned by the EMPLOYER. | Contrast Formulario 350, where casilla 4 is DIAN-assigned. Keep the series stable - the employee may quote it. |
| 900123456 / 7 | Casillas 5 / 6 | Employer NIT and verification digit. | Without dots, hyphens or letters, as the instructivo insists. |
| MERCANS COLOMBIA S.A.S. | Casilla 11 - Razon social | Legal person. | Casillas 7 to 10 stay blank; the instructivo says a natural-person issuer must not fill casilla 11. |
| 13 / 1020304050 | Casillas 24 / 25 | Cedula de ciudadania. | Had the employee held a PEP or a PPT, neither has a code in the six-value list printed on this form - escalated in column T. |
| 2026 01 01 / 2026 12 31 | Casillas 30 / 31 | Full calendar year certified. | A leaver would carry the termination date in casilla 31. |
| 2027 03 31 | Casilla 32 - Fecha de expedicion | Issued on the last business day of March 2027. | The statutory deadline under DUR 1625 de 2016 art. 1.6.1.13.2.40. |
| 144.000.000 | Casilla 36 - Pagos por salarios | Twelve months at COP 12,000,000, on the CST art. 127 definition. | Excludes anything belonging in casillas 38 to 46 - here, the prima and the cesantias. |
| 0 | Casilla 37 / Casilla 38 | No voucher or meal payments. | Written as ZERO, not left blank: 'escriba cero (0)'. This inverts the nomina electronica convention, where an inapplicable element is omitted. |
| 12.000.000 | Casilla 42 - Pagos por prestaciones sociales | The statutory service bonus (prima de servicios), paid in two instalments by 30 June and 20 December. | Excludes severance and its interest, which go to casillas 47 to 49. Vacation pay and dotaciones would also sit here. |
| 1.440.000 | Casilla 47 - Cesantia e intereses pagados al empleado | The 12% interest on the 31 December 2025 severance balance, paid to the employee during January 2026. | Excluded from the monthly withholding base under ET art. 206 num. 4, but still certified. Maps to Formato 2276 numeral 18. |
| 0 | Casilla 48 - Regimen tradicional | The employee is under the Ley 50 de 1990 regime. | Mutually exclusive with casilla 49. Maps to Formato 2276 numeral 20 - NOT numeral 19. |
| 12.000.000 | Casilla 49 - Cesantia consignada al fondo | One month's salary for the year, deposited by 14 February 2027. | Maps to Formato 2276 numeral 19 - NOT numeral 20. The second and third severance lines are TRANSPOSED between the two artefacts; map by meaning, never by position. Must reconcile to CO-CESANTIAS-006. |
| 157.440.000 | Casilla 52 - Total ingresos brutos | 144,000,000 + 12,000,000 + 1,440,000 + 12,000,000, per the form's own 'Sume 36 a 51'. | Compute from the ROUNDED casillas so the printed arithmetic holds. |
| 5.760.000 | Casilla 53 - Aportes obligatorios por salud | Twelve months at COP 480,000 - the employee's 4%. | Reconciles to twelve months of nomina electronica NIE163 and to the employee portion of PILA campo 55. |
| 7.200.000 | Casilla 54 - Aportes obligatorios a pensiones y solidaridad pensional | Twelve months at COP 480,000 pension plus COP 120,000 solidarity = COP 600,000 a month. | ONE casilla for TWO concepts. The nomina electronica keeps them as NIE166 and NIE168, and PILA as campos 47, 51 and 52. Keep them separate internally and sum only here. |
| 0 | Casillas 55 to 58 | No voluntary savings. | Casillas 55 and 56 split what the nomina electronica carries in ONE element (NIE198); casilla 58 (AVC) has no nomina electronica element at all and must be tagged on the wage type. |
| 13.120.000 | Casilla 59 - Ingreso laboral promedio de los ultimos seis meses | The rolling six-month average of labour income, driven by the actual monthly payments including the December prima instalment. | Below 350 UVT (COP 18,330,900), so the severance in casillas 47 and 49 is EXEMPT IN FULL under ET art. 206 num. 4. This is the field that cannot be reconstructed at year end - maintain it monthly. |
| 7.404.000 | Casilla 60 - Valor de la retencion en la fuente | The year's withholding: twelve ordinary months at COP 617,000 = 7,404,000, the prima having been withheld separately under ET art. 385 with its own table read and, on these figures, producing no additional withholding. | Must reconcile to twelve months of nomina electronica NIE177 for this employee. The sum across all employees must reconcile to twelve months of Formulario 350 casilla 93. |
| 0 | Casillas 61 to 78 | Not supplied by the employee. | Payroll does NOT populate these. They exist so the certificate can substitute for the income-tax return under ET art. 380; left at zero, the employee simply cannot rely on the substitution. |
| 7.404.000 | Casilla 75 - Total retenciones ano gravable | Casilla 60 + casilla 74 (nil). | - |
| (unsigned) | Certificacion ET art. 380 | Six declarations on gross wealth below 4,500 UVT, gross income and consumption below 1,400 UVT, and non-VAT-responsible status. | DISPLAY-ONLY on the form. Where all six hold and the employee signs, the certificate substitutes for the income-tax return 'para todos los efectos legales'. |
| 157.440.000 / 31.488.000 | Casilla 52 / Casilla 60 for a NON-RESIDENT | The same gross income, but withheld at the flat 20% of ET art. 408 = COP 31,488,000. | Four and a quarter times the resident's COP 7,404,000. Casillas 53 and 54 still report the contributions actually deducted, but they did NOT reduce the base - art. 408 taxes the gross. |
| 13.120.000 | Casilla 59 for a NON-RESIDENT | Reported, but of no effect. | The ET art. 206 num. 4 severance exemption belongs to the RESIDENT cedula-general regime and does not attach to a non-resident's flat withholding. |
| 7.404.000 |  | Annual withholding for this employee. | Reconcile on unrounded values with a tolerance - the two artefacts round differently. |
| 12.000.000 |  | Casilla 49 equals the fund deposit. | Late deposit costs ONE DAY'S SALARY PER DAY OF DELAY, per employee, uncapped - so this reconciliation has a direct financial consequence. |
| (map by meaning) | -> Formato 2276 numerals 18, 19, 20 | Casilla 47 to numeral 18, casilla 49 to numeral 19, casilla 48 to numeral 20. | NOT positional. The second and third severance lines are transposed between the two artefacts. |


## CO-DIAN-004-Formulario220 - Sheet 9: Sample File


```
SAMPLE FILE - VERIFIED CHANNEL STATEMENT AND COMPLETED-CERTIFICATE RENDERING

WHY THERE IS NO SAMPLE FILE

Formulario 220 is NOT SUBMITTED TO ANY AUTHORITY. It is a certificate issued to
the employee and retained by the employer (ET arts. 378-380; DUR 1625 de 2016
art. 1.6.1.13.2.40). DIAN prescribes the FORM but no transmission format,
because nothing is transmitted.

There is therefore no wire format, no XSD and no import layout to sample.
Fabricating one would be inventing a format that does not exist. What follows
is a COMPLETED-CERTIFICATE RENDERING: the casilla-value set the engine must
produce, which is the actual deliverable.

Amounts are in Colombian pesos, approximated to the nearest 1,000, with ZERO
written where a casilla has no value - both rules stated expressly in the
instructivo.

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
NON-RESIDENT VARIANT (v2.8 control B1)
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
The same certificate for a NON-RESIDENT employee changes exactly one figure:

 52  TOTAL INGRESOS BRUTOS ............................... 157.440.000  (unchanged)
 53  Aportes obligatorios por salud ......................   5.760.000  (unchanged)
 54  Aportes obligatorios a pensiones y solidaridad ......   7.200.000  (unchanged)
 59  Ingreso laboral promedio ultimos seis meses .........  13.120.000  (no effect)
 60  VALOR DE LA RETENCION EN LA FUENTE ..................  31.488.000  <== 20% of GROSS

ET art. 408 taxes the GROSS at a flat 20%: 20% x 157,440,000 = 31,488,000. The
contributions in casillas 53 and 54 are still certified because they were still
deducted - but they did NOT reduce the withholding base. Casilla 59 is reported
for completeness and has no effect, because the art. 206 num. 4 severance
exemption belongs to the resident cedula-general regime.

An employee who ACQUIRES RESIDENCE mid-year receives ONE certificate; casilla 60
sums the 20% months and the art. 383 months, and the form has no field to
disclose the split. Retain the split internally for audit.

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
MAPPING TO FORMATO 2276 - MAP BY MEANING, NOT BY POSITION
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Form 220 casilla 36  ->  2276 numeral  5   Pagos por salarios
  Form 220 casilla 37  ->  2276 numeral  7   Bonos, cheques, tarjetas, vales
  Form 220 casilla 38  ->  2276 numeral  8   Exceso alimentacion > 41 UVT
  Form 220 casilla 39  ->  2276 numeral  9   Honorarios
  Form 220 casilla 40  ->  2276 numeral 10   Servicios
  Form 220 casilla 41  ->  2276 numeral 11   Comisiones
  Form 220 casilla 42  ->  2276 numeral 12   Prestaciones sociales
  Form 220 casilla 43  ->  2276 numeral 13   Viaticos
  Form 220 casilla 44  ->  2276 numeral 14   Gastos de representacion
  Form 220 casilla 45  ->  2276 numeral 15   Compensaciones trabajo asociado
  Form 220 casilla 46  ->  2276 numeral 17   Otros pagos
  Form 220 casilla 47  ->  2276 numeral 18   Cesantias PAGADAS al empleado
  Form 220 casilla 49  ->  2276 numeral 19   Cesantias CONSIGNADAS al fondo   <== NOT 20
  Form 220 casilla 48  ->  2276 numeral 20   Cesantias REGIMEN TRADICIONAL    <== NOT 19
  Form 220 casilla 50  ->  2276 numeral 21   Pensiones
  Form 220 casilla 51  ->  2276 numeral 16   Apoyos economicos educativos
  Form 220 casilla 52  ->  2276 numeral 22   Total ingresos brutos
  Form 220 casilla 53  ->  2276 numeral 23   Aportes obligatorios salud
  Form 220 casilla 54  ->  2276 numeral 24   Aportes obligatorios pension y FSP
  Form 220 casilla 55  ->  2276 numeral 25   Aportes voluntarios RAIS
  Form 220 casilla 56  ->  2276 numeral 26   Aportes voluntarios fondos pensiones
  Form 220 casilla 57  ->  2276 numeral 27   Aportes AFC
  Form 220 casilla 58  ->  2276 numeral 28   Aportes AVC
  Form 220 casilla 59  ->  2276 numeral 33   Ingreso laboral promedio 6 meses
  Form 220 casilla 60  ->  2276 numeral 29   Retenciones practicadas
  Form 220 casillas 79-82 -> 2276 numeral 34 Dependiente economico

THE TWO HIGHLIGHTED LINES ARE TRANSPOSED. A positional mapping - 47 to 18,
48 to 19, 49 to 20 - swaps the fund deposit and the traditional-regime amount
in the information return. There is also a Form 220 concept with NO 2276
counterpart in this list (the meal payments UP TO 41 UVT, which 2276 reports
separately at numeral 32 as informative and Form 220 does not report at all)
and a 2276 concept with no Form 220 counterpart (numeral 6, emolumentos
eclesiasticos, which Form 220 folds into casilla 39).

(4) OPTION-VALUE COMPLETENESS: every column O reference now resolves to a Sheet 6 section; two missing PILA code tables and the Formato 2276 document-type table were built.
