Amounts in whole pesos, zero-filled where nil (Res. 227 art. 1.3.7.1.1 par. 2). | Resolucion DIAN 000227 de 23 de septiembre de 2025 (Resolucion Unica en Materia Tributaria, Aduanera y Cambiaria), arts. 1.3.7.1.1 (content), 1.3.9.1.3 (deadlines) y 1.3.10.1 (formatos y especificaciones tecnicas), which compile art. 51 de la Resolucion 162 de 2023 with par. 7 added by el art. 8 de la Resolucion 188 de 2024; ET art. 631-3 (the enabling power), arts. 55, 126-1, 206 num. 4, 383 y su par. 2, 387 par. 2, 387-1, 408; Ley 2277 de 2022 art. 8; Ley 50 de 1990 art. 99; Ley 52 de 1975 art. 1. | 85% (PRIMARY for content, DERIVED for layout). The THIRTY-SIX NUMERALS, the eight-value Entidad Informante code table, the seven paragraphs of interpretive rules and the complete filing calendar are transcribed VERBATIM from Res. DIAN 000227 de 2025 arts. 1.3.7.1.1 and 1.3.9.1.3, held locally. What is NOT in the source registry is the separate especificaciones tecnicas annex that fixes the XML ELEMENT NAMES, field ordering and column lengths - the resolution only points to it. Every XML path in this Spec is therefore marked DERIVED in column T and must be confirmed before a productive layout is configured. Content coverage is effectively complete; layout coverage is not. It would rise to 97% with the published especificaciones tecnicas for Formato 2276 v4. |


```
SUBMISSION CHANNEL VERIFICATION (v2.7 STEP 3.5)
- Channel: file_upload (XML) into the DIAN informacion exogena service | Portal: www.dian.gov.co, Servicios Informaticos Electronicos, with the Instrumento de Firma Electronica (IFE) | Source: Res. DIAN 000227 de 2025 arts. 1.3.7.1.1 and 1.3.10.1 | CONFIRMED as to channel; FORMAT TO CONFIRM as to the XML schema.
- This is NOT machine-to-machine. Unlike the nomina electronica there is no SOAP service, no document signature and no synchronous ApplicationResponse - the employer uploads a file through an authenticated portal session and reads the outcome on screen.
- The word 'XML' is used here because Res. 227 art. 1.3.10.1 lists Formato 2276 among the formats with published especificaciones tecnicas, and the exogena service accepts XML. It is NOT used because an XSD has been cited - none is in the local registry. Column T marks every element path DERIVED accordingly.
- The tax portal is not the social-security channel and not the operador de informacion. Formato 2276 goes to DIAN; PILA never does.

INTEGRATION TYPE AND DIRECTION
- integration_type = OUTBOUND (scope_manifest CO-OUT-2276). No inbound file: the portal returns an on-screen validation outcome, which the engine should capture for the audit trail.
- product_mapping_required = true. The format needs three separate cesantias lines, the meal-payment split at the 41-UVT threshold across TWO numerals, the rolling six-month average labour income, the RAIS-versus-voluntary-pension split that the nomina electronica cannot make, the AVC amount that has no nomina electronica element at all, and each dependant's identity document. None of these is derivable at year end from annual totals.
- NOT DERIVABLE FROM THE MONTHLY FILINGS. It has its own field list, its own validation and its own deadline. It also carries concepts Formulario 220 does not - numeral 6 (ecclesiastical emoluments), numeral 32 (meal payments up to 41 UVT, informative) and numerals 30, 31, 35 and 36.

RESIDENT / NON-RESIDENT TREATMENT (v2.8 control B1)
- RESIDENT: numeral 29 carries the ET art. 383 progressive-table withholding, and numerals 23 to 28 carry the contributions and voluntary savings that depurated the base.
- NON-RESIDENT: numeral 29 carries the FLAT 20% of ET art. 408 on the GROSS. Numerals 23 and 24 still report the contributions actually deducted, but they did NOT reduce the base - art. 408 admits no depuration. Numeral 33 is reported where severance was paid but has no effect, because the art. 206 num. 4 exemption belongs to the resident cedula-general regime.
- The format has NO residence field. Residence is therefore invisible to DIAN in this return except through the arithmetic relationship between numerals 22 and 29 - a non-resident's numeral 29 is close to exactly 20% of numeral 22, which is itself a useful internal control.
- Res. 227 par. 5 draws a SECOND distinction that is easy to confuse with residence: payments to natural persons receiving work income NOT from an employment relationship are reported HERE where they were withheld under ET art. 383, and in the ordinary payments-and-withholdings formats where they were not. That test is about the WITHHOLDING REGIME, not about residence.
- Where a non-resident's work was performed ENTIRELY outside Colombia the income is foreign-source, was not withheld, and is not reported here at all (DIAN Oficio 28380 of 2019).

RELATED FILINGS / CROSS-REFERENCES
- Numerals 5 to 33 map almost one for one to Formulario 220 casillas 36 to 60 (CO-DIAN-004) - but THE SECOND AND THIRD SEVERANCE LINES ARE TRANSPOSED: numeral 19 is Form 220 casilla 49 and numeral 20 is casilla 48. Map by meaning, never by position.
- Numeral 29 summed across the population reconciles to twelve months of Formulario 350 casilla 93 (CO-DIAN-002), and per employee to twelve months of nomina electronica NIE177 (CO-DIAN-001).
- Numerals 23 and 24 reconcile to the employee portions of PILA Registro Tipo 2 campos 55, 47, 51 and 52 (CO-PILA-003).
- Numeral 19 must reconcile to the deposit made under CO-CESANTIAS-006 by 14 February.
- Numeral 34 must agree with Formulario 220 casillas 79 and 80 - DIAN cross-checks a dependant claimed by more than one taxpayer.
- Numeral 16 is mutually exclusive with Formato 5247 concepto 5102 for the same payment (Res. 227 par. 7): employer-paid educational support is reported here, third-party administered support there. Formato 5247 is not a payroll filing.
- Numerals 30, 31, 35 and 36 are NOT payroll fields - VAT and collaboration-contract identifiers, owned by the client's tax function.
```


## CO-DIAN-005-Formato2276 - Sheet 3: Payroll Scope Summary


```
PAYROLL SCOPE SUMMARY - THIRD-PARTY INFORMATION RETURN ON WORK AND PENSION INCOME - FORMAT 2276 VERSION 4
```


| SCENARIO | REPORT? | NOTES / REASONING |
|---|---|---|
| Indefinite-term employee (contrato a termino indefinido) | YES | The core private-sector population. CST art. 47; Ley 2466 de 2025 art. 5. |
| Fixed-term employee (contrato a termino fijo, max 4 years) | YES | Identical reporting treatment to an indefinite-term contract. CST art. 46; Ley 2466 de 2025 art. 6. |
| Specific-work or task employee (contrato por obra o labor) | YES | An employment contract; CST art. 46. Nomina electronica TipoContrato = 3 (Obra o Labor). |
| Occasional / accidental / transitory worker (< 1 month, non-core activity) | YES | Still an employee under CST art. 6. Reported on every filing; the PILA planilla type and the day counts differ, not the obligation. |
| Part-time employee | YES | Colombia has no separate part-time regime. Reported normally; the PILA contribution base is floored at 1 SMMLV (COP 1,750,905) except for the worker type 51 partial-time regime. |
| All-inclusive salary employee (salario integral, >= 13 SMMLV) | YES | CST art. 132 num. 2. Reported with the integral-salary flag set; the contribution base is 70% of the integral salary, and the nomina electronica carries SalarioIntegral = true. |
| Apprentice (contrato de aprendizaje) | YES | Ley 2466 de 2025 art. 21 converted the apprenticeship into a special fixed-term LABOUR contract, regulated by Decreto 0223 of 5 March 2026. Nomina electronica TipoContrato = 4; PILA worker type 12 (lectiva) or 19 (productiva). |
| Intern / practical placement (practicas o pasantias) | YES | Nomina electronica TipoContrato = 5. Reported where remunerated. |
| Teleworkers and remote workers | YES | Ley 1221 de 2008 as amended by Ley 2466 de 2025 arts. 52-54. No reporting difference; the nomina electronica carries a dedicated Teletrabajo devengado element (NIE158). |
| Resident foreign national under a Colombian contract | YES | Full reporting. Withheld under the ET art. 383 progressive table once tax residence is acquired. |
| NON-RESIDENT foreign national under a Colombian contract | YES | IN SCOPE and frequently mis-configured. Withheld at the flat 20% of ET art. 408 on the GROSS, with no art. 388 depuration, until the 183-day residence test is met (DIAN Concepto 011108 of 29 June 2026). See the resident / non-resident block in the Report Matrix. |
| Employed old-age pensioner in active service | YES | PILA worker sub-type 01; no pension contribution (PILA pension rate field = 0), health and the levies still apply. |
| Executive director / statutory legal representative holding an employment contract | YES | Reported as an ordinary employee where an employment contract exists. |
| Independent contractor (contrato de prestacion de servicios) | NO | Not an employee. The contractor self-files PILA on 40% of fees (planilla types I / Y, descoped) and is reported by the payer in the third-party information return, not in this employer filing. Withholding on their fees does pass through Formulario 350, but under a different casilla. |
| Public-sector employee (contributor type 47, SGP-funded entity) | NO | Public-sector only; PILA planilla types T and F are descoped in scope_manifest. |
| Non-executive director paid honorarios only (junta directiva) | NO | Not employment income. Reported by the payer as honorarios, not as rentas de trabajo of an employee. |
| Pensioner whose pension is paid by a fund, not by the employer | NO | The paying fund is the withholding agent and the reporting party, not the employer. |
| Annual salary and work income by concept | YES | Numerals 5 to 17, itemised in thirteen separate concepts. |
| Ecclesiastical emoluments as a separate concept | YES | Numeral 6 - a concept Formulario 220 does NOT separate, folding it into casilla 39. |
| Meal payments up to 41 UVT | YES, but informative only | Numeral 32. Res. 227 par. 1: 'se considera de caracter informativo'. It does NOT enter the gross total in numeral 22, because it is income of the food supplier, not of the employee. Formulario 220 has no counterpart casilla. |
| Meal payments above 41 UVT | YES | Numeral 8, as taxable income of the employee. |
| Severance, split three ways | YES | Numerals 18 (paid to the employee), 19 (deposited to the fund) and 20 (traditional pre-1991 regime). Note the ordering difference against Formulario 220. |
| Employee mandatory contributions | YES | Numerals 23 and 24. Pension and the solidarity fund are COMBINED in numeral 24. |
| Voluntary savings, split four ways | YES | Numerals 25 (RAIS), 26 (voluntary pension funds), 27 (AFC) and 28 (AVC). The nomina electronica has one element for the first two and none for the fourth. |
| Rolling six-month average labour income | YES | Numeral 33, but ONLY for employees to whom ET art. 206 num. 4 applies. |
| Withholding practised | YES | Numeral 29, resident or non-resident alike. |
| Employer social-security contributions | NO | The return reports the BENEFICIARY's income and the BENEFICIARY's contributions. The employer's cost appears only on PILA. |
| VAT as a higher cost and VAT withholding | NO | Numerals 30 and 31 exist in the format but are not payroll figures - Colombian employment income carries no VAT. Owned by the client's tax function. |
| Trust and collaboration-contract identifiers | NO | Numerals 35 and 36. Res. 227 par. 4: for an ordinary employer reporting under concepto 1 they 'no se deben diligenciar'. |
| Reported on the accrual (causacion) date | NO | Every Colombian payroll filing is keyed to the PAYMENT or credit-in-account date, not to the accrual date. A December salary paid in January falls in the January filing. |
| Reported on the payment or credit-in-account date | YES | The universal Colombian rule - pago o abono en cuenta. Applies to the nomina electronica (Res. DIAN 000013 de 2021 art. 3), to Formulario 350, to Formulario 220 and to Formato 2276. |
| A semi-monthly payroll produces two filings a month | NO | It does not. The nomina electronica is expressly ONE monthly document per employee 'irrespective of whether the obliged party makes payments in different or shorter periods' (art. 3). PILA and Formulario 350 are likewise monthly. |
| Nil period still filed | VARIES | Formulario 350: NOT required where no operation subject to withholding occurred in the period (ET art. 606 par. 2). Nomina electronica: no document where there was no payment. PILA: a planilla is still required while the employment relationship subsists. |
| Corrections handled by re-filing the whole period | VARIES | Nomina electronica: NO - a dedicated adjustment note (NominaIndividualDeAjuste, Reemplazar or Eliminar) referencing the original CUNE. Formulario 350: yes, a corrective return. PILA: a planilla type N (correcciones) or M (mora). |
| Employee who left during the year | YES | One record covering the payments actually made in the year. There is no partial-year marker; the amounts simply reflect the shorter period. |
| Employee whose withholding was nil | YES | A record is still required. Res. 227 par. 2: 'Las casillas de valores son de obligatorio diligenciamiento. En caso de no tener ningun valor, se deben diligenciar con cero (0)'. |
| Natural person paid work income OUTSIDE an employment relationship | YES | Res. 227 par. 5: reported here WHERE the payment was withheld under ET art. 383. Where art. 383 did not apply, it goes to the ordinary payments-and-withholdings formats instead - not here. |
| Work income paid out of trust assets | PARTIAL | Reported with numeral 1 = 2 (Fiduciarias) and the trust identified in numeral 35. Not an ordinary corporate payroll case; where it arises, the reporting party is the fiduciaria, not the employer. |
| Educational support administered by a third party | NO | Res. 227 par. 7 sends it to Formato 5247 concepto 5102. Only support paid DIRECTLY by the employer goes to numeral 16. |
| Employee with a dependant claimed under art. 387 par. 2 | YES | Numeral 34. Must agree with Formulario 220 casillas 79 and 80 - this is where DIAN cross-checks a dependant claimed twice. |
| Employee who acquired residence mid-year | YES | ONE record. Numeral 29 sums the 20% months and the art. 383 months; the format has no field to disclose the split. |

## CO-DIAN-005-Formato2276 - Sheet 5: File Structure


```
FILE STRUCTURE AND TECHNICAL SPECIFICATIONS
```


| Item | Value |
|---|---|
| Channel | file_upload into the DIAN informacion exogena service at www.dian.gov.co, in an authenticated portal session signed with the Instrumento de Firma Electronica (IFE). CONFIRMED. |
| File format | XML prepared to the DIAN especificaciones tecnicas for Formato 2276 version 4. Res. DIAN 000227 de 2025 art. 1.3.10.1 lists the format in its formatos-y-especificaciones-tecnicas table as entry T3.45, version 4. |
| FORMAT TO CONFIRM | The especificaciones tecnicas annex that fixes the XML ELEMENT NAMES, the field ordering and the column lengths is a SEPARATE document and is NOT in the local source registry. Res. 227 prescribes WHAT to report, not how the file is shaped. Every element path in this Spec is therefore marked DERIVED in column T. Obtain the published especificaciones tecnicas before configuring a productive layout. |
| Why this is NOT machine-to-machine | There is no SOAP service, no XAdES document signature and no synchronous ApplicationResponse. Contrast the nomina electronica (CO-DIAN-001), which has all three. Here the employer uploads through an authenticated portal session and reads the outcome on screen. |
| What the model below is | A NEUTRAL FIELD MODEL - the resolution's own thirty-six numerals in their published order, plus the envelope attributes the deadline depends on. It is a LOGICAL MODEL, not the wire format. |
| Envelope | Informant NIT \| Taxpayer category (Gran Contribuyente / persona juridica / persona natural) \| Tax year \| Format 2276 version 4 |
| Block 1 - Entidad informante | N01 Entidad informante (code 1 to 8) \| N35 Identificacion del fideicomiso (only where N01 = 2) \| N36 Tipo documento e identificacion del participante en contrato (only where N01 is 3 to 8) |
| Block 2 - Beneficiario | N02 Tipo de documento y numero de identificacion \| N03 Apellidos y nombres \| N04 Ubicacion (departamento y municipio) |
| Block 3 - Ingresos | N05 Salarios \| N06 Emolumentos eclesiasticos \| N07 Bonos, cheques, tarjetas, vales \| N08 Exceso alimentacion > 41 UVT \| N09 Honorarios \| N10 Servicios \| N11 Comisiones \| N12 Prestaciones sociales \| N13 Viaticos \| N14 Gastos de representacion \| N15 Compensaciones trabajo asociado cooperativo \| N16 Apoyos economicos educativos \| N17 Otros pagos |
| Block 4 - Cesantias | N18 Pagadas al empleado \| N19 Consignadas al fondo \| N20 Regimen tradicional del CST |
| Block 5 - Pensiones y total | N21 Pensiones de jubilacion, vejez o invalidez \| N22 TOTAL INGRESOS BRUTOS |
| Block 6 - Aportes | N23 Aportes obligatorios salud \| N24 Aportes obligatorios pensiones y solidaridad \| N25 Aportes voluntarios RAIS \| N26 Aportes voluntarios fondos de pensiones \| N27 Aportes AFC \| N28 Aportes AVC |
| Block 7 - Retenciones e IVA | N29 Retenciones practicadas \| N30 IVA mayor valor del costo o gasto \| N31 Retencion de IVA |
| Block 8 - Informativos y especiales | N32 Alimentacion hasta 41 UVT (INFORMATIVE, outside N22) \| N33 Ingreso laboral promedio ultimos 6 meses \| N34 Dependiente economico |
| Paragrafo 1 | Numeral 32 (meal payments up to 41 UVT) 'se considera de caracter informativo'. It therefore does NOT enter the gross total in numeral 22. |
| Paragrafo 2 | 'Las casillas de valores son de obligatorio diligenciamiento. En caso de no tener ningun valor, se deben diligenciar con cero (0).' Every value field is populated; write ZERO, never blank. |
| Paragrafo 3 | The eight-value Entidad Informante code table - reproduced on Sheet 6. |
| Paragrafo 4 | For an ordinary employer the Entidad Informante is concepto 1 (Informante general), and numerals 35 and 36 are NOT filled. Where the income is paid out of trust assets, concepto 2 plus numeral 35 and no numeral 36. Where it is paid under a collaboration contract, the contract concepto plus numeral 36 and no numeral 35. |
| Paragrafo 5 | Payments to natural persons receiving work income NOT from an employment, legal or statutory relationship, but withheld under ET art. 383, ARE reported in Formato 2276. Where art. 383 withholding did NOT apply, they are reported in the ordinary payments-and-withholdings formats instead. |
| Paragrafo 6 | 'El valor reportado en el numeral 7 no debe contener la informacion reportada en los demas numerales del Formato 2276.' Numeral 7 is residual - it must not double-report anything already in another numeral. |
| Paragrafo 7 | Educational support paid DIRECTLY by the employer goes to numeral 16; support administered by a THIRD PARTY goes to Formato 5247 concepto 5102. Added by Res. 188 de 2024 art. 8; first applies to tax year 2025, filed in calendar 2026 (art. 1.3.9.1.3 par. 5). |
| Rounding | Whole pesos, no decimals, no thousand separators, zero-filled where nil. This is the fourth Colombian convention encountered across the six filings, alongside the nomina electronica (2 decimals round-half-to-even), PILA (round UP to the next 100) and Formulario 350 and 220 (nearest 1,000). Store the unrounded value once and apply each convention at output. |
| Nil values | Write ZERO. Res. 227 par. 2 is explicit. This matches Formulario 220 and INVERTS the nomina electronica, where an inapplicable element is omitted. |
| Grandes Contribuyentes | By the LAST digit of the NIT: 1 = 17th business day of April; 2 = 18th business day of April; 3 = 1st business day of May; 4 = 2nd; 5 = 3rd; 6 = 4th; 7 = 5th; 8 = 6th; 9 = 7th; 0 = 8th business day of May. |
| Personas juridicas y naturales | By the LAST TWO digits of the NIT, in five-number bands: 01-05 = 9th business day of May, rising one business day per band through 51-55 = 19th business day of May, then 56-60 = 1st business day of June, through 96-00 = 9th business day of June. |
| Category tested at filing | Art. 1.3.9.1.3 par. 1: the Gran Contribuyente, legal-person or natural-person status is the one held AT THE MOMENT OF FILING, not at the year end being reported. |
| Three different deadline rules | Formulario 350 uses the LAST digit and the 7th-16th business day of the following MONTH. PILA uses the LAST TWO digits and the 2nd-16th business day of the following MONTH. This return uses the LAST digit for Grandes Contribuyentes and the LAST TWO for everyone else, in MAY or JUNE of the following YEAR. Do not generalise one rule to another filing. |

```
FIELD OPTIONS - CODE TABLES
```


| Code | Abbrev | Local (ES) | EN Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | GEN | Informante general | THE VALUE FOR AN ORDINARY EMPLOYER. Par. 4: 'cuando el empleador reporte pagos o abonos en cuenta por concepto de rentas de trabajo y de pensiones, la casilla Entidad Informante se debe diligenciar con el concepto 1 - Informante general', and numerals 35 and 36 are left blank. |
| 2 | FID | Fiduciarias | Where the work income is paid out of trust assets. Requires numeral 35 (identificacion del fideicomiso); numeral 36 stays blank. |
| 3 | MAND | Mandatario y/o Administrador delegado | Requires numeral 36 (contract participants); numeral 35 stays blank. |
| 4 | CONS | Consorcio y/o Union Temporal | Requires numeral 36. |
| 5 | HIDRO | Exploracion y Explotacion de hidrocarburos, gases y minerales | Requires numeral 36. |
| 6 | JV | Joint Venture | Requires numeral 36. |
| 7 | CP | Gestor en Cuentas en Participacion | Requires numeral 36. |
| 8 | COOP-PUB | Convenios de cooperacion con entidades publicas | Requires numeral 36. |
| N18 | PAGADA | Cesantias e intereses efectivamente pagadas al empleado | Amounts ACTUALLY PAID to the employee. Formulario 220 casilla 47. Same position in both. |
| N19 | FONDO | Cesantias consignadas al fondo de cesantias | Deposited to the fund by 14 February under Ley 50 de 1990 art. 99. FORMULARIO 220 CASILLA 49 - NOT 48. |
| N20 | TRADICIONAL | Auxilio de cesantias del regimen tradicional del CST | Pre-1991 retroactive regime. FORMULARIO 220 CASILLA 48 - NOT 49. |
| (mapping warning) | - | - | THE SECOND AND THIRD LINES ARE TRANSPOSED between the two artefacts. A positional mapping - 47 to 18, 48 to 19, 49 to 20 - swaps the fund deposit and the traditional-regime amount. Map by MEANING. |
| 41 UVT | 2,147,334 | Tope mensual de alimentacion, art. 387-1 | Meal payments to a third party up to this monthly amount are NOT income of the employee. They go to numeral 32 as INFORMATIVE and stay outside the gross total in numeral 22. The excess goes to numeral 8 as taxable income. |
| 310 UVT | 16,235,940 | Salario mensual maximo para el beneficio de alimentacion | Where the employee's monthly salary EXCEEDS this, the art. 387-1 exclusion does not apply at all: the whole meal payment falls to numeral 7 and BOTH numeral 8 and numeral 32 stay nil. |
| 350 UVT | 18,330,900 | Ingreso laboral promedio para la exencion de cesantias, art. 206 num. 4 | Severance and interest are exempt in full where the six-month average in numeral 33 does not exceed this; tapered above. |
| 1,000 UVT | 52,374,000 | Tope mensual exento de pensiones, art. 206 num. 5 | Employer-paid pension income is taxable only above this monthly amount. Numeral 21. |
| 32 UVT | 1,675,968 | Tope mensual de la deduccion por dependientes, art. 387 par. 2 | The dependants deduction is 10% of gross employment income capped at this monthly amount. The dependant is identified in numeral 34. |
| 11 | RC | Registro civil | Civil birth registration. Realistic only at numeral 34 (economic dependant), never at numeral 2. |
| 12 | TI | Tarjeta de identidad | Identity card for a person aged 7 to 17. |
| 13 | CC | Cedula de ciudadania | National identity card. The great majority of beneficiaries and dependants. |
| 21 | TE | Tarjeta de extranjeria | Foreigner's identity card. |
| 22 | CE | Cedula de extranjeria | Foreign resident's identity card. |
| 31 | NIT | NIT | Tax identification number, where the beneficiary is an entity. |
| 41 | PA | Pasaporte | Passport. |
| 42 | DIE | Documento de identificacion extranjero | Foreign identity document. |
| 47 | PEP | PEP - Permiso Especial de Permanencia | Special residence permit. CO-GAP-06: there is still no code for the PPT. |
| 50 | NIT-EXT | NIT de otro pais | Foreign tax identification number. |
| 91 | NUIP | NUIP | Unique personal identification number; employee only. |
| (mapping warning) | - | - | THREE artefacts, THREE catalogues. Formulario 220 prints a SIX-value list (12, 13, 21, 22, 31, 41) and PILA uses two-letter codes (CC, CE, TI, PA, CD, SC, RC, PE). Map by MEANING through the Data Dictionary master value; never copy a code between channels. |
| 11 / 001 | BOG | Bogota D.C. - departamento y municipio | The beneficiary's location. NOT the same question as the nomina electronica place of work (NIE051 / NIE052) or the Formulario 220 place where the withholding was practised (casillas 34 / 35) - three artefacts, three different geographic questions about the same person. |
| GC last digit 1 | - | Decimo septimo dia habil del mes de abril | Grandes Contribuyentes. |
| GC last digit 2 | - | Decimo octavo dia habil del mes de abril | Grandes Contribuyentes. |
| GC last digits 3-0 | - | Primer a octavo dia habil del mes de mayo | Grandes Contribuyentes: 3 = 1st business day of May, then one business day per digit through 0 = 8th business day of May. |
| PJ/PN 01-05 | - | Noveno dia habil del mes de mayo | Personas juridicas y naturales, by the LAST TWO digits of the NIT. |
| PJ/PN 06-55 | - | Decimo a decimo noveno dia habil del mes de mayo | One business day per five-number band. |
| PJ/PN 56-00 | - | Primer a noveno dia habil del mes de junio | The bands continue into June: 56-60 = 1st business day of June through 96-00 = 9th business day of June. |
| (par. 1) | - | Calidad al momento de informar | The Gran Contribuyente, legal-person or natural-person status used is the one held AT THE MOMENT OF FILING, not at the reported year end. |
| 2276 / 4 | T3.45 | Informacion de rentas de trabajo y pensiones, version 4 | Listed in the formatos-y-especificaciones-tecnicas table at Res. 227 art. 1.3.10.1 as entry T3.45. A version mismatch is rejected at upload. |
| 5247 / - | - | Formato para apoyos economicos administrados por terceros | NOT a payroll filing. Educational support ADMINISTERED BY A THIRD PARTY is reported there under concepto 5102 instead of at numeral 16 here (Res. 227 par. 7). |
| N30 / N31 | - | IVA mayor valor del costo o gasto / Retencion de IVA | Colombian employment income carries no VAT. Relevant only where the beneficiary is a VAT-responsible contractor paid under the ET art. 383 table. Owned by the client's tax function. |
| N35 / N36 | - | Fideicomiso / participante en contrato | Res. 227 par. 4: for an ordinary employer reporting under concepto 1 they 'no se deben diligenciar'. Mutually exclusive with each other. |


## CO-DIAN-005-Formato2276 - Sheet 7: Population Rules


```
POPULATION RULES - HOW TO FILL EACH FIELD FROM PAYROLL
```


| Field Name | Source System | Population Logic | Edge Cases |
|---|---|---|---|
| Numeral 1 - Entidad informante | Configuration | For an ordinary employer reporting employment and pension income, set the code to 1 - Informante general - and leave numerals 35 and 36 BLANK. Res. 227 par. 4 states this in terms. Depart from concepto 1 only where the income was genuinely paid out of trust assets (concepto 2, plus numeral 35) or under a collaboration contract (conceptos 3 to 8, plus numeral 36). | The three cases are mutually exclusive and each drives a different pair of numerals. Getting the concepto wrong changes which identifier fields DIAN expects and produces a validation failure rather than a silent misstatement. |
| Numerals 5 to 21 - the income concepts | Payroll engine | Accumulate each concept separately for the whole tax year on the PAYMENT date. Then apply the two exclusion rules the resolution imposes: par. 6 forbids numeral 7 from repeating anything reported in another numeral, and par. 7 sends third-party-administered educational support to Formato 5247 rather than to numeral 16. | Note two concepts that Formulario 220 does NOT separate: numeral 6 (ecclesiastical emoluments), which Form 220 folds into casilla 39, and numeral 32 (meal payments up to 41 UVT), for which Form 220 has no casilla at all. |
| Numeral 22 - Total ingresos brutos | Engine (computed) | Sum numerals 5 to 21. DO NOT include numeral 32: par. 1 makes it informative, because meal payments below the threshold are income of the food supplier and not of the employee. Assert that numeral 22 equals Formulario 220 casilla 52 for the same employee and year. | Including numeral 32 in the total overstates the employee's gross income and breaks the reconciliation to Formulario 220 - and it is an easy mistake because every other value numeral does enter the total. |
| Numeral 33 - Ingreso laboral promedio | Payroll engine | Populate ONLY for employees to whom ET art. 206 num. 4 applies - that is, where severance or its interest was paid or deposited in the year, so that numerals 18, 19 or 20 are non-zero. The resolution says 'UNICAMENTE'. Maintain the rolling six-month average year-round; on a termination use the last six months OF EMPLOYMENT. | The average cannot be reconstructed from annual totals at year end. Populating it for every employee regardless is also wrong: the resolution restricts it, and an unnecessary value invites an enquiry. |
| Numeral 34 - Dependiente economico | Employee master data | Populate the document type and number of each dependant claimed under ET art. 387 par. 2 during the year. Keep it byte-identical to Formulario 220 casillas 79 and 80. | THIS IS THE FIELD DIAN USES TO CROSS-CHECK a dependant claimed by more than one taxpayer - typically both parents claiming the same child. A divergence between this return and the certificate the employee holds produces an enquiry to the employee, not to the employer. |
| Write zero, never blank | Engine (formatting) | Res. 227 par. 2: 'Las casillas de valores son de obligatorio diligenciamiento. En caso de no tener ningun valor, se deben diligenciar con cero (0).' Every value numeral is populated. | Matches Formulario 220 and INVERTS the nomina electronica, where an inapplicable element is omitted rather than zero-filled. Two DIAN artefacts, two opposite conventions. |
| Numeral 7 must not double-report | Engine (validation) | Par. 6 forbids numeral 7 from containing anything reported in another numeral. Before filing, assert that no amount is counted both in numeral 7 and in numerals 5, 8, 12, 17 or elsewhere. | The risk is real because voucher and card payments can plausibly be classified as salary (numeral 5), as a meal benefit (numerals 8 or 32) or as other payments (numeral 17). Fix the classification on the wage type, once. |
| The meal-payment fork | Payroll engine | Apply the two ET art. 387-1 tests MONTHLY and in sequence. First: does the employee's monthly salary exceed 310 UVT (COP 16,235,940 in 2026)? If YES, the whole meal payment goes to numeral 7 and both numeral 8 and numeral 32 stay nil for that month. If NO, split the payment at 41 UVT (COP 2,147,334): the part up to the threshold goes to numeral 32 (informative), and the EXCESS goes to numeral 8 (taxable). | Three possible destinations for one payment, decided monthly. An employee can fork one way in some months and the other way in others within the same annual record, so the accumulation must be per month and then summed - not decided once on the annual figures. |
| Numerals 35 and 36 are mutually exclusive | Engine (validation) | Numeral 35 is populated only where numeral 1 = 2; numeral 36 only where numeral 1 is 3 to 8; both blank where numeral 1 = 1. Never both. | Res. 227 par. 4 spells out each of the three cases explicitly. |
| Numerals 19 and 20 are mutually exclusive per employee | Engine (validation) | An employee is in the Ley 50 de 1990 fund-deposit regime or in the pre-1991 traditional regime, not both. Assert that at most one of numerals 19 and 20 is non-zero for any beneficiary. | The only exception is a formal regime election in the transition year - rare enough to warrant an explicit override rather than a silent allowance. |
| Whole pesos, no separators | Engine (formatting) | Amounts are whole Colombian pesos with no decimals and no thousand separators. Round once, at the annual accumulation, not per month. | This is the fourth rounding convention across the six filings. Store the unrounded value once and apply each convention at output; never derive one artefact's figures from another's rounded output. |
| Numeral 24 combines two concepts | Engine (accumulation) | Numeral 24 carries mandatory PENSION contributions AND the solidarity-fund surcharge in one figure, exactly as Formulario 220 casilla 54 does. The nomina electronica reports three separate elements and PILA three separate campos. | Sum them only at this point. Combining them earlier makes the monthly artefacts impossible to produce correctly. |
| Numerals 25 and 26 split one nomina electronica element | Engine (accumulation) | Numeral 25 is voluntary RAIS contributions under ET art. 55; numeral 26 is voluntary contributions to VOLUNTARY pension funds under ET art. 126-1. The nomina electronica has ONE element for both (PensionVoluntaria, NIE198). | Carry the distinction on the wage type from configuration. It cannot be recovered from the XML at year end. |
| Numeral 28 has no nomina electronica element | Engine (accumulation) | AVC contributions to the Fondo Nacional del Ahorro have no dedicated nomina electronica element and fall to OtraDeduccion (NIE197). Tag the wage type so the amount can be recovered here and at Formulario 220 casilla 58. | Without the tag the amount is indistinguishable from any other 'other deduction' in the annual accumulation. |
| Numeral 29 - RESIDENT | Payroll engine (tax) | Accumulate the withholding actually practised under the ET art. 383 progressive table, Procedure 1 or Procedure 2, on the art. 388 depurated base. Assert that it equals Formulario 220 casilla 60 for the same employee and twelve months of nomina electronica NIE177. | An employee whose depurated base fell in the 0% band all year still gets a record, with numeral 29 at zero and numerals 5 to 22 populated. |
| Numeral 29 - NON-RESIDENT | Payroll engine (tax) | Accumulate the flat 20% of ET art. 408 applied to the GROSS. Numerals 23 and 24 still report the contributions actually deducted, but they did NOT reduce the base - art. 408 admits no depuration. Numeral 33 is reported where severance was paid but has no effect, because the art. 206 num. 4 exemption belongs to the resident cedula-general regime. | USEFUL INTERNAL CONTROL: for a pure non-resident record, numeral 29 should be almost exactly 20% of numeral 22. A material divergence means either a residence change during the year or a misclassification. The format has no residence field, so this arithmetic is the only signal. |
| Mid-year residence change | Payroll engine (tax) | Produce ONE record. Numeral 29 sums the months withheld at 20% under art. 408 and the months withheld under the art. 383 table. The format has no field to disclose the split. | Retain the split internally. It also breaks the 20%-of-numeral-22 control above, so document the reason before the control fires. |
| Non-employment work income under par. 5 | Payroll engine / accounts payable | Where the employer pays a natural person work income that does NOT arise from an employment, legal or statutory relationship, report it HERE if it was withheld under ET art. 383 (art. 383 par. 2, added by Ley 2277 de 2022 art. 8, using the pagos mensualizados method). Where art. 383 withholding did NOT apply, report it in the ordinary payments-and-withholdings formats instead - not here. | The test is about the WITHHOLDING REGIME, not about residence and not about employment status. It is the most commonly misread paragraph of the article, and it determines which FORMAT the payment appears in, not merely which numeral. |
| Work performed entirely abroad by a non-resident | Payroll engine (tax) | Foreign-source income, not withheld, and NOT reported in this return at all. | The evidencing field is the nomina electronica place of work (NIE050). Keep the two consistent: a document showing a non-CO place of work for a non-resident should produce no 2276 record. |
| Numeral 22 arithmetic | Engine (validation) | Numeral 22 must equal the sum of numerals 5 to 21, EXCLUDING numeral 32. Assert it, and assert equality with Formulario 220 casilla 52 for the same employee. | The exclusion of numeral 32 is the trap. Every other value numeral enters the total. |
| Numerals 8 and 32 are a pair | Engine (validation) | Where either is non-zero the other is normally non-zero too, because the same underlying payment is split at 41 UVT. The exception is an employee earning over 310 UVT, where both are nil and the payment sits in numeral 7 instead. | Three-way interlock between numerals 7, 8 and 32, driven by two monthly UVT tests. |
| Numerals 18, 19, 20 and 33 travel together | Engine (validation) | Where any of numerals 18, 19 or 20 is non-zero, numeral 33 should be populated, because ET art. 206 num. 4 applies. Where all three are zero, numeral 33 should be blank. | The resolution restricts numeral 33 with the word 'unicamente', so populating it unnecessarily is as wrong as omitting it when required. |
| Numeral 1 drives numerals 35 and 36 | Engine (validation) | Concepto 1 - both blank. Concepto 2 - numeral 35 populated, numeral 36 blank. Conceptos 3 to 8 - numeral 36 populated, numeral 35 blank. | Implement as a three-branch rule, not as two independent nullable fields. |
| Cross-report reconciliation | Engine (reconciliation) | Before filing, assert per beneficiary: numeral 22 equals Formulario 220 casilla 52; numeral 29 equals casilla 60 and twelve months of nomina electronica NIE177; numerals 23 and 24 equal casillas 53 and 54 and the employee portions of PILA campos 55, 47, 51 and 52; numeral 19 equals the deposit made under CO-CESANTIAS-006; numeral 34 equals Formulario 220 casillas 79 and 80. Across the population, assert that the sum of numeral 29 equals twelve months of Formulario 350 casilla 93. | MAP THE SEVERANCE LINES BY MEANING: numeral 18 to casilla 47, numeral 19 to casilla 49, numeral 20 to casilla 48. A positional mapping swaps the fund deposit and the traditional-regime amount, and the error is invisible in the totals because both feed numeral 22. |

| (scenario) | Beneficiary | Juan Carlos Perez Gomez, CC 1020304050, employed the whole of 2026, salary COP 12,000,000 a month, tax RESIDENT. | The same employee and the same annual build-up as the Formulario 220 workbook, so the two can be reconciled line by line. |
| 1 | N01 - Entidad informante | Informante general. | Res. 227 par. 4 requires concepto 1 for an ordinary employer, and numerals 35 and 36 are then left blank. |
| 13 / 1020304050 | N02 - Tipo de documento y numero de identificacion | Composite: document-type code plus the number. | The join key across every Colombian artefact. Note that the three artefacts use THREE different document-type catalogues - the nomina electronica's numeric table 5.2.1, Formulario 220's six-value list, and PILA's two-letter codes. Map, never copy. |
| PEREZ / GOMEZ / JUAN / CARLOS | N03 - Apellidos y nombres | Composite: four sub-fields in the standard DIAN order. | DIAN matches on the surname columns, so the order matters. |
| 11 / 001 | N04 - Ubicacion del beneficiario | Bogota D.C. | The BENEFICIARY's location - a different question from the nomina electronica place of work and from the Formulario 220 place where the withholding was practised. |
| 144000000 | N05 - Pagos por salarios | Twelve months at COP 12,000,000. | Equals Formulario 220 casilla 36. |
| 0 | N06 - Emolumentos eclesiasticos | Not applicable. | A SEPARATE numeral here; Formulario 220 folds ecclesiastical emoluments into casilla 39 (honorarios). Written as ZERO, per par. 2. |
| 0 | N07 - Bonos, cheques, tarjetas, vales | No such payments. | Par. 6: this numeral must not repeat anything reported elsewhere in the format. It is residual. |
| 0 / 0 | N08 / N32 - Alimentacion | No third-party meal payments, so both the excess (N08) and the informative amount up to 41 UVT (N32) are nil. | Had there been meal payments, the monthly fork would apply: salary above 310 UVT sends the whole amount to N07; salary at or below 310 UVT splits it at 41 UVT between N32 (informative) and N08 (taxable). |
| 12000000 | N12 - Prestaciones sociales | The statutory service bonus, paid in two instalments by 30 June and 20 December. | Excludes severance and its interest, which have their own three numerals. Equals Formulario 220 casilla 42. |
| 1440000 | N18 - Cesantias e intereses PAGADAS al empleado | The 12% interest on the 31 December 2025 balance, paid in January 2026. |  |
| 12000000 | N19 - Cesantias CONSIGNADAS al fondo | One month's salary for 2026, deposited by 14 February 2027. |  |
| 0 | N20 - Regimen tradicional del CST | The employee is under the Ley 50 de 1990 regime, so this is nil. |  |
| 157440000 | N22 - Total ingresos brutos | 144,000,000 + 12,000,000 + 1,440,000 + 12,000,000 = 157,440,000. | Numerals 5 to 21 summed, EXCLUDING numeral 32 - par. 1 makes that informative. Equals Formulario 220 casilla 52. |
| 5760000 | N23 - Aportes obligatorios por salud | Twelve months at COP 480,000. |  |
| 7200000 | N24 - Aportes obligatorios a pensiones y solidaridad pensional | Twelve months at COP 600,000 - pension 480,000 plus solidarity 120,000. | ONE numeral for TWO concepts, as in Formulario 220 casilla 54. The nomina electronica keeps them as NIE166 and NIE168; PILA as campos 47 and 51. Sum only here. |
| 0 / 0 / 0 / 0 | N25 / N26 / N27 / N28 | No voluntary savings. | N25 and N26 split what the nomina electronica carries in ONE element (NIE198); N28 (AVC) has no nomina electronica element at all and must be tagged on the wage type. |
| 7404000 | N29 - Retenciones practicadas | The year's withholding under the ET art. 383 table. |  |
| 13120000 | N33 - Ingreso laboral promedio de los ultimos seis meses | Populated because severance was paid and deposited, so ET art. 206 num. 4 applies. | Below 350 UVT (COP 18,330,900), so the severance in N18 and N19 is exempt in full. The resolution restricts this numeral with the word 'UNICAMENTE' - do not populate it where numerals 18, 19 and 20 are all nil. |
| (blank) | N34 - Dependiente economico | No dependant claimed. | Where one is claimed, this must be byte-identical to Formulario 220 casillas 79 and 80. It is where DIAN cross-checks a dependant claimed by more than one taxpayer. |
| (blank) / (blank) | N35 / N36 | Not applicable under concepto 1. | Res. 227 par. 4: for an ordinary employer they 'no se deben diligenciar'. They are mutually exclusive with each other. |
| 0 / 0 | N30 / N31 - IVA | Not payroll fields. | Colombian employment income carries no VAT. Relevant only for a VAT-responsible contractor paid under the art. 383 table; owned by the client's tax function. |
| 157440000 / 31488000 | N22 / N29 for a NON-RESIDENT | The same gross, withheld at the flat 20% of ET art. 408. | USEFUL CONTROL: for a pure non-resident record, N29 is almost exactly 20% of N22. Here 31,488,000 / 157,440,000 = 20.00%. A resident's ratio is 4.7%. The format has no residence field, so this arithmetic is the only signal - and a mid-year residence change breaks it, which is itself worth flagging before the control fires. |
| 5760000 / 7200000 | N23 / N24 for a NON-RESIDENT | Unchanged - the contributions were still deducted. | But they did NOT reduce the withholding base: art. 408 taxes the gross. |
| 13120000 | N33 for a NON-RESIDENT | Reported where severance was paid, but of no effect. | The art. 206 num. 4 exemption belongs to the resident cedula-general regime. |
| Persona juridica, NIT ...56 | Deadline | 1st business day of June 2027, per Res. 227 art. 1.3.9.1.3. | THREE DIFFERENT DEADLINE RULES across the pipeline: Formulario 350 by the LAST digit, 7th-16th business day of the following MONTH; PILA by the LAST TWO digits, 2nd-16th business day of the following MONTH; this return by the last digit for Grandes Contribuyentes and the last two for everyone else, in MAY or JUNE of the following YEAR. |
| (at filing) | Calidad del informante | Art. 1.3.9.1.3 par. 1: the status held AT THE MOMENT OF FILING governs. | An employer that becomes a Gran Contribuyente between the year end and the filing date files on the EARLIER Gran Contribuyente calendar, not the later one. |


## CO-DIAN-005-Formato2276 - Sheet 9: Sample File


```
SAMPLE FILE - VERIFIED CHANNEL STATEMENT AND NEUTRAL RECORD MODEL

WHY THERE IS NO XML SAMPLE

Formato 2276 version 4 is uploaded as a file to the DIAN informacion exogena
service, and the exogena service accepts XML. That much is CONFIRMED from
Resolucion DIAN 000227 de 2025 arts. 1.3.7.1.1 and 1.3.10.1, both held locally.

What is NOT held locally, and is not published inside the resolution, is the
ESPECIFICACIONES TECNICAS annex for Formato 2276 version 4 - the document that
fixes the XML element names, the field ordering and the column lengths. The
resolution only points to it ("de acuerdo con los parametros establecidos en las
especificaciones tecnicas del Formato 2276 Version 4") and lists it in the
formats table at art. 1.3.10.1 as entry T3.45.

Writing an XML instance here would therefore mean INVENTING element names. That
is exactly the fabrication the v2.7 standard forbids and the C25 audit fails.

What follows instead is a NEUTRAL RECORD MODEL: the resolution's own thirty-six
numerals in their published order, populated with the worked example. This is
the content the engine must produce; the wrapper comes from the especificaciones
tecnicas.

FORMAT TO CONFIRM vs a real filing: obtain the published especificaciones
tecnicas for Formato 2276 v4 and map each numeral below to its element name,
ordinal position and column length before configuring a productive layout.

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ENVELOPE (attributes the deadline and validation depend on)
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Informant NIT ......................... 900123456
  Taxpayer category ..................... Persona juridica
  Tax year reported ..................... 2026
  Format / version ...................... 2276 / 4   (Res. 227 art. 1.3.10.1, T3.45)
  Deadline .............................. 1st business day of June 2027
                                          (NIT ends 56; art. 1.3.9.1.3)

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
RECORD - ONE PER BENEFICIARY
Res. DIAN 000227 de 2025, art. 1.3.7.1.1, numerals 1 to 36 in published order
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  N01  Entidad informante ...................... 1  (Informante general)
  N02  Tipo doc. y numero de identificacion .... 13 / 1020304050
  N03  Apellidos y nombres ..................... PEREZ / GOMEZ / JUAN / CARLOS
  N04  Ubicacion del beneficiario .............. 11 / 001  (Bogota D.C.)

  N05  Pagos por salarios ...................... 144000000
  N06  Pagos por emolumentos eclesiasticos .....         0
  N07  Bonos, cheques, tarjetas, vales, etc. ...         0
  N08  Exceso alimentacion > 41 UVT (art.387-1)          0
  N09  Pagos por honorarios ....................         0
  N10  Pagos por servicios .....................         0
  N11  Pagos por comisiones ....................         0
  N12  Pagos por prestaciones sociales .........  12000000
  N13  Pagos por viaticos ......................         0
  N14  Pagos por gastos de representacion ......         0
  N15  Compensaciones trabajo asociado coop. ...         0
  N16  Apoyos economicos educativos (Estado) ...         0
  N17  Otros pagos .............................         0

  N18  Cesantias e intereses PAGADAS al empleado   1440000    -> F220 casilla 47
  N19  Cesantias CONSIGNADAS al fondo ..........  12000000    -> F220 casilla 49  (NOT 48)
  N20  Cesantias REGIMEN TRADICIONAL del CST ...         0    -> F220 casilla 48  (NOT 49)

  N21  Pensiones de jubilacion, vejez, invalidez         0
  N22  TOTAL INGRESOS BRUTOS ................... 157440000
       (numerals 5 to 21; numeral 32 is EXCLUDED - par. 1 makes it informative)

  N23  Aportes obligatorios por salud ..........   5760000
  N24  Aportes obligatorios pensiones y FSP ....   7200000
  N25  Aportes voluntarios RAIS ................         0
  N26  Aportes voluntarios fondos de pensiones .         0
  N27  Aportes a cuentas AFC ...................         0
  N28  Aportes a cuentas AVC ...................         0

  N29  RETENCIONES PRACTICADAS .................   7404000
  N30  IVA mayor valor del costo o gasto .......         0   (not a payroll field)
  N31  Retencion de IVA ........................         0   (not a payroll field)

  N32  Alimentacion mensual hasta 41 UVT .......         0   INFORMATIVE - outside N22
  N33  Ingreso laboral promedio ultimos 6 meses  13120000   (only where art.206 num.4 applies)
  N34  Dependiente economico ...................   (blank)   (none claimed)
  N35  Identificacion del fideicomiso ..........   (blank)   (par. 4: blank under concepto 1)
  N36  Participante en contrato ................   (blank)   (par. 4: blank under concepto 1)

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
NON-RESIDENT VARIANT (v2.8 control B1)
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
The same record for a NON-RESIDENT differs in exactly one numeral - the format
has no residence field:

  N22  TOTAL INGRESOS BRUTOS ................... 157440000   (unchanged)
  N23  Aportes obligatorios por salud ..........   5760000   (unchanged - still deducted)
  N24  Aportes obligatorios pensiones y FSP ....   7200000   (unchanged - still deducted)
  N29  RETENCIONES PRACTICADAS .................  31488000   <== 20% of GROSS, ET art. 408
  N33  Ingreso laboral promedio ................  13120000   (reported, no effect)

INTERNAL CONTROL: for a pure non-resident record N29 / N22 = 20.00% exactly.
For this resident it is 4.70%. Because the format carries no residence flag,
that ratio is the only signal available - and a mid-year residence change
legitimately breaks it, so document the reason before the control fires.

The contributions in N23 and N24 are still reported because they were still
deducted, but they did NOT reduce the withholding base: ET art. 408 taxes the
gross with no depuration (DIAN Concepto 011108 of 29 June 2026). N33 is reported
where severance was paid but has no effect, because the art. 206 num. 4
exemption belongs to the resident cedula-general regime.

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
THE FOUR TRAPS IN THIS RECORD
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1. N32 is INFORMATIVE and does NOT enter N22 (par. 1). Every other value numeral
   does. Including it overstates gross income and breaks the reconciliation to
   Formulario 220 casilla 52.

2. N19 and N20 are TRANSPOSED against Formulario 220 casillas 48 and 49. Map by
   MEANING. A positional mapping swaps the fund deposit and the traditional-
   regime amount, and the error is invisible in N22 because both feed it.

3. N07 must not double-report anything in another numeral (par. 6). Voucher and
   card payments can plausibly be classified as salary, as a meal benefit or as
   other payments - fix the classification on the wage type, once.

4. N33 is restricted by the word UNICAMENTE to employees to whom ET art. 206
   num. 4 applies. Populating it for everyone is as wrong as omitting it when
   required.
```

```
REPORT MATRIX - ANNUAL SEVERANCE-SAVINGS DEPOSIT TO THE EMPLOYEE'S CHOSEN FUND
```


| Report ID | Report Name (EN) | Report Name (Local ES) | Authority | Frequency | Due Date Rule | Submission Method | File Format | Legal Basis | Accuracy Assessment |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| CO-CESANTIAS-006 | Annual Severance-Savings Deposit to the Employee's Chosen Fund | Consignacion anual de cesantias al fondo de cesantias elegido por el trabajador | Fondos de Cesantias (Proteccion, Porvenir, Colfondos, Skandia and the state-owned Fondo Nacional del Ahorro), reached through a licensed operador de informacion. NOT a government authority - this is the only one of the six in-scope Colombian filings whose recipient is a private administrator, which is why no government schema exists for it. | Annual - one record per employee per accrual year, liquidated at 31 December and deposited in the following February. Optionally supplemented by a monthly advance deposit through PILA (Ley 2466 de 2025 art. 64 par. 1), which is NOT yet operable. | BEFORE 15 FEBRUARY of the year following the 31 December liquidation - Ley 50 de 1990 art. 99 num. 3, verbatim: 'El valor liquidado por concepto de cesantia se consignara antes del 15 de febrero del ano siguiente, en cuenta individual a nombre del trabajador en el fondo de cesantia que el mismo elija'. The last compliant day is therefore 14 February, and the MinTrabajo draft decree confirms the same boundary from the other side: 'configurandose la mora a partir del 15 de febrero'. NON-BUSINESS-DAY SHIFT - TO CONFIRM: where 14 February falls on a Saturday, Sunday or holiday, Colombian practice moves the last day to the next business day, and for accrual year 2025 (14 February 2026 = Saturday) the shift to Monday 16 February 2026 was reported uniformly by El Tiempo, El Pais, Infobae and Cronista. No MinTrabajo concepto or Ministry circular stating the rule was located, so it rests on the general term rule and press consensus. FOR ACCRUAL YEAR 2026 THE LAST DAY IS SUNDAY 14 FEBRUARY 2027, which on the same reasoning shifts to Monday 15 February 2027 - but 15 February is also the first day of mora on the literal text, so DO NOT RELY ON THE SHIFT: deposit by Friday 12 February 2027. The penalty for getting this wrong is one day's salary per day of delay, per employee, uncapped. | PORTAL, TWO CHANNELS, THROUGH A LICENSED OPERADOR DE INFORMACION - not to a government portal. Channel A, online_form: 'Planilla En Linea' / 'Elaborar planilla', keying each employee into the operator's form. Channel B, file_upload: 'Liquidacion Por Archivo' / 'Envio de Archivo Unificado de Cesantias', uploading an employer-generated file. Payment then follows by PSE or by credit, and the operator returns a planilla number and a comprobante PDF. Verified independently in the published manuals of two operators - ACH Colombia S.A. (SOI), www.nuevosoi.com.co, instructivo DDS-DES-INS-008 v2, and Simple S.A., www.pagosimple.com, 'Manual Cesantias'. This is NOT machine-to-machine: there is no XSD, no SOAP service, no document signature and no certificate authentication. | For channel B, TWO PUBLISHED LAYOUTS, both without a header row: (1) FIXED-WIDTH TXT - 20 fields in 258 positions, record type constant '02', 5-position sequence, byte-exact Inicial-Final positions; (2) EXCEL 97-2003 .XLS - 16 columns A to P, data from the first row, all records on one sheet. SOI states .txt and .csv are accepted and that a file above 1 MB must be compressed before upload. NO XML AND NO XSD EXISTS for this filing - the word XML must not appear against it. Amounts are whole pesos, right-aligned and zero-filled, with a 4-position zero filler in front of each amount. NOTE that scope_manifest records the format as CSV: CSV is accepted, but the published field-level layouts are the fixed-width TXT and the XLS, and this Spec is written to those. | Ley 50 de 1990 art. 99 nums. 1-3 (the annual liquidation at 31 December, the employee's choice of fund, the deposit before 15 February and the one-day's-salary-per-day-of-delay penalty), as added to by Ley 2466 de 2025 art. 64 (pars. 1 and 2: the 8.33% monthly advance deposit and the 1% monthly interest option); CST (Decreto 2663 de 1950) arts. 249 (the entitlement), 250 (loss of the right), 253 (the base), 254 (prohibition of partial payment), 310 (construction workers, 3 days per completed month); CST art. 57 par. 4 as added by Ley 2466 de 2025 art. 15 (direct payment is discharging, affiliation still compulsory); Ley 52 de 1975 art. 1 (12% interest, paid to the employee in January); Decreto 1072 de 2015 arts. 2.2.1.3.1, 2.2.1.3.4, 2.2.1.3.5 and 2.2.1.3.13 (the current regulation, and the four articles the 2026 MinTrabajo draft decree would amend); CST art. 488 as substituted by Ley 2466 de 2025 art. 62 (three-year limitation); ET arts. 206 num. 4 and 385-386 (the tax treatment, which lives on Formulario 220 and Formato 2276, not here); Decreto 1469 de 2025 (SMMLV 2026 = COP 1,750,905 and transport allowance = COP 249,095, which set both the base and the minimum-recaudo validation). | 88% (PRIMARY for the obligation, PRIMARY for a published field layout, DERIVED for per-fund variation). What is byte-exact: the 20-field / 258-position fixed-width layout and the 16-column XLS layout, taken from the published Simple S.A. 'Manual Cesantias' sections 3.1 and 3.2 - every position, length, type, validation rule, the five-value fondo code table and the eight-value document-type table are transcribed, not derived. That is a material improvement on scope_manifest, which recorded this filing as having no published layout. The channel is confirmed independently by a second operator's published instructivo (ACH Colombia / SOI). The law is verbatim from the local registry for Ley 2466 de 2025 arts. 15 and 64 and quoted verbatim for Ley 50 de 1990 art. 99 num. 3. WHAT WOULD RAISE IT: (a) the Fondo Nacional del Ahorro instructivo for private employers, whose published URL now 404s after the FNA site redesign - FNA is a state entity and its layout would be the one genuinely official schema; (b) a real filed planilla and its comprobante, to confirm that the other operadores de informacion accept the same 258-position record rather than an operator-specific variant; (c) the MinSalud PILA regulation and the enacted decree, which would replace the whole MONTHLY ADVANCE section's DRAFT flags with real PILA positions. It would then reach 97%. |


```
SUBMISSION CHANNEL VERIFICATION (v2.7 STEP 3.5)
- Channel: online_form AND file_upload (fixed-width TXT / XLS 97-2003 / CSV) | Portal: a licensed operador de informacion - ACH Colombia S.A. (SOI), www.nuevosoi.com.co, and Simple S.A., www.pagosimple.com, both verified; Aportes en Linea, Asopagos, Enlace Operativo and MiPlanilla offer the same service | Source: SOI instructivo DDS-DES-INS-008 v2 and Simple S.A. 'Manual Cesantias' ss. 3.1-3.2, both held in 00-official-sources/pdfs/ | CONFIRMED as to channel and as to the 258-position layout; TO CONFIRM whether every operator accepts that same layout.
- THE RECIPIENT IS NOT A GOVERNMENT AUTHORITY. The money goes to a private fondo de cesantias, or to the state-owned FNA, in an individual account in the employee's name. There is no authority portal, no fiscal receipt and no tax-authority validation - which is exactly why no XSD exists and why this filing must never be labelled XML.
- THE OPERATOR IS THE SAME KIND OF INTERMEDIARY AS FOR PILA, BUT THE SERVICE IS NOT PILA. Cesantias is a Pago Complementario a la Seguridad Social (PCSS) and is selected as a distinct service inside the operator's portal, with its own planilla number series and its own period concept - a YEAR, not a month. Simple S.A. notes that the login is the same one used for PILA, which is the source of the frequent error of building a PILA planilla and expecting the cesantias to be in it.
- The DIAN portals play no part in this filing. Formulario 350, Formulario 220 and Formato 2276 go to DIAN; the cesantias deposit never does. What DIAN sees of cesantias is the reporting of the amounts on Formulario 220 casillas 48-49 and Formato 2276 numerals 19-20, which are separate filings with their own Specs (CO-DIAN-004 and CO-DIAN-005).
- File-size and structure constraints from the published instructivos: no header row; the data start on the first line or row; all records on one Excel sheet; a file above 1 MB must be compressed before upload (SOI); the fixed-width record is 258 characters and the sequence must start at 00001 and be consecutive.

INTEGRATION TYPE AND DIRECTION
- integration_type = BOTH (scope_manifest). OUTBOUND: the annual planilla, keyed or uploaded. INBOUND: CO-IN-CESANTIAS-CONFIRMATION, the deposit confirmation evidencing compliance with the February deadline. Both directions are modelled on Sheet 4 - the outbound record as T01-T20, the inbound values as E10, E11, E90, E91 and E93.
- product_mapping_required = true. The engine must hold the fund code as EMPLOYEE master data (it is the employee's statutory choice, not a company default), the 360-day service count, the CST art. 253 base including the transport allowance, the accrual-year period concept, the per-employee deposit-compliance status and, when the decree is enacted, the monthly-advance regime flag per contract.
- NOT DERIVABLE FROM THE MONTHLY FILINGS. The population is not the PILA population: a leaver who left in June is absent from the December PILA but is owed a pro rata deposit, while a salario integral employee is present in PILA and owed nothing here. Do not use the operator's seed-from-last-PILA convenience (E08).
- THE ONE-TO-MANY TRAP ON THE RETURN. Where the medio de pago is Pago Credito, the operator issues ONE PLANILLA NUMBER PER FONDO, so the reconciliation store must accept many planilla numbers for one payroll run (E05, E10).
- The engine must also produce two things that are NOT in this file and are commonly conflated with it: the 12% interest paid to the EMPLOYEE in January (IN01-IN03), and the tax figures that appear on Formulario 220 and Formato 2276. Different payees, different deadlines, different penalties.

RESIDENT / NON-RESIDENT TREATMENT (v2.8 control B1)
- RESIDENT AND NON-RESIDENT ARE TREATED IDENTICALLY IN THIS FILING, AND THAT IS THE POINT. Cesantias is a LABOUR entitlement under the CST, not a tax concept, so it attaches to the employment relationship and not to tax residence. A non-resident foreign national employed under a Colombian contract accrues cesantias on the same base, at the same 360-day rate, is deposited to a fund of their own choosing and is reported in this planilla on exactly the same record as a resident. There is no non-resident variant of the layout and no withholding on the deposit.
- WHERE RESIDENCE DOES BITE IS THE TAX TREATMENT, WHICH IS NOT THIS FILING. For a RESIDENT, ET art. 206 num. 4 exempts the severance savings and their interest in full where the average monthly income of the last six months is at or below 350 UVT (COP 18,330,900 in 2026), then tapers 90 / 80 / 60 / 40 / 20 per cent across the 350-410, 410-470, 470-530, 530-590 and 590-650 UVT bands, with nothing exempt above 650 UVT. For a NON-RESIDENT the art. 206 num. 4 exemption is unavailable - it belongs to the resident cedula general - and the payment is taxed at the flat 20% of ET art. 408 on the gross with no art. 388 depuration. Neither outcome changes a single character of the record sent to the fund.
- THE DOCUMENT TYPE IS WHERE THE FOREIGN EMPLOYEE ACTUALLY BREAKS. The layout admits CE, PA, CD, SC, PE and (in the XLS variant) PT, and SOI records that a Permiso Especial de Permanencia number must be exactly 15 digits. A regularisation that converts a PEP or PPT into a Cedula de Extranjeria changes the join key (T03 + T04) that the fund holds, so the change must reach the fund or the following February's deposit lands on an orphan account. See T03 and T04.
- A Colombian employee posted abroad but retained on a Colombian contract remains in scope; an employee of a foreign entity with no Colombian employment contract is out of scope entirely, because there is no CST relationship to accrue cesantias on.

RELATED FILINGS / CROSS-REFERENCES
- CO-DIAN-004 (Formulario 220) casillas 48 and 49 - the same cesantias figures reported to DIAN for the employee's certificate. They must reconcile to this planilla.
- CO-DIAN-005 (Formato 2276 v4) numerals 19 and 20 - the same figures again, and NOTE that numerals 19 and 20 are TRANSPOSED against Formulario 220 casillas 48 and 49. Map by meaning, per the CO-DIAN-005 Spec.
- CO-PILA-003 - the future channel for the monthly advance deposit under Ley 2466 de 2025 art. 64 par. 1, once MinSalud defines the tipo de cotizacion (see MA04). The PILA Spec will need a new contribution type when that happens.
- CO-DIAN-001 (nomina electronica) - carries the cesantias and the interest as devengado elements when they are PAID, on the payment-date rule. The XML shows the payment; this planilla shows the deposit; they are not the same event and can fall in different months.
- CCG Module 9 (termination and statutory benefits) for the base, the taper and the indemnity; CCG Module 10.1 report CO-RPT-006 and the consolidated 2026 filing calendar.
```


## CO-CESANTIAS-006-ConsignacionAnual - Sheet 3: Payroll Scope Summary


```
PAYROLL SCOPE SUMMARY - ANNUAL SEVERANCE-SAVINGS DEPOSIT TO THE EMPLOYEE'S CHOSEN FUND
```


| SCENARIO | REPORT? | NOTES / REASONING |
|---|---|---|
| Indefinite-term employee (contrato a termino indefinido) | YES | The core private-sector population. CST art. 47; Ley 2466 de 2025 art. 5. |
| Fixed-term employee (contrato a termino fijo, max 4 years) | YES | Identical reporting treatment to an indefinite-term contract. CST art. 46; Ley 2466 de 2025 art. 6. |
| Specific-work or task employee (contrato por obra o labor) | YES | An employment contract; CST art. 46. Nomina electronica TipoContrato = 3 (Obra o Labor). |
| Occasional / accidental / transitory worker (< 1 month, non-core activity) | YES | Still an employee under CST art. 6. Reported on every filing; the PILA planilla type and the day counts differ, not the obligation. |
| Part-time employee | YES | Colombia has no separate part-time regime. Reported normally; the PILA contribution base is floored at 1 SMMLV (COP 1,750,905) except for the worker type 51 partial-time regime. |
| All-inclusive salary employee (salario integral, >= 13 SMMLV) | YES | CST art. 132 num. 2. Reported with the integral-salary flag set; the contribution base is 70% of the integral salary, and the nomina electronica carries SalarioIntegral = true. |
| Apprentice (contrato de aprendizaje) | YES | Ley 2466 de 2025 art. 21 converted the apprenticeship into a special fixed-term LABOUR contract, regulated by Decreto 0223 of 5 March 2026. Nomina electronica TipoContrato = 4; PILA worker type 12 (lectiva) or 19 (productiva). |
| Intern / practical placement (practicas o pasantias) | YES | Nomina electronica TipoContrato = 5. Reported where remunerated. |
| Teleworkers and remote workers | YES | Ley 1221 de 2008 as amended by Ley 2466 de 2025 arts. 52-54. No reporting difference; the nomina electronica carries a dedicated Teletrabajo devengado element (NIE158). |
| Resident foreign national under a Colombian contract | YES | Full reporting. Withheld under the ET art. 383 progressive table once tax residence is acquired. |
| NON-RESIDENT foreign national under a Colombian contract | YES | IN SCOPE and frequently mis-configured. Withheld at the flat 20% of ET art. 408 on the GROSS, with no art. 388 depuration, until the 183-day residence test is met (DIAN Concepto 011108 of 29 June 2026). See the resident / non-resident block in the Report Matrix. |
| Employed old-age pensioner in active service | YES | PILA worker sub-type 01; no pension contribution (PILA pension rate field = 0), health and the levies still apply. |
| Executive director / statutory legal representative holding an employment contract | YES | Reported as an ordinary employee where an employment contract exists. |
| Independent contractor (contrato de prestacion de servicios) | NO | Not an employee. The contractor self-files PILA on 40% of fees (planilla types I / Y, descoped) and is reported by the payer in the third-party information return, not in this employer filing. Withholding on their fees does pass through Formulario 350, but under a different casilla. |
| Public-sector employee (contributor type 47, SGP-funded entity) | NO | Public-sector only; PILA planilla types T and F are descoped in scope_manifest. |
| Non-executive director paid honorarios only (junta directiva) | NO | Not employment income. Reported by the payer as honorarios, not as rentas de trabajo of an employee. |
| Pensioner whose pension is paid by a fund, not by the employer | NO | The paying fund is the withholding agent and the reporting party, not the employer. |
| Annual severance-savings liquidation at 31 December | YES | T14. The whole purpose of the filing - one amount per employee per accrual year. |
| Basic salary of December of the accrual year | YES | T12, as the layout expressly requires: 'el ultimo salario basico reportado en el mes de diciembre del ano causado de cesantias'. |
| Auxilio de transporte inside the liquidation base | YES | CST art. 253 includes it in the base, and Ley 2466 de 2025 art. 64 par. 1 states it expressly for the monthly advance. It is NOT a separate field - it is inside T14. The operator's own calculator omits it, which is the single most expensive trap in this filing; see T14. |
| Days of service on the 360-day commercial year | YES | T10, 1 to 360. Paid leave and recognised incapacity count as service. |
| Employee's chosen fund | YES | T09, one of the five codes 02 / 03 / 10 / 15 / 19. The choice is the employee's under Ley 50 de 1990 art. 99 num. 3. |
| Employee residential address, landline, e-mail | YES | T15 and T18-T19 - the fund needs them to communicate with its own member. Note it is the RESIDENCE, unlike PILA and the nomina electronica which carry the work location. |
| Contract hire date | YES | T20, and it is the HIRE date, not the start of the accrual period. See T20. |
| Interest on severance savings at 12% | NO | IN01-IN03. Paid DIRECTLY TO THE EMPLOYEE in January under Ley 52 de 1975 art. 1, never to the fund. Different payee, different deadline, different penalty. Shown on the Spec as not-applicable rows precisely because it is so often conflated with the deposit. |
| Salario integral employees' cesantias | NO | CST art. 132 num. 2 - the all-inclusive salary ABSORBS the cesantias, so there is no deposit and the employee must not appear in the planilla at all. They DO appear in PILA, which is why the seed-from-PILA convenience at E08 is dangerous. |
| Partial withdrawals and advances taken during the year | NO | Not a field of this layout. CST art. 254 prohibits partial payment before termination except for the authorised housing and education purposes; a lawful partial settlement reduces the balance and the interest, and is evidenced separately. |
| Withholding tax on the severance savings | NO | No withholding is practised on the DEPOSIT. The tax treatment (ET art. 206 num. 4 for a resident, ET art. 408 for a non-resident) is settled when the money is PAID to the employee, and is reported on Formulario 220 and Formato 2276. |
| Employer social-security contributions | NO | Cesantias is a prestacion social and is NOT part of the contribution base (Ley 1393 de 2010 art. 30 and CST art. 128 govern that boundary). Nothing about it appears in PILA today - the monthly advance under Ley 2466 art. 64 will change the CHANNEL, not the nature. |
| A declared record count or control total inside the file | NO | The layout has no header and no trailer record. A truncated upload is NOT self-detecting. Reconcile the operator's returned total (E11) against the engine's own sum. |
| Pre-1991 retrospective-regime employees | PARTIAL | Employees hired before 1 January 1991 who did not migrate remain on the retrospective annual-adjustment regime, which has NO annual deposit to a fund - the employer holds the liability and settles on termination. They are therefore out of this planilla, but they ARE reported as a separate line on Formulario 220 and Formato 2276. Hold the regime as master data on the contract. |
| Reported on the accrual (causacion) date | NO | Every Colombian payroll filing is keyed to the PAYMENT or credit-in-account date, not to the accrual date. A December salary paid in January falls in the January filing. |
| Reported on the payment or credit-in-account date | YES | The universal Colombian rule - pago o abono en cuenta. Applies to the nomina electronica (Res. DIAN 000013 de 2021 art. 3), to Formulario 350, to Formulario 220 and to Formato 2276. |
| A semi-monthly payroll produces two filings a month | NO | It does not. The nomina electronica is expressly ONE monthly document per employee 'irrespective of whether the obliged party makes payments in different or shorter periods' (art. 3). PILA and Formulario 350 are likewise monthly. |
| Nil period still filed | VARIES | Formulario 350: NOT required where no operation subject to withholding occurred in the period (ET art. 606 par. 2). Nomina electronica: no document where there was no payment. PILA: a planilla is still required while the employment relationship subsists. |
| Corrections handled by re-filing the whole period | VARIES | Nomina electronica: NO - a dedicated adjustment note (NominaIndividualDeAjuste, Reemplazar or Eliminar) referencing the original CUNE. Formulario 350: yes, a corrective return. PILA: a planilla type N (correcciones) or M (mora). |
| Employee who left during the accrual year | NO | The cesantias of a leaver are settled IN THE FINAL SETTLEMENT and paid to the employee, not deposited to the fund in the following February. Only employees still in service at 31 December generate a planilla record. Including a leaver double-pays. |
| Employee hired during the accrual year | YES | One record, with T10 carrying the actual days of service on the 360-day convention and T20 still carrying the real hire date. |
| Employee whose liquidation is below one day of the SMMLV | NO | The layout rejects it: 'el valor minimo del recaudo debe ser de un (1) dia de un (1) SMLMV del ano causado' - COP 58,363.50 for accrual year 2026. Such a residue is settled with the employee, not deposited. |
| Employee paid directly under CST art. 57 par. 4 | NO | Where the employer proves direct payment fehacientemente, the payment discharges the obligation and no non-deposit penalty applies - so no planilla record. BUT affiliation to a fund remains compulsory, and the discharge is rebuttable on the freedom-and-purpose test. See E92. |
| Construction worker under CST art. 310 | YES | A special regime - three days' salary per completed month rather than 30 - but the same planilla record. Only T14 changes; the layout has no field to disclose the regime. |
| Apprentice under a contrato de aprendizaje | YES | Ley 2466 de 2025 art. 21 converted the apprenticeship into a special fixed-term LABOUR contract, regulated by Decreto 0223 of 5 March 2026, so the ordinary CST prestaciones including cesantias apply to the productive phase. |
| Employee who changed fund during the year | YES | One record, to the fund the employee has chosen at the time of the deposit. The choice is the employee's and is master data on the employee; a mid-year change must reach payroll before the February run or the deposit goes to the old fund. |
| Monthly advance deposit already made through the year | PARTIAL | Not yet operable - the enabling decree is a draft and no MinSalud PILA regulation exists. When it is, the annual consolidation still runs and only the DIFFERENCE is deposited by 14 February; omitting monthly payments is not mora provided the annual total is completed by then. See MA01-MA05. |

Displayed on the settlement summary and on the comprobante. It must equal the sum of T14 across the detail records of the planilla; where the medio de pago splits the planilla per fondo, the sum of the per-fondo totals must equal the run total. This is the only arithmetic check the channel gives you - there is no header record in the file carrying a declared total, so a truncated upload is NOT self-detecting. Compare the returned total against the engine's own sum before paying. | Y | - | Whole Colombian pesos. No decimals, no thousand separators, no sign. The published layout gives amount fields 9 integer positions, right-aligned and zero-filled, with a 4-position zero filler in front of each. Round the computed liquidation to the peso - do NOT round up to the next 100 as PILA requires (Decreto 1990 de 2016 art. 1 governs contributions, not the cesantias deposit), and do NOT round to the nearest 1,000 as Formulario 350 requires (ET art. 577). Blending the three conventions is the classic Colombian defect. | N | Amount | Per planilla | DERIVED - not a field of the published detail layout. Column E is therefore N and the Data Dictionary correctly holds no Contractual Salary field. Obligatorio. Nine integers, no decimals, no commas, no points, not less than zero. The layout is explicit about WHICH salary: 'el ultimo salario basico reportado en el mes de diciembre del ano causado de cesantias'. THIS IS THE OPERATOR'S REPORTING FIELD, NOT THE STATUTORY BASE. CST art. 253 fixes the statutory base as the last monthly salary provided it has not varied in the last three months, otherwise the average of the last year of service (or of the whole period if shorter), INCLUDING the auxilio de transporte. T10, T12 and T13 back-reference this field. | TXT positions 128-136 \| XLS column J | 9 | N (9) whole pesos, zero-filled | 002000000 | Mercans.Cesantias_Annual_Liquidation | Obligatorio, nine integers. TWO RULES AND ONE TRAP. Rule one, the floor: 'el valor minimo del recaudo debe ser de un (1) dia de un (1) SMLMV del ano causado' - for accrual year 2026 one day of the SMMLV of COP 1,750,905 is COP 58,363.50, so a record below that is rejected. Rule two, the override: the layout's own formula is salario basico * (dias trabajados / 360) BUT 'en este campo el aportante puede especificar un valor diferente y no se haran calculos automaticos de este valor'. THE TRAP: the operator's formula omits the AUXILIO DE TRANSPORTE, which CST art. 253 requires in the base. For an employee on the 2026 minimum wage the operator would compute 1,750,905 while the statute requires (1,750,905 + 249,095) = 2,000,000 - a shortfall of COP 249,095 that is a SHORT DEPOSIT, penalised at one day's salary for every day of delay under Ley 50 de 1990 art. 99 num. 3, uncapped. ALWAYS write the engine's own figure into this field and never rely on the portal calculator. Where the monthly advance regime of MA01 is in use, this field carries the CONSOLIDATED annual figure and only the difference computed at MA05 is actually deposited in February. | Y | - | Whole Colombian pesos. No decimals, no thousand separators, no sign. The published layout gives amount fields 9 integer positions, right-aligned and zero-filled, with a 4-position zero filler in front of each. Blending the three conventions is the classic Colombian defect. | N | Amount | Per employee, per accrual year | Simple S.A.
